Abstract: Generating realistic human avatars in complex motions--such as clothing dynamics--requires modeling of global and local deformations which remains challenging in monocular settings. We address this problem by leveraging neural Jacobian fields (NJFs) for representing semi-rigid deformations. We train self-supervised neural networks for predicting Jacobian matrices that give the pose-dependent deformations, by solving a Poisson equation. However, monocular input presents several difficulties such as self-occluded regions and invisible surfaces. To address these issues, we introduce three key components: a constrained Poisson solver, signed distance-based Jacobian regularization, and a deformation-guided residual flow loss, which together suppress boundary artifacts, recover frequently occluded regions such as armpits and thighs, and enforce temporal consistency during motion. Experiments on benchmark and in-the-wild videos demonstrate that our method generates temporally stable and geometrically coherent avatars, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [36] [WildProp: Visual Estimation of Wildlife Body Proportions at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31125)
*Mustafa Chasmai,Aaron Sun,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildProp 是一种无需训练、基于检索驱动的框架，利用大规模无约束图像库和单张用户标注的规范图像，通过姿态感知检索、密集块级匹配、几何一致性过滤和鲁棒聚合，估计野生动物种群的体型比例分布，在鸟类和两栖动物数据集上达到10-20%的中位相对误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的种群级形态测量依赖受控成像或实体标本操作，难以规模化。现有监督式关键点方法需要逐物种训练，泛化能力有限。因此需要一种可适应任意物种、无需逐物种训练、能直接利用大规模无约束网络图像进行形态测量的方法。

Method: WildProp 将形态测量建模为检索驱动的对应问题：(1) 用户提供一张带部位标注的规范图像；(2) 使用基础模型特征进行姿态感知检索，从大规模图像库中召回相似姿态的个体；(3) 通过密集块级匹配将规范图像上的部位端点迁移至检索图像；(4) 利用几何一致性检查过滤不可靠预测；(5) 聚合所有检索图像的测量结果，估计种群级别的比例分布。

Result: 在三个涵盖鸟类和两栖动物的大型形态测量数据集上，中位相对误差为10-20%。多个案例研究展示了该方法在鸟类、蛙类、昆虫和花卉等多种类群中的广泛适用性。消融实验表明，姿态感知检索对稳定估计至关重要，鲁棒聚合策略有效缓解了关键点和姿态噪声。

Conclusion: 精心策展的二维对应关系可在网络规模图像上提供可扩展的形态测量代理指标，支持跨类群、地理和季节的比较与亚组分析。

Abstract: Population-level morphometric measurements underpin ecological and evolutionary studies but traditionally require controlled imaging or physical specimen handling, limiting scalability. We present WildProp, a training-free framework that estimates wildlife body proportion distributions directly from large-scale, unconstrained image repositories. We cast morphometric estimation as a retrieval-driven correspondence problem: given a single user-annotated canonical image, WildProp performs pose-aware retrieval using foundation model features, transfers part endpoints via dense patch-level matching, filters predictions using geometric consistency, and aggregates measurements across retrieved images to estimate population-level ratio distributions. Unlike supervised keypoint pipelines, our approach adapts to arbitrary species and user-defined parts without per-species training. Evaluations on three large morphometric datasets spanning birds and amphibians show median relative errors of 10-20%. We further highlight the broad applicability of our approach through a number of case studies measuring various proportions across diverse taxa, including birds, frogs, insects, and flowers. Ablations demonstrate that pose-aware retrieval is critical for stable estimation, while robust aggregation mitigates keypoint and pose noise. Our results indicate that carefully curated 2D correspondences over web-scale imagery can provide scalable morphometric proxies for comparative and subgroup analyses across taxa, geography, and seasonality.

</details>


### [37] [SkillSpotter: Pose-Aware Multi-View Skilled Action Detection and Grading in Ego-Exo Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31127)
*Björn Braun,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkillSpotter是一种面向自我-外部视角视频的姿势感知多视图架构，能够同时检测个体技能动作并对其执行质量进行分级。该方法在Ego-Exo4D基准上将类别级mAP从12.40提升至21.82（+76%），平衡准确率从55.99%提升至60.40%，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在体育、烹饪、音乐等领域的AR眼镜或固定摄像头的个性化实时教练场景中，系统不仅需要识别用户"做了什么"，还需判断其"做得如何"。Ego-Exo4D基准虽已形式化了这一任务，但现有方法在动作分级上表现接近随机，且检测与分级的评估相互纠缠，亟需更有效的解决方案。

Method: 首先将七种最先进的时间动作检测架构适配到该任务，并扩展评估协议以解耦检测与分级。随后提出SkillSpotter，包含三个任务特定的模块：(1)自适应时间抑制，用于处理不同活动中技能动作密度的变化；(2)门控3D人体姿态融合，将身体运动学作为视觉特征的互补信号；(3)双向跨视图注意力，有效融合自我视角和外部视角信息。

Result: SkillSpotter在Ego-Exo4D基准上实现类别级mAP 21.82（较最佳基线提升76%），平衡准确率60.40%（较55.99%提升）。各模块可迁移至其他时间动作检测模型并带来一致增益，方法还泛化至HoloAssist数据集。

Conclusion: SkillSpotter通过姿势感知的多视图融合策略，有效解决了自我-外部视角下技能动作的联合检测与质量分级问题。模块化设计使其具备良好的可迁移性和泛化能力，为该领域的实际部署（如AR实时教练系统）提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: To enable personalized, real-time coaching using Augmented Reality glasses or fixed camera setups in domains such as sports, cooking, or music, a system must understand not just what a person does, but how well they execute an activity. In an ego-exo video setting, this requires simultaneously detecting individual skilled actions and classifying each as correct or needing improvement, which Ego-Exo4D's proficiency demonstration benchmark formalized. We first adapt seven state-of-the-art temporal action detection architectures to this task, extend the evaluation protocol to disentangle detection from grading, and show that existing methods grade near-randomly. We then introduce SkillSpotter, a pose-aware multi-view architecture that jointly detects and grades skilled actions through three task-specific modules: (1) adaptive temporal suppression to handle the varying density of skilled actions across diverse activities, (2) gated 3D body pose fusion to leverage body kinematics as a complementary signal to visual features, and (3) bidirectional cross-view attention to combine ego and exo views effectively. SkillSpotter improves class-specific mAP from 12.40 to 21.82 (+76%) and balanced accuracy from 55.99% to 60.40% over the best baseline. SkillSpotter's modules transfer to other temporal action detection models with consistent gains, and our method generalizes beyond Ego-Exo4D to HoloAssist. Code: https://github.com/eth-siplab/SkillSpotter

</details>


### [38] [MSNN-LINet: Cross-Modal Learning via Continuous Linear Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31135)
*Gabriel Clinger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LINet 是一种用于 RGB-D 场景分类的多流神经网络，通过线性积分卷积（LIConv2d）在每一层实现连续跨模态融合，而非传统的离散融合策略，并在 SUN RGB-D 上取得了领先的从头训练结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态架构将特征融合视为离散的、临时性事件——早融合过早纠缠表征，晚融合将其隔离至最后一层，而混合或注意力方法需要架构猜测来放置中间融合模块。作者旨在解决这一结构性妥协，实现连续的跨模态学习。

Method: 提出 LINet，包含三条并行流（RGB、深度、集成流）。核心创新是线性积分卷积（LIConv2d），在每层非线性激活前，集成流接收来自两个模态流的原始滤波信号并进行组合，模拟神经元胞体整合先于发放的生物机制。针对连续集成暴露的初始化病理（Kaiming 初始化导致梯度崩溃），采用 1/N 常数初始化进行缓解。引入渐进式模态 dropout（从零开始递增的遮盖概率），防止通路坍塌（负协同学习），强制形成鲁棒的独立流表征。

Result: 在 SUN RGB-D 19 类场景分类任务上，ResNet18 规模的 LINet 从头训练达到 45.2% 平均类别准确率，优于此前从头训练结果；使用域内 RGB-D（ScanNet）预训练后提升至 49.6%。

Conclusion: 连续融合策略优于传统的离散早/晚/混合融合，通过线性积分卷积和渐进式模态 dropout 实现了更有效的跨模态表征学习，同时揭示了连续融合中的初始化梯度流问题及其解决方案。

Abstract: We present LINet (Linear Integration Network), a Multi-Stream Neural Network (MSNN) for RGB-D scene classification. Current multi-modal architectures treat feature fusion as a discrete, ad-hoc event: early fusion entangles representations prematurely, late fusion isolates them until the final layer, and hybrid or attention-based methods require architectural guesswork to place intermediate fusion blocks. LINet addresses this structural compromise by maintaining three dedicated parallel streams (RGB, depth, and integration) where a novel Linear Integration Convolution (LIConv2d) operator enables continuous cross-modal learning at every layer. The integration stream receives raw filtered signals from both modality streams and combines them before the nonlinear activation threshold, conceptually inspired by somatic integration preceding the neuronal firing decision. Implementing continuous integration exposes a critical initialization pathology: Kaiming initialization of the bridging weights scrambles gradients before they reach the stream backbones, producing a failure mode that resembles overfitting but is corrupted gradient flow. A 1/N constant initialization mitigates this. We employ progressive modality dropout, a curriculum adapted to continuous fusion in which blanking probability increases from zero, preventing pathway collapse, a form of negative co-learning, by forcing robust independent stream representations. Trained from scratch on SUN RGB-D 19-class scene classification, LINet reaches 45.2% mean class accuracy at ResNet18 scale, outperforming prior from-scratch results, and rises to 49.6% with in-domain RGB-D (ScanNet) pretraining.

</details>


### [39] [FROST: Training-Free Few-Shot Segmentation with Frozen Features and Nonparametric Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31136)
*Junghwan Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FROST 是一种无需训练的少样本分割方法，将参考前景和背景视为冻结 DINOv3 特征在单位球面上的点云，通过非参数密度比进行查询标记分类，利用贝叶斯规则以零阈值决策。在遥感场景中，FROST 将类别建模为分布而非点估计，在17个遥感基准上以5.6 mIoU的优势领先训练型和免训练方法，且模型规模最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本分割方法存在两大局限：一是基于训练的方法会绑定到训练分布；二是基于原型的免训练方法将多模态类别压缩为单个原型、若干聚类原型或离散聚类等有损摘要，无法保留类别内部结构。遥感场景中类别通常由大量分散的小实例组成，有损摘要会模糊类别边界，因此需要一种能捕捉类别完整分布且无需训练的解决方案。

Method: FROST 利用冻结的 DINOv3 自监督特征，将参考前景和背景分别建模为单位球面上的两个点云。对每个查询 token，通过非参数密度比（前景密度/背景密度）进行分类，使用贝叶斯规则在等先验下将阈值固定为零。随着支持样本增加，密度估计方差缩小，决策边界自然锐化。所有超参数（如核带宽、空间门控）均从支持集中自动读取，无需人工调参。

Result: 在17个遥感基准数据集上，FROST 全面超越免训练方法和基于学习的方法。仅使用单个标注样本即可领先 5.6 mIoU，且随着支持集增大，领先优势进一步扩大。同时，FROST 在所有对比方法中模型规模最小。

Conclusion: 类别应由分布而非点来描述，冻结的自监督特征本身已蕴含足够结构来直接估计该分布。FROST 验证了这一理念在遥感少样本分割中的有效性，实现了训练自由、数据高效且性能领先的分割。

Abstract: Few-shot segmentation asks a model to delineate a target class in a query image from only a handful of annotated examples, a setting most acute in remote sensing, where labels are scarce and the imagery departs sharply from the natural images on which vision backbones are pretrained. Prevailing approaches either train a segmenter on labelled episodes, which raises accuracy within the training distribution but binds the model to it, or reduce each class to a lossy summary of frozen features, a single prototype, a few cluster prototypes, or a discrete clustering, none of which preserves the internal structure of a multimodal class. We argue that a class is better described by a distribution than by a point, and that frozen self-supervised features already carry enough structure to estimate that distribution directly. We introduce FROST, a training-free few-shot segmenter that treats the reference foreground and background as two point clouds on the unit sphere of frozen DINOv3 features and labels each query token by a nonparametric density ratio, with a threshold the Bayes rule fixes at zero under equal priors. Because the variance of a density estimate shrinks as its sample grows, the decision sharpens as references accumulate, and every remaining quantity from the kernel bandwidth to the spatial gate is read from the support set rather than tuned. We develop FROST for overhead imagery, where a class is typically a scatter of many small and dissimilar instances that a density tracks but a lossy summary blurs. Across seventeen remote-sensing benchmarks FROST surpasses both training-free and learning-based methods, leading by 5.6 mIoU from a single annotated example and widening its lead as the support set grows, all while remaining among the smallest models compared. Code is available at https://github.com/jhpark-ai/FROST.

</details>


### [40] [WaterGen: Decoupling Scene and Medium in Underwater Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31147)
*Jiayi Wu,Tianfu Wang,Tianyi Xiong,Dehao Yuan,Xiaomin Lin,Md Jahidul Islam,Cornelia Fermuller,Christopher Metzler,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaterGen 提出了一种解耦的水下图像生成方法，将场景内容生成与水介质退化建模分离，在潜扩散框架内实现多样化场景与可控水下外观的独立控制，生成的合成数据能有效提升水下复原与语义分割等下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下计算机视觉任务（检测、复原、分割）受限于大规模、多样化训练数据的匮乏。现有方法要么可控但缺乏真实感/多样性，要么生成真实场景但无法精确独立地建模水介质效应，无法同时满足真实感、多样性和可控性三者。

Method: 将水下图像生成分解为两个解耦阶段：(1) 使用无退化水下图像微调潜扩散U-Net，学习生成多样化、真实的水下场景内容潜嵌入；(2) 将物理精确的水介质退化合成建模为条件解码过程，作用于上述潜嵌入。通过这种解耦设计实现对场景内容的多样化生成和水下外观的完全控制。

Result: 利用 WaterGen 构建的大规模合成水下数据集，在场景结构多样性和水效应精确性方面均表现优异，并在水下图像复原和语义分割两项下游任务中持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 通过在潜扩散框架内解耦场景生成与介质建模，WaterGen 打破了真实感、多样性与可控性之间的权衡，为水下视觉任务提供了高质量的大规模合成数据解决方案。

Abstract: Underwater computer vision tasks, such as detection, restoration, and segmentation, are limited by the scarcity of large-scale and diverse training data. We introduce WaterGen, a method for generating large-scale, realistic, and diverse underwater images that provides independent control of the scene and water medium conditions. Our approach treats underwater image generation as the decoupled control of two factors: realistic and diverse scene content (what is in the image), and accurate and controllable water medium effects (what the water does to the image). Existing methods generally achieve only part of this objective: they either provide controllability with limited realism or diversity, or generate realistic scenes without accurately and independently modeling water-medium effects. Our key insight, that allows us to avoid this compromise, is that scene generation and medium modeling can be decoupled within a latent diffusion framework, enabling diverse scene generation together with accurate and controllable underwater appearance. To do this, we decompose underwater image synthesis into two stages. First, we fine-tune the latent diffusion U-Net using degradation-free underwater images so that it learns to generate diverse and realistic latent embeddings of underwater scene content without medium-induced degradation. Second, we formulate the physically accurate medium degradation synthesis as a conditional decoding process applied to these latent embeddings. This decoupled design allows our model to generate diverse scenes with full control of underwater appearance. We leverage WaterGen to build large-scale synthetic underwater datasets that are diverse in scene structures and accurate in water effects and pseudo-labels. We demonstrate that our synthetic data consistently improve downstream performance in underwater restoration and semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [41] [PruneGround: Plug-and-play Spatial Pruning for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31148)
*Duc Cao Dinh,Khai Le-Duc,Florent Draye,Chris Ngo,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PruneGround 是一个即插即用的3D视觉定位框架，通过语言引导的空间剪枝缩小搜索空间、多视角描述重构简化复杂表达、以及将检测预训练的LLM改造为定位模型，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DVG方法对整个场景进行推理，导致预测模糊且计算开销大。作者观察到许多指代表达依赖于局部空间上下文，仅对应受限的空间区域而非整个场景，因此提出通过空间剪枝来缩小搜索范围。

Method: PruneGround包含三个核心模块：(1) Language-Guided Spatial Pruning (LGSP)：利用冻结的视觉语言模型识别语言相关区域，缩小搜索空间；(2) MultiView-Conditioned Description Reformulation (MCDR)：将复杂表达分解为简化的目标-锚点关系，并通过多视图推理补充缺失的空间线索；(3) LLM-Grounder：将检测预训练的空间LLM改造为语言条件定位模型，在剪枝区域内对齐点云与语言表征。

Result: 在ScanRefer的三个设置和Nr3D/Sr3D的10个设置中的9个上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过空间剪枝、描述简化和LLM改造三者结合，PruneGround有效解决了3DVG中计算冗余和预测模糊的问题，表现出强大的泛化能力和SOTA性能。

Abstract: 3D Visual Grounding (3DVG) aims to localize target objects in 3D scenes given natural language descriptions. Existing approaches typically perform reasoning over the entire scene, leading to ambiguous predictions and high computational cost, especially in cluttered environments. We observe that many referential expressions rely on local spatial context and often correspond to restricted spatial regions rather than the full scene. Motivated by this insight, we propose PruneGround, an effective plug-and-play framework for 3DVG built upon three key components. First, we introduce Language-Guided Spatial Pruning (LGSP), which leverages a frozen Vision Language Model (VLM) to identify language-relevant regions, thereby reducing spatial computation and grounding candidates in the narrower search space. Second, we propose MultiView-Conditioned Description Reformulation (MCDR), which decomposes complex expressions into simplified target-anchor relations and augments missing spatial cues through multi-view reasoning. Finally, we propose LLM-Grounder, which repurposes a detection-pretrained spatial LLM into a language-conditioned grounding model by aligning point cloud and linguistic representations within the pruned region. Extensive experiments on the three most popular point cloud benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art results on all three ScanRefer settings and on 9 out of 10 Nr3D/Sr3D settings. Code and models are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/PruneGround

</details>


### [42] [Rethinking Foundation Model Collaboration: Enhancing Specialized Models through Proxy Task Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31157)
*Hongyi Lin,Yang Liu,Jinhua Zhao,Xiaobo Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAT框架提出一种基础模型与专用模型协作的新范式：专用模型负责生成结构化预测假设，基础模型在重构的多模态候选空间上进行代理推理（如选择/验证），而非直接替代专用模型。该范式在2D/3D检测、轨迹预测和语义分割上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽被广泛集成到具身智能系统中，但在需要精确几何与数值估计的结构化预测任务上仍弱于专用模型。核心问题：基础模型应替代专用预测器，还是通过与其优势更匹配的任务进行协作？

Method: 提出FAT（Foundation-model-Augmented Task-specific reasoning）框架，将协作视为任务分解：①专用模型在原生输出空间生成几何/物理有效的假设；②信息空间重构，将假设转化为多模态候选表征；③基础模型在受限的代理任务（如选择或验证）上进行推理。具体实例ProxySelect采用视觉-语言模型实现该框架。

Result: 在2D目标检测、3D目标检测、轨迹预测和语义分割四个任务上，ProxySelect一致地提升了专用基线模型性能，且显著优于基础模型直接回归方法，同时计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 提出通用协作原则：专用模型保留任务特定的结构化输出能力，基础模型通过上下文代理推理来精炼其假设。这种"分解-重构-代理推理"的范式在多种结构化预测任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly integrated into embodied intelligence systems, but directly assigning them structured prediction tasks requires precise geometric and numerical estimation, where specialized models often remain stronger. This capability mismatch raises a key question: should foundation models replace task-specific predictors, or should they collaborate through tasks better aligned with their strengths? We propose FAT, a foundation-model-augmented task-specific reasoning framework that treats collaboration as task decomposition rather than model replacement. FAT decomposes structured prediction into specialist prediction, information-space reconstruction, and foundation-model proxy reasoning. The specialist generates geometrically and physically valid hypotheses in the native output space, while the foundation model performs a bounded proxy task, such as selection or verification, over reconstructed multimodal candidates. We instantiate this principle as ProxySelect with a vision--language model. Across 2D object detection, 3D object detection, trajectory prediction, and semantic segmentation, ProxySelect consistently improves specialized baselines and substantially outperforms direct foundation-model regression at lower computational cost. These results suggest a general collaboration principle: specialized models preserve task-specific structure, while foundation models refine their hypotheses through contextual proxy reasoning.

</details>


### [43] [Reasoning-aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31160)
*Anh Dung Dinh,Simon Khan,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种双路径推理架构，将自动驾驶VLA规划器中的链式因果推理拆分为"例行推理器"和"审慎推理器"，通过FlatRoPE位置编码和动作感知RL训练，实现推理延迟降低约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLA自动驾驶规划器中，链式因果推理（CoC）是自回归生成的，主导了推理延迟，而轨迹生成头是并行的、开销低。自动驾驶对延迟有严格约束，因此加速推理步骤是核心问题。观察到CoC推理中大多数token只是延续例行设置，仅少数token需要新的视觉证据来应对意外情况。

Method: 提出双推理器架构：(1) 例行推理器（routine reasoner），通过关注轨迹历史来处理可预测的延续；(2) 审慎推理器（deliberative reasoner），即原始VLA目标模型，关注当前视觉证据处理新情况。以推测解码框架为模板协调两者。两项关键技术：FlatRoPE——在草案模型中使用1D旋转位置嵌入，打破目标模型3D M-RoPE的旋转对称性，将注意力从视觉token重定向到轨迹历史token；AARL（动作感知RL）——使用动作质量奖励结合静态参考KL锚点的后训练阶段。

Result: 相对于原始Alpamayo规划器，双推理器系统将推理步骤运行时间降低约4倍。

Conclusion: 通过将CoC推理按需求定性拆分为两条专门化路径，并利用FlatRoPE和AARL两项技术实现高效协作，可在保持规划质量的同时大幅降低推理延迟，满足自动驾驶的操作约束。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) planners for autonomous driving emit a chain-of-causation (CoC) reasoning step \emph{before} producing a trajectory. The reasoning is autoregressive and dominates inference latency, while the trajectory head is parallel and cheap. Latency is an operational constraint in autonomous driving, so accelerating the reasoning step is the central problem we address. We observe that CoC reasoning has two qualitatively different needs: most tokens continue routine setup that follows naturally from the ego-trajectory history, and a small fraction encode commitments that require fresh visual evidence about an unexpected situation. We split this reasoning into two specialized paths: a \emph{routine reasoner} that handles the predictable continuation by attending to trajectory history, and a \emph{deliberative reasoner} (the unmodified VLA target) that handles novel cases by attending to current visual evidence, using the speculative decoding framework as the architectural template for how the two paths cooperate. Unlike standard speculative decoding, our routine reasoner is not a smaller replica of the target; the two reasoners are deliberately specialized to read different parts of the prompt. We propose two techniques to realize this. First, we introduce \textbf{FlatRoPE}, a 1D rotary positional embedding in the draft that breaks the rotational symmetry of the target's 3D M-RoPE, redirecting attention away from visual tokens and onto trajectory-history tokens. Second, we introduce \textbf{Action-aware RL (AARL)}, a post-training stage that uses an action-quality reward together with a static-reference KL anchor. Together, our two-reasoner system reduces the reasoning-step running time by approximately $4\times$ relative to the original Alpamayo planner.

</details>


### [44] [Seeing Through the Weights: Privacy Leakage in Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31164)
*Oleksii Nasypanyi,Jaemin Cho,Utku Ozbulak,Byungkon Kang,Francois Rameau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示场景坐标回归（SCR）模型存在严重的隐私泄露风险，提出了一种基于查询的攻击方法，能够从训练好的SCR网络参数中重建出训练场景的三维几何和外观信息，推翻了SCR方法天然保护隐私的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCR方法将场景隐式编码在神经网络权重中，而非显式存储图像或地图，因此被普遍认为具有隐私保护特性。作者质疑这一假设，旨在验证SCR模型是否真的能防止训练场景的几何与外观信息被逆向提取。

Method: 提出一种查询式攻击：使用与目标场景无关的代理图像批量查询SCR模型，获取逐像素稠密3D坐标；通过小幅输入扰动下的稳定性筛选可靠点；在白盒设定下进一步精化；跨批次累积稳定点以重建场景几何；最后反转网络特征合成任意视角图像，恢复外观信息。

Result: 在室内外数据集上的实验表明，攻击者可以高几何保真度地重建训练环境的大部分区域，并恢复近似的颜色外观，暴露可识别的场景布局和潜在敏感元素。

Conclusion: SCR模型的隐私保护假设在实践中不成立。当此类系统部署于私密或安全关键空间时，存在真实的隐私泄露风险，需重新审视其安全性设计。

Abstract: Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) methods are increasingly adopted for visual localization. In these approaches, the scene is implicitly encoded within a neural network that regresses a 3D world coordinate for each image pixel. Because the scene is represented only through the network parameters and not stored explicitly as images or maps, such methods are often assumed to be privacy-preserving. In this work, we show that this assumption is incorrect in practice.
  Specifically, we introduce a query-based attack that reconstructs the 3D geometry of the training environment from an SCR model under different levels of model access. To do so, we repeatedly query the model with batches of proxy images unrelated to the target scene to obtain dense pixel-wise 3D coordinates. Reliable points are identified through their stability under small input perturbations and can be further refined in a white-box setting. These stable points are accumulated across independent query batches to recover the scene geometry. From the recovered 3D representation, we also invert the network features to synthesize images from arbitrary viewpoints, revealing additional appearance information.
  Experiments on indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate that substantial portions of training environments can be reconstructed with high geometric fidelity. Beyond geometry, we also recover an approximate color appearance, which exposes recognizable layout and potentially sensitive scene elements. This directly contradicts claims in the literature that SCR representations are privacy-preserving by design, and reveals a real risk when such systems are deployed in private or security-critical spaces. The project page is available at https://jaeminch0.github.io/seeing-through-the-weights-privacy-leakage-in-scene-coordinate-regression.

</details>


### [45] [Beyond Single Character: Evaluating MLLMs for Sentence-Level Oracle Bone Inscription Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31169)
*Ziqi Li,Zijian Chen,Tingzhu Chen,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S-OBI提出了首个面向句子级甲骨文理解的评测基准，通过字形替换合成清晰规范的长句实例，设计语义匹配、语义槽提取和上下文推理三类任务（共695个问答对），揭示现有多模态大模型在句子级甲骨文理解上的严重不足，尤其是视觉感知错误会沿推理链传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲骨文AI研究主要停留在字符级识别，忽略了完整卜辞中长文本的语义连贯性和上下文依赖关系；同时，原始拓片存在噪声和不完整等问题，影响对句子级理解能力的聚焦评估。

Method: 提出S-OBI基准：基于95篇经专家鉴定、修正和验证的带翻译原始拓片，通过字形替换与合成（用现有甲骨文数据集中的清晰字形样本替换拓片中的字符），在保持整体契刻结构和语义组织的前提下，生成清晰、规范的句子级甲骨文实例。在此基础上设计语义匹配、语义槽提取、上下文推理三类任务，构建695个问答对。

Result: 实验表明，当前多模态大模型在句子级甲骨文理解上表现不佳；关键发现是模型对未掩码区域的视觉感知错误会沿推理链传播，导致对被掩码字符的错误预测，说明现有模型的句子级理解仍严重依赖字符级识别能力。

Conclusion: S-OBI为评估MLLM是否能够超越孤立字符识别、迈向结构化铭文层级理解提供了一个诊断性基准，揭示了当前模型从字符识别到语义推理之间的关键能力断层。

Abstract: Existing AI-assisted oracle bone inscription (OBI) visual recognition and understanding studies mainly focus on character-level, ignoring the long-form textual coherence and contextual dependencies embedded in complete divination charges. Recently, the powerful visual perception capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have opened new possibilities for OBI information processing. In this work, we introduce S-OBI, a novel benchmark for evaluating MLLMs in Sentence-level OBI understanding. Instead of using noisy and incomplete rubbings as the visual input, S-OBI synthesizes clear and standardized sentence-level OBI instances through glyph substitution and composition. According to 95 original rubbings with translations that have been identified, corrected, and verified by experts, we replace characters in the original rubbings with corresponding clean glyph samples sourced from existing OBI datasets while preserving the overall inscriptional structure and semantic organization. This mitigates the influence of low-level distortions and enables a more focused evaluation of sentence-level OBI understanding. Based on this, we design semantic matching, semantic slot extraction, and contextual reasoning tasks and obtain 695 question-answer pairs. Experiments reveal the inferiority of contemporary MLLMs on sentence-level OBI understanding. In particular, visual perception errors in unmasked regions propagate through the reasoning chain, leading to erroneous predictions for masked characters, which indicates that sentence-level OBI understanding in current models remains strongly dependent on character-level recognition. Overall, S-OBI provides a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating whether MLLMs can move beyond isolated character recognition toward structured inscription-level understanding.

</details>


### [46] [HSDF-Lane: Height-Aligned Signed Distance Field with Semantic Lane Prior for 3D Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31172)
*Jiyong Boo,Byeongin Joung,Hyemin Yang,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HSDF-Lane 通过隐式建模路面为高度对齐的有符号距离场（HSDF），结合可微分渲染生成精确高度图和表面对齐特征，并引入车道感知语义位置编码（LSPE），在 OpenLane 基准上达到了 3D 车道检测和高度图估计的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目 3D 车道检测方法存在两个核心问题：一是基于 BEV 投影的平坦地面假设在真实道路（非平面）上导致几何失真；二是近期虽引入显式高度图处理非平面表面，但仅依赖稀疏锚点回归，且将恢复的几何信息仅用于空间变换，未将其融入语义理解，几何与语义之间存在割裂。

Method: 提出 HSDF-Lane，包含两大创新： (1) 将路面隐式建模为稠密 3D 特征体上的高度对齐有符号距离场（HSDF），通过可微分渲染联合输出精确高度图与表面对齐特征；(2) 设计车道感知语义位置编码（LSPE），从表面对齐特征中提取车道存在先验，注入 Transformer 查询中，实现几何结构与语义引导的耦合。

Result: 在 OpenLane 基准数据集上进行广泛实验，HSDF-Lane 在 3D 车道检测和高度图估计两项任务上均取得了最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: HSDF-Lane 通过 HSDF 隐式建模与 LSPE 几何-语义耦合机制，有效解决了单目 3D 车道检测中的深度歧义和非平面道路几何失真问题，验证了将几何重建与语义理解深度融合的有效性。

Abstract: Monocular 3D lane detection plays a critical role in autonomous driving, yet recovering reliable 3D geometry from a single image remains challenging due to inherent depth ambiguity. Prior methods project image features into Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) space under a flat-ground assumption, causing geometric distortion on real-world roads. Recent methods instead predict explicit height maps to capture non-planar surfaces, but still rely on sparse anchor-based regression and exploit the recovered geometry merely for spatial transformation rather than semantic understanding. To overcome these limitations, we propose HSDF-Lane, which implicitly models the road surface as a Height-aligned Signed Distance Field (HSDF) over a densely sampled 3D feature volume. Through differentiable rendering, the HSDF jointly produces an accurate height map and surface-aligned features. We further introduce Lane-aware Semantic Positional Encoding (LSPE), which injects a lane-existence prior derived from the surface-aligned features into the transformer queries, coupling geometric structure with semantic guidance. Extensive experiments on the OpenLane benchmark show that HSDF-Lane achieves state-of-the-art performance in both 3D lane detection and height map estimation.

</details>


### [47] [GaussianMap: Learning Gaussian Representation for Multi-Sensor Online HD Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31177)
*Hongyu Lyu,Julie Stephany Berrio Perez,Mao Shan,Stewart Worrall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussianMap 提出一种在线高清地图构建框架，用自适应高斯原语替代传统固定分辨率BEV网格，通过学习场景中灵活的高斯表示来更高效地编码稀疏但需精确定位的道路元素，在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线HD地图构建方法普遍采用固定分辨率密集BEV网格作为中间场景表示，但地图元素（如车道线、人行横道）在空间上稀疏且需要细粒度几何定位，均匀分配的BEV表示存在大量冗余，对矢量化地图预测效率低下。

Method: 提出GaussianMap框架：(1) 在BEV平面上定义一组高斯原语，每个原语编码一个灵活的局部区域（含几何属性和特征向量），自适应分配表示容量；(2) 设计前馈高斯编码器，通过高斯交互建模和多传感器特征聚合逐步精化原语；(3) 将精化后的高斯表示splat到BEV特征图，再解码为矢量化地图。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上，GaussianMap在仅用相机和相机-激光雷达融合两种设定下均达到最优性能（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 自适应高斯表示能更高效地编码稀疏地图元素，减少BEV表示的冗余，是替代传统固定分辨率BEV网格的有效方案，代码将开源。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems benefit from high-definition (HD) maps that provide critical information about road infrastructure. The online construction of HD maps offers a scalable approach to generate local vectorized maps from onboard sensor observations. Existing methods commonly adopt bird's-eye-view (BEV) features as the intermediate scene representation, encoding the surrounding space with fixed-resolution dense grids. However, map elements are spatially sparse yet require fine-grained geometric localization, making uniformly allocated BEV representations redundant and less effective for vectorized map prediction. In this work, we propose GaussianMap, an online HD map construction framework that learns an adaptive Gaussian representation of the surrounding scene. This representation consists of a set of Gaussian primitives on the BEV plane, each encoding a flexible local region with geometric properties and a feature vector, allowing the model to allocate representational capacity to map-relevant regions. To generate such a representation from sensor observations, we introduce a feed-forward Gaussian encoder that progressively refines these primitives through Gaussian interaction modeling and multi-sensor feature aggregation. The refined Gaussian representation is then splatted into a BEV feature map and decoded into vectorized map predictions. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that GaussianMap achieves state-of-the-art performance in both camera-only and camera-LiDAR fusion settings. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [48] [Learning to Deny: Action Denial in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31187)
*Raiyaan Abdullah,Shehreen Azad,Yogesh Singh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF101-AD基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的"动作否认"能力——即识别某动作在强上下文线索下并未发生的能力。实验表明，当前最先进的MLLM在此任务上表现惨淡（准确率从85%+骤降至50%以下），暴露了视频理解中缺乏因果推理的盲点。作者进一步提出CausalAct因果图框架，通过自然语言提示注入因果结构，显著降低了误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在标准视频理解基准上表现优异，但其"否认"能力——即在强上下文暗示下判断某个动作并未真正发生的能力——尚未被系统探索。模型倾向于"肯定"看似合理的动作，而非验证其是否真实发生。

Method: (1) 构建UCF101-AD基准：包含成对的"动作存在"和"动作否认"视频片段，负样本保留与正样本相同的人物、物体、场景等上下文线索，仅目标动作被显式移除。(2) 对20个最先进MLLM进行全面评估。(3) 提出CausalAct因果图框架，通过自然语言提示将场景结构表达为上下文-交互-动作的因果链，增强模型的因果推理能力。

Result: 在UCF101-AD上，准确率超过85%的模型在其动作否认对应任务上骤降至50%以下，暴露出一致的"肯定偏差"。引入CausalAct因果提示后，误报率显著降低，证明否认是一种可学习的推理技能。

Conclusion: 现代视频理解模型存在严重的因果推理盲点：它们倾向于根据上下文线索肯定动作，而非验证动作是否真实发生。UCF101-AD为诊断和改进多模态模型的因果推理能力提供了新的评估视角，CausalAct证明该能力可通过结构化因果提示来提升。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have rapidly advanced video understanding, achieving strong zero-shot and few-shot recognition across standard benchmarks. Yet their ability to deny an action by recognizing when an activity is not happening despite strong contextual cues remains largely unexplored. We introduce UCF101-AD, a large-scale benchmark consisting of paired Action-Presence and Action-Denial clips, designed to evaluate this capacity for denial. Each negative video in UCF101-AD preserves the same contextual and motion cues, including persons, objects, and locations, as its positive counterpart, but the defining action itself is explicitly absent. Evaluating 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals a consistent failure: models that exceed 85% accuracy on the positive action classes collapse below 50% on their action-denial counterparts, indicating a strong inclination to affirm plausible actions rather than verify that they truly occur. This exposes a critical blind spot in modern video understanding: the inability to reason causally about whether a motion actually happens. To probe this issue, we explore a causal graph formulation, CausalAct, which expresses scene structure through natural-language prompts linking context, interaction, and motion. Incorporating such causal cues substantially reduces false positives, demonstrating that denial is a learnable reasoning skill. UCF101-AD provides a new lens for diagnosing and improving causal reasoning in multimodal models. Dataset and relevant code: https://github.com/raiyaan-abdullah/Learn-to-Deny.

</details>


### [49] [Distilling Temporal Coherence into 2D Networks for Transrectal Ultrasound Prostate Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31198)
*Dong Yeong Kim,JunGyu Lee,Jaewon Choi,June Young Seo,Myeongseop Kim,Jinwook Choi,Taek Min Kim,Young-Gon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种时序一致的轻量级前列腺TRUS视频分割框架，通过置信度加权时序约束和双尺度原型对齐，在保持2D网络实时推理速度的同时实现3D级别的时间一致性，无需逐帧密集标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床TRUS视频分割面临两难：2D方法因忽略时序上下文导致帧间不一致，3D方法则延迟过高无法实时。临床观察发现前列腺几何形态稳定，但周围声学环境因生理运动和探头压力而波动，传统时序约束会从这些不稳定区域传播错误梯度。

Method: 1) 置信度加权时序一致性损失：基于光流warping残差生成置信度图，选择性衰减不可靠区域的梯度；2) 双尺度原型对齐模块：通过对比学习分别对齐局部边界特征和全局语义特征的原型；3) 利用几何等变伪标注和预训练教师模型的知识蒸馏，免除逐帧密集标注需求。

Result: 在SUN-SEG和新提出的TRUS-V基准（2679帧）上，该方法在实时推理速度下达到了最先进的分割精度和时序一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将时序一致性蒸馏到2D网络中，以单帧推理的代价获得时序稳定的分割结果，为实时图像引导干预提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time video segmentation of the prostate in Transrectal Ultrasound (TRUS) is essential for image-guided interventions. While conventional 2D methods suffer from inter-frame inconsistencies by disregarding temporal context, 3D architectures incur prohibitive latency. To resolve this dilemma, we present a Temporally Consistent Learning Framework that distills temporal coherence into a 2D network during training, preserving single-frame inference efficiency. Our design is driven by a key clinical observation: the prostate exhibits geometric stability, whereas the surrounding acoustic environment fluctuates due to physiological motion and transducer pressure. Because conventional temporal constraints propagate erroneous gradients from these unstable regions, we introduce a Confidence-Weighted Temporal Consistency objective derived from optical flow warping residuals, selectively attenuating contributions from unreliable regions. Complementing this pixel-wise constraint, a Dual-scale Prototype Alignment Module enforces semantic coherence through contrastive optimization of local boundary and global semantic features. Furthermore, to eliminate the need for dense per-frame video annotations, we employ geometric equivariance-based pseudo-labeling with knowledge distillation from a pretrained teacher. Extensive experiments on SUN-SEG and our newly introduced TRUS-V benchmark (2,679 frames) demonstrate state-of-the-art accuracy and temporal consistency at real-time speed. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/DYDevelop/DTC-TRUS.

</details>


### [50] [ExPLoRe: Expert Patch-Level Loss Routing for Multi-Objective Masked Image Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31201)
*Konstantinos Georgiou,Maofeng Tang,Hairong Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExPLoRe 提出了一种专家路径级别损失路由方法，利用 Soft MoE 的调度权重作为逐块损失系数，通过损失耦合机制实现内容依赖的多目标空间异质性加权，在 ImageNet-1K 上取得有竞争力的线性探测和微调性能，并开发了专门的 MoE 下游迁移适配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标掩码图像建模方法使用全局标量统一加权各目标，忽略了图像块之间的空间异质性——不同 patch 对不同学习目标的侧重应当不同。

Method: 提出 ExPLoRe，将 Soft Mixture of Experts 的调度权重（dispatch weights）重新利用为可学习的逐块损失系数。核心机制是损失耦合（loss-coupling）：让损失梯度通过调度权重流回路由器，使路由器能根据图像内容为不同 patch 分配不同的目标权重。通过 detach 消融实验验证该机制的有效性。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 上使用 ViT-Base：Token+CLS 组合提升 k-NN +0.5%、线性探测 +4.4%；Token+Pixel 组合提升 k-NN +2.2%；线性探测达 80.6%，微调达 85.3%。下游迁移方面，提出的 Freeze Routing、Expert Dropout 和 Freeze Attention 适配策略使 MoE 微调提升 +1.5%，在 ADE20K 分割任务上弥合了 2.5–2.9 mIoU 的差距。

Conclusion: ExPLoRe 通过损失耦合实现了内容感知的逐块多目标加权，性能具有竞争力；针对 MoE 下游迁移的适配策略有效解决了直接微调的性能差距问题，使 MoE 模型在下游任务上达到甚至超越非 MoE 基线。

Abstract: Multi-objective masked image modeling (MIM) combines complementary learning signals (token distillation, CLS alignment, and pixel reconstruction) but existing methods weight these objectives with global scalars, ignoring spatial heterogeneity across patches. We present ExPLoRe (Expert Patch-Level Loss Routing), which repurposes Soft Mixture of Experts (MoE) dispatch weights as learned, per-patch loss coefficients. The key mechanism is loss-coupling: allowing loss gradients to flow through dispatch weights to the router enables content-dependent specialization, where different patches receive different emphases across objectives. A detach ablation confirms loss-coupling as the core mechanism, degrading performance by 1.6% when gradients are blocked. On ImageNet-1K with ViT-Base, ExPLoRe improves over non-MoE baselines on two objective combinations (Token+CLS: +0.5% k-NN, +4.4% linear probe; Token+Pixel: +2.2% k-NN), achieving 80.6% linear probe and 85.3% finetuning accuracy, competitive with published methods. For downstream transfer, we develop adaptation recipes (Freeze Routing, Expert Dropout, and Freeze Attention) that improve MoE finetuning by +1.5% over the vanilla MoE, and close a 2.5--2.9 mIoU segmentation gap so that MoE models match or exceed non-MoE baselines on ADE20K.

</details>


### [51] [AC3S: Adaptive Conditioning for 3D-Aware Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31204)
*Eric Ji,Qiran Hu,Wufei Ma,Sarthak Jain,Yingying Li,Minh N. Do,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AC3S 提出了一种自适应条件控制的 3D 感知合成数据生成框架，通过自监督视觉提示调制器动态调节 ControlNet 条件强度以避免过度条件化，并结合多智能体 VLM 生成 3D 感知提示，实现高质量、结构一致的合成图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的合成数据生成方法虽具有强光真感，但难以同时保证精确的 3D 结构和姿态一致性。利用边缘图等视觉提示的方法常因过度条件化（over-conditioning）导致图像真实感下降，限制了数据集质量。

Method: 提出 AC3S 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自监督视觉提示调制器，动态调整 ControlNet 的条件强度，防止过度条件化，保留扩散模型的生成表现力；(2) 多智能体视觉语言模型框架，生成与底层几何结构对齐的细粒度 3D 感知提示，增强多样性与语义一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法显著提升了合成图像的质量及其在下游任务中的实用性，能够大规模生成带有精确 2D 和 3D 标注的高质量合成数据集。

Conclusion: AC3D 框架通过自适应条件控制与 3D 感知提示生成，有效解决了扩散模型在合成数据生成中的过度条件化问题，实现了光真感与 3D 结构一致性的兼顾，展现出优越的图像质量和下游应用价值。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation has emerged as a powerful tool for improving data scalability in computer vision. Recent diffusion-based pipelines have demonstrated strong photorealism. However, how to enforce precise 3D structure and pose consistency in generated images remains challenging. Existing methods leverage visual prompts such as edge maps to guide diffusion models, but often suffer from over-conditioning artifacts that degrade image realism and limit dataset quality. In this paper, we present a diffusion-based image generation framework that enforces 3D structural alignment while preserving photorealism through adaptive conditioning. Our framework, Adaptive Conditioning for 3D-Aware Synthetic Data Generation (AC3S), introduces a self-supervised visual prompt modulator that dynamically adjusts the strength of ControlNet conditioning, preventing over-conditioning and enabling the diffusion model to retain its generative expressiveness. To further enhance diversity and semantic consistency, we develop a multi-agent vision language model framework that composes detailed and 3D-aware prompts aligned with the underlying geometric structure. Together, these components enable the scalable generation of high-quality synthetic datasets with accurate 2D and 3D annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly improves image quality and downstream utility.

</details>


### [52] [AA: A Multi-view Multimodal Dataset for Screen-based Gaze Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31211)
*Chang Liu,Jiaqi Liu,Zhoutong Ye,Xinjie Shen,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AA是一个多视角多模态的屏幕式视线估计数据集，包含8个屏幕固定摄像头和2个侧面摄像头采集的同步面部数据，并提供精确的屏幕空间视线标注，支持全局与局部视觉线索的多模态学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视线估计数据集普遍采用单视角采集，在视角变化和遮挡场景下鲁棒性不足。作者旨在构建一个多视角数据集，从屏幕固定和侧面多个角度覆盖面部观测，以提升视线估计模型在视角变化和遮挡条件下的泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Method: 搭建多摄像头采集系统（8个屏幕边框固定摄像头 + 2个侧面摄像头），在受控注视条件下同步采集面部视频与屏幕空间视线坐标；对每个样本同时提供多视角面部全局图像和结构化面部区域裁剪，并制定受试者独立的评估划分方案和标准化数据处理流程。

Result: 构建了AA多视角多模态视线估计数据集，提供多视角覆盖的面部观测与精确视线标注，以及结构化的面部区域裁剪，支持全局与局部特征的多模态学习，并配套标准化的处理流程与评估方案。

Conclusion: AA数据集通过多视角设计和多模态数据结构，填补了屏幕式视线估计领域在多视角覆盖方面的空白，为视角鲁棒的视线估计研究提供了可复现的基准平台。

Abstract: We present AA, a multi-view multimodal dataset for screen-based gaze estimation. The dataset captures synchronized facial observations from eight fixed screen-mounted cameras and two additional side-view cameras, paired with precise screen-space gaze targets collected under controlled fixation conditions. Each sample contains multi-view face observations together with structured facial region crops, enabling multimodal learning from both global and local visual cues. Unlike existing single-view gaze datasets, AA provides multi-view coverage from both screen-mounted and side-mounted perspectives, enabling more robust modeling under viewpoint variation and occlusion. The dataset includes subject-independent evaluation splits and a standardized data processing pipeline to support reproducible research in gaze estimation.

</details>


### [53] [CooperScene: Multi-Modal Cooperative Autonomy Benchmark with C-V2X Communication Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31219)
*Bo Wu,Ruoshen Mo,Justin Yue,Yanyu Zhang,Janice Nguyen,Guoyuan Wu,Amit Roy-Chowdhury,Matthew J. Barth,Hang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CooperScene 是一个高保真协同自动驾驶数据集，核心特点在于纳入了真实世界的 C-V2X 通信特性，覆盖多智能体（3 辆 CAV + 1 个 RSU）、多模态传感器和多样化场景，旨在解决现有数据集在通信带宽动态性、异构感知和可扩展性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知数据集普遍忽略了真实部署中的关键挑战：有限的通信带宽及其动态变化、异构传感模态的融合、以及超越单一协同伙伴的可扩展性问题。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个更贴近实际部署条件的数据集和评测基准。

Method: 构建 CooperScene 数据集：涵盖交叉路口、高速公路匝道和停车场等多种场景；部署 3 辆 CAV 和 1 个路侧单元 (RSU)，均配备多模态传感器和商用现成 C-V2X 通信模块；所有场景以 10 Hz 频率标注全局一致的 3D 标签，总计 59K 帧、344K 个物体；通过严格的传感器/智能体同步、厘米级定位与空间对齐、精确跨模态标定及 3GPP 标准兼容的 C-V2X 通信来保证数据质量。

Result: 构建了一个包含 59K 帧、344K 个标注物体的高保真协同数据集，覆盖多样化场景，建立了面向真实可部署环境的多智能体缩放和实际性能评测基准。

Conclusion: CooperScene 为协同自动驾驶研究提供了一个更真实、更具挑战性的评测平台，特别是针对真实 C-V2X 通信条件下的多智能体协同感知、预测与规划任务，推动了该领域从仿真向真实部署的跨越。

Abstract: Cellular vehicle-to-everything (C-V2X) enables cooperative perception, prediction, and planning beyond the field of view of individual agents. However, existing datasets often overlook the complexities of real-world deployment, such as limited communication bandwidth and its dynamics, heterogeneous sensing modalities, and scalability beyond a single cooperative partner. In this paper, we introduce CooperScene, a high-fidelity cooperative autonomy dataset with real-world C-V2X communication characterization. The dataset is organized into diverse scenes, including intersections, highway ramps, and parking lots. These scenes involve three connected and autonomous vehicles (CAVs) and one infrastructure roadside unit (RSU), all equipped with multi-modal sensors and commercial off-the-shelf C-V2X communication radios. All scenes are annotated with globally consistent 3D labels at 10 Hz, totaling 344K objects across 59K frames, underpinned by tight sensor- and agent-synchronization, centimeter-level localization and spatial alignment, precise cross-modality calibration, and 3GPP-standard-compliant C-V2X communication. CooperScene establishes a rigorous benchmark for evaluating multi-agent scaling and actual performance in real-world deployable settings. Project website for data and benchmark: https://cisl.ucr.edu/CooperScene

</details>


### [54] [ForgeDrive: Bidirectional Cross-Conditioning for Unified Visual-Action Generation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31226)
*Xuchang Zhong,He Zheng,Chenxu Zhao,Tianxiong Lv,Hangqi Fan,Bohua Wang,Yushan Liu,Zhihao Liao,Leigang Luo,Congyang Zhao,Yang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForgeDrive 提出了一种统一的自回归扩散框架，通过"先行动后想象"范式和视觉-动作交叉条件建模，将驾驶仿真、规划与视觉里程计融为一体，在NAVSIM上超越了现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于世界模型的自动驾驶遵循"先想象后行动"范式，但视觉生成阶段的误差会级联影响动作规划，限制了整体性能。需要一种新的范式来弥合视觉生成与动作规划之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出ForgeDrive：1）将未来分解为逐时间步的帧-动作对序列，实现视觉与动作的交叉条件建模；2）训练时解耦两种模态的扩散时间步，采用UniDiffuser风格的噪声调度器，使模型能双向推断（从图像推动作、从动作推图像）；3）推理时采用"先行动后想象"范式——先生成动作，再利用生成的动作提升未来帧生成质量，形成正向循环；4）引入未来自车状态预测以增强规划能力。

Result: 在NAVSIM数据集上的大量实验表明，ForgeDrive 不仅将驾驶仿真、规划和视觉里程计统一到单一模型中，而且在不依赖任何后训练策略的情况下，超越了现有强规划器。

Conclusion: "先行动后想象"范式的ForgeDrive有效解决了传统"先想象后行动"的误差级联问题，通过动作与视觉的交叉条件建模实现了二者的相互增强，为世界模型驱动的自动驾驶提供了更鲁棒的统一框架。

Abstract: World-model-based autonomous driving endows the model with the ability to understand scene evolution. Yet this promise is undermined by the prevailing imagine-then-act paradigm, which allows errors from the more challenging visual generation stage to cascade into action planning. We introduce ForgeDrive, a unified autoregressive diffusion framework with visual-action cross-conditioning that closes this gap through act-then-imagine paradigm. ForgeDrive factorizes the future as a sequence of per-timestep frame-action pairs, intertwining each action with its corresponding visual observation. During training, we decouple the diffusion timesteps of the two modalities and introduce a UniDiffuser-style noise scheduler to get the ability to infer either modality from its counterpart and deepen understanding of relationships between images and actions. At inference, we propose a novel act-then-imagine inference paradigm, and find that at each step, action generation is a capability internalized during training, requiring no clean future frame as a prerequisite at inference time; instead, the generated action can improve the accuracy of future frame generation, which in turn enhances the quality of the next action. Additionally, we augment each step with future ego-status prediction, further sharpening planning ability. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM demonstrate that ForgeDrive not only unifies driving simulation, planning, and visual odometry into a single model, but also outperforms existing strong planners without any post-training strategy.

</details>


### [55] [UHD-MFF: Shattering Barriers in Multi-Focus Ultra-High-Definition Image Fusion via Learnable Lookup Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31242)
*Yibing Zhang,Xunpeng Yi,Qinglong Yan,Yeda Wang,Han Xu,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对超高清多焦点图像融合面临的三大障碍（数据、模型适应性、部署可行性），提出首个大规模超高清多焦点融合数据集UHD-MFF，以及基于查找表的UMF-LUT框架，包含粗区域查找表和细节边缘查找表，实现实时4K多焦点融合，性能优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多焦点图像融合方法主要局限于低分辨率图像，在超高清场景下面临三大障碍：数据可用性不足（缺乏大规模UHD数据集）、模型适应性差（现有模型难以处理超高分辨率）、部署可行性低（计算开销过大），严重阻碍了多焦点融合在实际应用中的推广。

Method: 提出UMF-LUT框架，由两个核心组件构成：(1) C-LUT（粗区域查找表）：在低分辨率尺度上联合查询多梯度线索和语义线索，实现区域级决策；(2) D-LUT（细节边缘查找表）：在高分辨率尺度上利用拉普拉斯线索，提供互补的边缘级决策信息。整体采用尺度特化的查找表设计，以极小计算开销实现超高清多焦点融合。

Result: 在视觉保真度和定量指标上均超越现有SOTA方法，可在极小计算开销下实现实时4K多焦点融合，展现出在智能手机等设备上的部署潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作有效推动了多焦点图像融合向超高清成像场景的发展，打破了数据、模型适应性和部署可行性三大壁垒，为UHD多焦点融合的实际应用（如智能手机端实时处理）开辟了新路径。

Abstract: With the advancement of imaging technology, ultra-high-definition images have become increasingly essential in modern visual applications. However, existing multi-focus image fusion remains largely confined to low-resolution images and faces three major barriers in UHD scenarios, namely data availability, model adaptability, and deployment feasibility, which severely hinder its practical application. To shatter these barriers, first, we propose the UHD-MFF dataset, the first large-scale ultra-high-resolution multi-focus fusion dataset. Second, we propose a scale-specialized lookup-table framework tailored for ultra-high-resolution images, termed as UMF-LUT. It consists of Coarse-Region Lookup Table (C-LUT) and Detail-Edge Lookup Table (D-LUT). Specifically, C-LUT performs joint queries of multiple gradient cues and semantic cues at low-resolution scales to enable region-level decision-making. Also, D-LUT operates at high-resolution scales, leveraging efficient Laplacian cues to provide complementary edge-level decision information. Such a design makes the model particularly well-suited for ultra-high-resolution multi-focus image fusion. Finally, it offers strong deployability with minimal computational overhead, enabling real-time 4K multi-focus fusion and showing promising potential for smartphone. Extensive experiments demonstrate that it outperforms SOTA methods in both visual fidelity and quantitative metrics. It effectively advances the development of multi-focus image fusion toward ultra-high-resolution imaging scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/zyb5/UHD-MFF.

</details>


### [56] [HyperVLP: Enhancing Hierarchical Surgical Video-Language Pre-training in Hyperbolic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31245)
*Yaojun Hu,Kun Yuan,Nassir Navab,Haochao Ying,Jian Wu,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双曲空间下的手术视频-语言预训练框架，通过显式保留手术知识的层次结构（动作→步骤→阶段），缓解了传统方法将异构信号压缩到单一平面嵌入空间所导致的结构性假阴性问题，并在多机构、多术式的零样本/少样本阶段识别任务上取得了持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视觉-语言基础模型将手术教育视频中的多维度、层次化知识（细粒度动作叙述、中粒度步骤标题、全局术前摘要）统一映射到平坦的嵌入空间，忽略了跨层次的语义包含关系（动作属于步骤、步骤构成阶段），导致长程依赖建模能力弱化，且容易产生结构性假阴性。

Method: 提出一种双曲手术视频-语言预训练框架（Hyperbolic Surgical VLP），核心包括两方面：1）通过双曲嵌入空间自然捕捉树状层次结构，缓解因忽略过程性上下文而产生的结构性假阴性；2）在父阶段与其组成子步骤之间施加语义一致性约束，显式建模跨层级语义包含关系。

Result: 在多个手术基准数据集上，该方法在跨术式和跨机构的零样本与少样本阶段识别任务中均取得了持续且一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模手术知识的层次结构对于手术视频理解至关重要。双曲空间因其对层次关系的天然表达能力，是比欧氏空间更适合手术视频-语言预训练的表征空间。

Abstract: Surgical vision-language foundation models typically adopt educational materials, such as surgical lecture videos, to transfer surgical knowledge encoded in language into visual representations. These knowledge are multi-dimensional and hierarchical: fine-grained action cues appear in narration, mid-level key steps are summarized in subsection headings, and global procedural context, such as patient history and surgical strategy, is described in abstract texts. Prior work largely collapses these heterogeneous signals into a single flat embedding space, implicitly assuming independence across hierarchy levels. However, this is suboptimal because it ignores cross-level semantic containment, e.g., actions belong to steps, steps compose phases, weakens long-range dependency modeling. To this end, we propose a hyperbolic surgical video-language pre-training framework that explicitly preserves the hierarchical structure by mitigating structural false negatives induced by procedural context and enforcing semantic consistency between parent phases and their constituent child steps. Extensive experiments on multiple surgical benchmarks show consistent gains in zero- and few-shot phase recognition across procedures and institutions.

</details>


### [57] [Rethinking the Role of Feature Engineering and Learning Strategies in Few-Shot Hidden Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31249)
*Xiaochuan Guo,Jihao Gu,Haixu Liu,Yuxin Liu,Qi Wang,Yufei Wang,Fei Wang,Kun Li,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XInsight Lab团队提出了一种紧凑的多模态时序建模框架，在第四届EI-MIGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 3中以0.76923的测试准确率获得第一名。该框架通过交叉注意力机制融合静态姿态特征与动态微运动差分特征，结合多示例学习自适应池化，解决了长视频中隐式情感证据弱且稀疏的难题，并揭示了通用视觉基础模型在微动态任务中的表示坍缩现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频中隐式情感证据具有弱且稀疏的特点，现有方法难以有效捕捉微动态情感线索。同时，通用视觉基础模型在微动态任务中存在表示坍缩问题，需要针对性地设计多模态时序建模方案。

Method: 提出紧凑的多模态时序建模框架：(1)融合多源特征，包括2D/3D骨架、面部Blendshapes、DINOv2/v3、X-CLIP视频特征和Gemini语义先验；(2)设计交叉注意力机制，以静态姿态特征(Base)为Query，动态微运动差分特征(Offset)为Key和Value，通过局部相对速度消除个体体型和身份的静态偏置；(3)采用基于多示例学习(MIL)的自适应池化方法提取瞬时情绪并抑制长序列背景噪声。

Result: 在第四届EI-MIGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 3中取得第一名，测试准确率达0.76923。实验揭示了通用视觉基础模型在微动态任务中的表示坍缩现象，分析了网络因捷径学习和机械记忆陷入"公开排行榜驱动的伪泛化"的底层机制。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态时序建模框架有效应对了长视频弱隐式情感识别挑战，交叉注意力与MIL自适应池化的组合设计在竞赛中验证了优越性。同时，对视觉基础模型表示坍缩的发现为未来微动态情感理解研究提供了重要警示与理论洞见。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the solution developed by our team, XInsight Lab, which achieved first place in Track 3 of the 4th EI-MIGA-IJCAI Challenge with a test accuracy of 0.76923. To address the challenge of weak and sparse implicit emotion evidence in long videos, this paper extends the winning solution from the previous competition and proposes a compact multi-modal temporal modeling framework. The framework integrates and evaluates the effects of multi-source features, including 2D/3D skeletons, facial expression Blendshapes, DINOv2/v3 vision foundation models, X-CLIP video features, and Gemini semantic priors. Architecturally, we propose a cross-attention mechanism that utilizes static pose features, denoted as Base, as the Query and dynamic micro-motion differential features, denoted as Offset, as the Key and Value. By capturing local relative velocities, this mechanism eliminates static biases related to individual body shape and identity. Concurrently, an adaptive pooling method based on Multiple Instance Learning is employed to extract instantaneous emotions while suppressing background noise in long sequences. Finally, the paper reveals the representation collapse phenomenon of general vision foundation models in micro-dynamic tasks, and analyzes the underlying mechanisms where networks fall into public-leaderboard-driven pseudo-generalization due to shortcut learning and rote memorization.

</details>


### [58] [Decodable Is Not Grounded: A Vision-Ablation Arbiter for VLM Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31257)
*Chih-Ting Liao,Fei Shen,Xin Cao,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 线性探针（linear probe）结合操控恢复（steering recovery）会系统性地夸大VLM对图像的真正视觉基础能力。通过将图像替换为灰色空白这一因果控制实验，论文揭示了三种被探针混淆的视觉基础模式：真实基础（vision-dependent）、先验替代（vision-independent prior）和倒置基础（inverted，可解码且可操控但符号错误）。在14个VLM中，水平轴为基础、垂直轴为先验、深度轴为倒置，该倒置现象随模型规模增大而出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前VLM潜在知识读取方法（线性探针+操控恢复）存在系统性缺陷：它们无法区分模型是真正基于视觉感知进行推理，还是依赖语言先验或错误表征，从而高估模型的视觉基础能力。

Method: 提出"空白图像仲裁法"（blank-image arbiter）：将输入图像替换为灰色空白作为因果控制条件。通过对比正常图像与空白图像下的探针解码和操控效果，区分三种视觉基础模式。同时使用无训练旋转（training-free rotation）和低秩编辑（low-rank edit）来纠正倒置表征，追踪不同模型的校正复杂度谱系。

Result: 在14个VLM（6个语言模型家族，2B–27B参数）的空间推理任务中：水平轴为真实视觉基础，垂直轴为语言先验，深度轴为倒置基础（模型得分低于随机）。倒置现象在8个模型中的7个上可复现，且随模型规模增大而出现。最小修正编辑方式因几何结构而异：最清晰模型可用无训练旋转匹配训练编辑，而分布式倒置则需要训练的低秩编辑。

Conclusion: 线性探针和操控恢复会系统性地夸大VLM的图像基础能力。空白图像仲裁法作为一种廉价、自校准的因果控制手段，能够清晰区分真实感知、倒置感知和先验替代，应成为VLM潜在知识和操控宣称的默认控制实验。

Abstract: The standard way to read latent knowledge out of a model, a linear probe confirmed by a steering recovery, can systematically overstate what a vision-language model (VLM) actually grounds in the image. We show this on spatial reasoning, where the error is invisible to both probing and steering yet exposed by a one-line causal control: replacing the image with a gray blank. Probes decode the within-axis answer at 73--97% across axes, and a training-free projection lifts a near-chance axis from 59% to 79%, exactly the signature of unlocking latent knowledge. The blank-image arbiter refutes it, revealing three grounding regimes that probing conflates: an axis can be grounded (vision-dependent, correct), a prior (vision-independent, with its decode and its apparent recovery a directional default rather than perception), or, surprisingly, inverted: decodable, causally controllable, but deployed with the wrong sign, so the model scores below chance and the error requires looking. The taxonomy holds across the studied VLMs: in fourteen models spanning six language-model families and 2B--27B, horizontal is grounded, vertical is a prior, and depth is inverted, with the inversion emerging at scale within families. The decode-versus-deploy inversion replicates on seven of eight models across five families, and the minimal edit that re-deploys it varies with geometry: a training-free rotation matches a trained edit on the cleanest model, while distributed inversions need a trained low-rank edit, tracing a per-model correction-complexity spectrum. The cheap, self-calibrating arbiter cleanly separates grounded perception, inverted perception, and prior substitution; we argue it should be a default control for latent-knowledge and steering claims in VLMs.

</details>


### [59] [WarpHammer: Densifying Scene Warps with 3D Object Priors for Extreme View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31258)
*Michael Green,Gavriel Habib,Dvir Samuel,Tal Berkovitz Shalev,Issar Tzachor,Rami Ben-Ari,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WarpHammer是一个无需训练的框架，通过引入原生3D生成先验（如SAM3D）重建的显式3D物体表征来增强投影条件新颖视图合成中的扭曲场景，解决了大视角变化下扭曲稀疏和伪影问题，并首次支持融合外部无位姿辅助视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影条件新颖视图合成方法在小视角变化下表现良好，但在大轨道运动下会急剧退化：扭曲视图在目标物体周围变得稀疏，隐藏表面主导新视图，出现镜像状伪影，导致生成器丢失像素内容和扭曲所携带的隐式相机线索。需要一种方法来解决这一失效模式。

Method: 提出WarpHammer：1）利用原生3D生成先验（SAM3D）获得物体的显式3D重建，将其增强到扭曲场景中，补充缺失的前景表面并遮挡不应再可见的背景点，恢复外观和相机线索；2）进一步利用该显式表征，将参考图像与来自外部源的辅助视图通过预训练多视图几何基础模型联合处理，预测统一点云并融合进3D物体重建中，无需用户提供辅助视图的相机位姿。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，WarpHammer在基线方法崩溃的大视角偏差下仍能生成稳定的新颖视图，并且是首个能够自然融合外部无位姿物体辅助视图的场景级新颖视图合成方法。

Conclusion: WarpHammer通过引入显式3D物体重建有效解决了投影条件NVS在大视角变化下的失效问题，并开辟了融合外部辅助视图的新能力，为场景级新颖视图合成提供了更鲁棒和灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Projection-conditioned novel view synthesis (NVS) warps an explicit 3D reconstruction of the input view into the target camera and conditions a generator on the warped rendering. This works well for small viewpoint changes but degrades sharply under large orbital motion: the warp becomes sparse around the orbited object, where hidden surfaces dominate the new view and mirror-like artifacts emerge, causing the generator to lose both pixel content and the implicit camera cue carried by the warp. We introduce WarpHammer, a training-free framework that resolves this failure mode by augmenting the warped scene with an explicit 3D reconstruction of the object obtained from a native 3D generative prior (e.g., SAM3D). The reconstructed object adds missing foreground surfaces and occludes background points that should no longer be visible, restoring both appearance and camera cues without fine-tuning the base model. The same explicit object representation further unlocks a capability current NVS pipelines do not support: incorporating auxiliary views of the object from sources outside the target scene, for example, a casual snapshot of a car paired with a manufacturer studio shot of the same model. We process the reference and auxiliary images jointly with a pretrained multi-view geometry foundation model, which predicts a unified point cloud that we fuse into the 3D object reconstruction. This yields substantially more faithful geometry than single-image reconstruction, without requiring user-provided camera poses for the auxiliary views. On five benchmarks, WarpHammer produces stable novel views at viewpoint deviations where strong baselines collapse, and is the first scene-level NVS method that can naturally fuse auxiliary, pose-unknown object views from an external source.

</details>


### [60] [Learning from Failure: Inference-Time Self-Improvement for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31270)
*Xueqiao Sun,Xiaohan Wang,Ludwig Schmidt,Serena Yeung-Levy,Yuhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以失败驱动的自改进循环，利用LLM对失败轨迹进行诊断、提出推理时解决方案并生成代码补丁，将失败经验转化为智能体能力提升。在OSWorld基准上，该方法使OpenCUA-72B的成功率从42.3%提升至48.9%（+6.6个百分点），无需额外训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机操作智能体的自改进范式仅利用成功轨迹进行微调，丢弃了蕴含模型弱点的失败轨迹。作者认为失败轨迹包含丰富的诊断信息，应当被系统性地利用来驱动智能体改进，以此作为对现有成功驱动管线的补充。

Method: 提出一种数据驱动的失败驱动自改进循环：首先使用LLM对失败轨迹进行自动化诊断，识别失败模式；然后针对诊断出的问题，由LLM提出推理时（inference-time）的解决方案；最后生成代码补丁（code patches），并经人类轻量验证后，对智能体进行升级。整个过程无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在OSWorld基准上，将SOTA模型OpenCUA-72B的成功率从42.3%提升至48.9%，获得6.6个百分点的绝对提升，且仅引入适度的推理开销，无额外训练成本。

Conclusion: 失败驱动的自改进是成功驱动管线的有效补充，能够在不增加训练成本的前提下，以轻量推理开销实现高效的智能体能力提升，为构建更高效的自改进循环提供了新方向。

Abstract: Computer-use agents, which leverage multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to operate computers and complete tasks, have attracted significant attention for their utility and versatility. A major challenge in developing these agents is collecting large-scale, high-quality trajectories. The standard approach generates synthetic data through a self-improving loop: an agent is placed in a verifiable environment and iteratively fine-tuned on its successful trajectories. Despite its effectiveness, this paradigm exploits only successful trajectories and discards the failed ones, even though failures carry rich information about a model's weaknesses. In this work, we explore a complementary failure-driven self-improvement loop, a data-centric paradigm that turns failed trajectories into agent improvements. Specifically, we employ an LLM to diagnose failure modes, propose inference-time solutions, and generate code patches -- lightly verified by humans -- that upgrade the agent. We validate this approach with the state-of-the-art OpenCUA-72B model on the OSWorld benchmark, improving the success rate from 42.3% to 48.9%, a gain of 6.6 percentage points, without any additional training cost and with only modest inference overhead. Our results demonstrate that failure-driven self-improvement is a viable complement to success-based pipelines, enabling more efficient agent improvement.

</details>


### [61] [CLIMB: Centroid-Based Hierarchical Memory for Online Continual Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31275)
*Julien Lefebvre,Stefan Duffner,Mathieu Lefort*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIMB 提出了一种结合层次化质心记忆库与知识蒸馏的在线持续自监督学习方法，在不规则任务分布下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线持续自监督学习（OCSSL）方法要么仅依赖重放缓冲区利用潜空间结构，要么仅依赖正则化，未能将两者有效结合。此外，现有基准评估通常假设规则的任务边界，与真实场景不符。

Method: CLIMB 方法包含两个核心组件：（1）层次化质心记忆库：将相似图像聚类为质心，在限制总存储图像数量的前提下，为对比学习提供难区分的样本，同时覆盖已观测分布的多样性；（2）对重放样本进行知识蒸馏，限制表征漂移。两者协同工作，实现在线持续学习。

Result: 在 Split CIFAR-100 和 Split ImageNet-100 的标准基准以及不规则任务分布的新协议上，CLIMB 均超越了当前最先进的 OCSSL 方法。

Conclusion: 将重放缓冲区与知识蒸馏相结合的 CLIMB 方法在 OCSSL 任务中表现优异，层次化质心记忆库有效平衡了样本多样性与判别性，为持续自监督学习提供了新的强基线。

Abstract: Online Continual Self-Supervised Learning (OCSSL) aims to learn representations from a continuous stream of unlabeled data, without knowledge of task boundaries and under memory constraints. Existing methods rely either on replay buffers that exploit latent space structure, or on regularization alone. We present CLIMB (Continual Learning with Intelligent Memory Bank), which combines both simultaneously. Our method introduces a hierarchical centroid-based memory, bounded in total number of stored images, combined with knowledge distillation on replayed examples to limit representation drift. The memory groups similar images into centroids, providing hard-to-discriminate examples for contrastive learning while covering the diversity of observed distributions. Experiments on Split CIFAR-100 and Split ImageNet-100, on standard benchmarks from the state-of-the-art as well as a new protocol with irregular task distributions show that CLIMB outperforms state-of-the-art OCSSL methods.

</details>


### [62] [Editing Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31278)
*Fabio Quattrini,Carmine Zaccagnino,Enis Simsar,Marta Tintoré Gazulla,Rita Cucchiara,Alessio Tonioni,Silvia Cascianelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MICE 是一种无需训练的多实例并发图像编辑策略，通过调控多模态扩散Transformer中联合注意力的加性偏置，实现可扩展的多实例编辑，有效抑制语义干扰和属性泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次前向多元素编辑方法中，当多条指令针对不同区域时，语义干扰导致属性泄漏和编辑解耦性差，且问题随编辑数量增加而加剧。需要一种能够实现可扩展、高保真的多实例并发编辑方法。

Method: 提出 MICE（Multi-Instance Concurrent Editing），一种基于多模态扩散Transformer的无训练策略。核心机制是修改联合注意力的加性偏置：允许实例内注意力、惩罚相邻区域token间的交互、抑制不相关的跨实例注意力。通过用户提供的分割掩码识别实例特定的编辑指令token、潜变量token和上下文token，实现属性绑定并保持全局视觉一致性。

Result: 在 LoMOE-Bench 上评估，并引入更具挑战性的 MICE-Bench（平均每图8.5个并发编辑）。实验表明 MICE 在视觉质量保持和编辑指令忠实度方面均优于强基线方法和近期竞争者。

Conclusion: MICE 通过调控注意力偏置实现高效、高保真的多实例并发编辑，无需额外训练，在复杂多编辑场景下展现出优越的可扩展性和编辑解耦能力。

Abstract: Editing multiple elements of an image in a single forward pass is a practical alternative to multi-turn image manipulation, offering improved efficiency and potentially better harmonization. However, when several instructions target different regions, semantic interference often leads to attribute leakage and poor edit disentanglement, especially as the number of edits increases. In this work, we propose MICE (Multi-Instance Concurrent Editing), a training-free strategy for scalable multi-instance image editing with Multimodal Diffusion Transformers. MICE modifies the additive bias of joint attention to regulate interactions between instance-specific edit instructions, latent, and context tokens identified via user-provided segmentation masks. Specifically, MICE allows intra-instance attention, penalizes interactions between neighboring region tokens, and suppresses unrelated cross-instance attention. As a result, our method enforces attribute binding while preserving global visual consistency. We evaluate MICE on LoMOE-Bench and introduce MICE-Bench, a more challenging benchmark with an average of 8.5 concurrent edits per image. The experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms strong baselines and recent competitors in terms of visual quality preservation and faithfulness to the editing instructions.

</details>


### [63] [Deep Spectral Models for Robust Dental Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31293)
*Tibor Kubík,François Guibault,Michal Španěl,Hervé Lombaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ToothForge 是一种深度谱生成框架，通过在谱域中对齐的谱嵌入来学习三维牙齿形状的潜在流形，解决了牙科数据量有限且网格连接不一致的问题，性能媲美甚至超越现有空间域方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙冠形态的精确建模对诊断、正畸规划和修复设计至关重要，但训练数据通常规模有限，且来自不同临床来源的形状难以保证一致的网格连接性，现有空间域方法对此类条件适应性不足。

Method: 提出 ToothForge 框架，核心包括：(1) 在谱域中操作，利用 Laplace-Beltrami 特征基将三维形状映射到紧致的内蕴表示；(2) 通过谱同步（spectral synchronization）对齐不同样本的谱嵌入，缓解特征基的不稳定性；(3) 在低维同步谱空间中学习牙齿形状的潜在流形。

Result: 通过鲁棒性分析、消融实验，以及与基于 PCA 的统计形状模型和基于点的生成框架的基准对比，结果表明同步谱建模在重建和生成性能上达到或超越空间域方法，同时保持紧致性和几何可解释性。

Conclusion: 同步谱系数与低维学习空间的结合，使该框架特别适用于牙科和医学领域中常见的小数据集场景，并在无法保证跨诊所形状连接一致性的真实场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of dental crown morphology is fundamental for diagnosis, orthodontic planning, and computer-aided restoration design. However, datasets suitable for training such models are typically limited in size. We present ToothForge, a deep spectral generative framework that models dental crown geometries from compact, intrinsic representations. By operating in the spectral domain, ToothForge learns a latent manifold of 3D tooth shapes through synchronized spectral embeddings, ensuring consistent modeling across samples with varying connectivity. Spectral synchronization mitigates the instability of Laplace-Beltrami eigenbases and enables efficient learning in a low-dimensional space. The framework is thoroughly evaluated through robustness analysis, ablation studies, and benchmarking against PCA-based statistical shape models and point-based generative frameworks. Results show that synchronized spectral modeling achieves reconstruction and generative performance comparable to or exceeding spatial approaches, while maintaining compactness and geometric interpretability. Together, the compact synchronized coefficients and low-dimensional learning space make the framework particularly suitable for limited datasets, as often encountered in dental and medical domains, and applicable in real-world scenarios where guaranteeing consistent connectivity across shapes from various clinics is unrealistic.

</details>


### [64] [Wavelet-Optimized Pseudo-3D Accelerated Diffusion Model for Truncated Computed Laminography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31318)
*Genyuan Zhang,Junyao Wang,Chuandong Tan,Fenglin Liu,Yongning Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CL-DM，一种小波优化的伪3D加速扩散模型，用于计算机层析成像（CL）截断伪影去除。通过扩展重建目标至数据不完全区域，并结合2D扩散切片聚合与3D MBIR数据一致性约束，辅以z方向小波正则化和平移不变机制，显著扩展有效成像范围并消除截断伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CL成像中视场限制导致投影数据截断，是一个不适定逆问题，产生严重重建伪影。现有深度学习方法存在两大缺陷：(1)基于2D架构，缺乏严格数据一致性约束；(2)伪影去除仅限于视场内，丢弃了视场外可恢复信息。

Method: 首先进行CL视场分析，将空间划分为数据完整区、数据不完全区和数据缺失区，将重建目标扩展至数据不完全区。核心方法CL-DM包含：(1)标准2D扩散模型进行切片聚合；(2)3D MBIR方法确保严格数据一致性；(3)沿z方向的小波正则化，配合平移不变(TI)机制和低频保持策略以缓解层间不连续性；(4)3D快速采样架构加速推理。

Result: 大量仿真和实际实验表明，CL-DM在消除截断伪影和恢复高保真、连续的3D结构方面表现优越，同时有效扩展了成像范围并提升了扫描效率。

Conclusion: CL-DM通过将重建目标扩展到数据不完全区域，结合扩散模型与物理驱动的MBIR方法，并引入小波正则化以保证层间连续性，成功实现了CL截断重建的高质量3D恢复，为板状大尺寸物体的无损检测提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Computed Laminography (CL) is a key technology for the nondestructive testing of large plate-shaped objects. However, field-of-view (FOV) limitations inevitably lead to truncation of projected data, an ill-posed inverse problem that causes severe reconstruction artifacts. Existing deep learning methods typically rely on 2D architectures that lack rigorous data consistency constraints. Furthermore, they conventionally confine artifact removal strictly to the FOV, discarding potentially recoverable information outside it. To overcome these limitations, we first introduce a comprehensive CL FOV analysis, categorizing the space into data-complete, data-incomplete, and data-free regions. By extending our reconstruction target to encompass the data-incomplete region, we significantly expand the effective imaging range and enhance scanning efficiency. To achieve this, we propose a novel wavelet-optimized pseudo-3D accelerated diffusion model for CL truncation reconstruction (CL-DM). Our method utilizes a standard 2D diffusion model for slice aggregation, combined with a 3D model-based iterative reconstruction (MBIR) method to ensure strict data consistency. To mitigate inter-slice discontinuities, we introduce wavelet regularization along the z-direction, paired with a translation-invariant (TI) mechanism and a low-frequency preservation strategy. Finally, we introduce a 3D fast sampling architecture, significantly accelerating inference speed. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that CL-DM is superior in effectively eliminating truncation artifacts and restoring high-fidelity, continuous 3D structures.

</details>


### [65] [Visual Semantic Entropy: Do Vision Language Models Recognize Visual Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31407)
*Ta Duc Huy,Trang Nguyen,Townim Chowdhury,Ankit Yadav,Minh-Son To,Zhibin Liao,Johan W. Verjans,Vu Minh Hieu Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型在视觉模糊输入上容易产生过度自信的预测。本文分析发现传统语义熵方法因视觉嵌入过度自信导致输出多样性不足，而现有基于输入扰动的方法又过度受文本变化主导。为此提出视觉语义熵(VSE)，仅对图像进行扰动来探测视觉变化，通过语义聚类和加权散度度量不确定性，在多个VLM和VQA基准上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM不确定性估计方法存在两大缺陷：(1) 基于输出多样性的语义熵(SE)方法在视觉嵌入过度自信时，随机解码无法产生足够的输出多样性，导致不确定性被低估；(2) 基于输入扰动（文本改写或图文联合扰动）的方法，其输出变异往往由文本变化主导而非视觉歧义，使得不确定性估计反映的是提示敏感性而非真正的视觉不确定性。因此需要一种能纯粹捕捉视觉歧义的不确定性估计方法。

Method: 提出Visual Semantic Entropy (VSE)：固定文本查询不变，仅对输入图像进行扰动（如添加噪声、几何变换等），生成邻近视觉变体；对每个变体的模型生成答案进行语义聚类，形成语义原型（semantic prototypes）；计算各原型之间的质量加权散度（mass-weighted dispersion）作为视觉不确定性度量。该设计确保输出变异仅来源于视觉变化，排除了文本因素的干扰。

Result: 在5个现代视觉语言模型和5个多样化的VQA基准数据集上进行了广泛评估，VSE在捕捉视觉歧义方面表现出色，在VLM不确定性估计任务上建立了新的最优水平(state-of-the-art)。

Conclusion: VSE通过仅扰动图像的方式，有效隔离并捕捉了视觉歧义对模型预测的影响，克服了SE低估不确定性和文本扰动方法过度反映提示敏感性的问题，为VLM不确定性估计提供了一种更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Vision-language models can produce confident answers on visually ambiguous inputs, resulting in biased predictions. Common entropy-based methods, such as Semantic Entropy (SE), rely on output diversity. Yet our analysis shows that overconfident visual embeddings suppress output diversity under stochastic decoding, causing SE to underestimate uncertainty in such cases. Recent methods instead probe output diversity through input perturbations, including textual paraphrasing or joint text-image perturbations, and show improved performance. We study these approaches and reveals that the resulting variability is often dominated by textual changes rather than visual evidence, causing uncertainty estimates to reflect prompt sensitivity rather than visual ambiguity. We therefore propose Visual Semantic Entropy (VSE), which perturbs only the image to probe nearby visual variations while keeping the text query fixed. VSE measures uncertainty by clustering generated answers into semantic prototypes and computing the mass-weighted dispersion among them. Extensive evaluation across five modern vision-language models and five diverse VQA benchmarks demonstrates that VSE effectively captures visual ambiguity, establishing a new state-of-the-art for VLM uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [66] [Accelerated Likelihood Maximization for Diffusion-based Versatile Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31323)
*Hyunsoo Lee,Inwoo Hwang,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALM 是一种无需训练的反向扩散采样策略，通过直接优化未观测变量并引入加速机制，实现了跨任务、全局一致的高质量内容生成，全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在面对部分输入的内容生成任务时存在两难：基于训练的方法任务特定性强但计算昂贵、泛化差；免训练方法效率高但缺乏对未观测变量的显式优化，导致全局不一致。因此需要一种兼顾效率与全局一致性的免训练方案。

Method: 提出 Accelerated Likelihood Maximization (ALM)，一种嵌入反向扩散过程的免训练采样策略。核心创新：(1) 在采样过程中直接对未观测区域进行显式优化，而非依赖预生成区域的隐式约束；(2) 引入加速策略，在保持性能的同时大幅提升计算效率。

Result: 在多种数据域和任务上的实验表明，ALM 一致性地超越了现有最先进方法，验证了其在通用内容生成范式中的优越性。

Conclusion: ALM 通过显式优化未观测变量并结合加速策略，突破了扩散模型在部分输入条件下生成全局一致内容的瓶颈，为通用内容生成建立了高效且强大的新范式。

Abstract: Generating diverse, coherent, and plausible content from partially given inputs remains a fundamental challenge for diffusion models. Existing approaches face clear limitations: training-based approaches offer strong task-specific results but require costly computation, and they generalize poorly across tasks. Training-free approaches offer better efficiency, but they do not explicitly optimize over unobserved variables, leading to globally inconsistent results. To address these limitations, we introduce Accelerated Likelihood Maximization (ALM), a novel training-free sampling strategy integrated into the reverse diffusion process that significantly extends the applicability of diffusion models beyond simple generation tasks. Unlike previous methods that implicitly influence missing regions through pre-generated region constraints, we directly optimize the unobserved region during the sampling process, enabling globally coherent and plausible generation. Furthermore, we incorporate an acceleration strategy that significantly improves computational efficiency without sacrificing performance. Experimental results demonstrate that ALM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in various data domains and tasks, establishing a powerful paradigm for versatile content generation.

</details>


### [67] [Bridging Video Understanding and Generation in a Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31326)
*Yuqi Wang,Runyi Li,Ruoyu Feng,Renjie Chen,Wenfeng Lin,Mingyu Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vega 是一个统一视频理解与生成的框架，通过共享词汇表联合建模文本与视觉表示，并采用自回归预测与扩散渲染相结合的混合架构，在视频生成（VBench）和视频理解（VideoMME）基准上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在图像领域已广泛探索统一理解与生成的建模范式，但视频领域仍处于起步阶段。核心挑战在于：视频理解偏好紧凑、判别性的语义表征，而视频生成需要保留视觉细节和时序一致性的稠密信号。视频天然同时包含空间语义与时序动态，比静态图像更适合统一多模态建模，因此亟需一个能弥合视频理解与生成鸿沟的统一框架。

Method: Vega 提出了一种混合架构：(1) 共享词汇表——统一建模文本与视觉 token；(2) 自回归（AR）模块——专注于预测关键帧的语义化视觉 token，提供结构化语义表征；(3) 扩散渲染模块——以 AR 模块输出的结构化表征为引导，渲染生成稠密、高分辨率的视频帧。AR 负责高层语义规划，扩散负责底层细节生成，二者协同工作。

Result: Vega 在视频生成基准 VBench 和视频理解基准 VideoMME 上均取得了强劲的性能表现，验证了统一框架在两类任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: Vega 成功将统一的图像理解与生成范式拓展到视频领域，通过自回归语义建模与扩散细节渲染的混合架构，有效解决了视频理解与生成在表征需求上的矛盾，证明了视频作为统一多模态建模模态的潜力。

Abstract: Recently, unified image generation and understanding have been extensively explored. However, extending such unified modeling paradigms to the video domain remains largely underexplored. A central challenge is that video understanding favors compact, discriminative semantic representations, whereas video generation requires dense signals that preserve visual details and temporal coherence. Videos naturally capture both spatial semantics and temporal dynamics, making them a more suitable modality for unified multimodal modeling compared to static images. In this paper, we propose Vega, a unified framework that bridges video understanding and generation. Vega leverages a shared vocabulary to jointly model text and visual representations and employs a hybrid architecture combining autoregressive (AR) prediction with diffusion-based rendering. Specifically, the AR model focuses on predicting semantically meaningful visual tokens for keyframes, providing a structured representation that guides the diffusion module in rendering dense, high-resolution video frames. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Vega achieves strong performance on video generation benchmarks such as VBench and video understanding benchmarks like VideoMME.

</details>


### [68] [Patient-Level Elbow Abnormality Detection: Leakage-Aware Evaluation of Learned Preprocessing, Calibration, and Triage-Oriented Operating Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31348)
*Ahmed Sallam,Ahmet Kaplan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在MURA肘部X线数据集上评估了含DnCNN学习预处理组件的多种预处理管线对骨科异常检测的影响。结果表明，各预处理策略间的差异有限且依赖于具体配置，未经预处理的DenseNet121基线在判别性能上始终具有竞争力，无一种预处理策略在所有指标上产生一致的判别优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究学习型预处理管线在分诊场景下对骨科X线异常检测的判别与校准性能的影响，弥补当前对学习型预处理方法在医学影像任务中实际增益缺乏系统性评估的研究空白，尤其是在防泄漏的患者级评估协议下的验证。

Method: 采用MURA数据集的肘部X线片，在患者级、防泄漏评估协议下，以DenseNet121为基线骨干网络，对比多种预处理策略：原始输入、CLAHE增强、以及是否叠加轻量级DnCNN作为学习型预处理前端。评估指标包括判别指标（AUROC、PR-AUC）、校准指标（ECE、Brier分数），并在高特异性目标下进行验证选定的操作点分析。

Result: 不同预处理策略间差异适度且依赖配置，无一种策略在判别指标上持续优于原始输入基线。原始输入与多样化输入结合DnCNN前端可降低ECE和Brier分数，改善校准；但CLAHE结合DnCNN未改善校准。总体而言，原始输入DenseNet121基线在所有指标上始终保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 在患者级评估下，学习型预处理带来的增益有限且高度依赖具体配置；简单原始的DenseNet121基线表现稳健且具有竞争力，提示在实际部署中应在引入预处理复杂度前审慎评估其实际收益。

Abstract: In this study, we examine learned preprocessing pipelines in the context of triage-oriented orthopedic abnormality detection task using elbow radiographs from MURA dataset. The evaluation focuses on patient-level detection of musculoskeletal abnormalities under a leakage-aware protocol. We compare multiple preprocessing pipelines, with and without a lightweight DnCNN module as a learned preprocessing component, to assess their impact on discrimination and calibration. Performance is assessed using discrimination metrics (AUROC, PR-AUC), calibration measures (ECE, Brier score), and validation-selected operating point analysis targeting high specificity. Results show that differences across preprocessing strategies are modest and configuration-dependent, with no consistent discrimination advantage over the raw-input DenseNet121 baseline. The raw and diverse inputs combined with the DnCNN front-end showed reduced ECE and Brier score, while CLAHE combined with DnCNN did not improve calibration. Overall, the results suggest that under patient-level evaluation, preprocessing gains are modest and configuration-dependent; the raw-input DenseNet121 baseline remains competitive throughout, and no tested preprocessing strategy produced a consistent discrimination advantage across all metrics.

</details>
