We propose LUNA, an LBS-free universal neural animation model that directly maps multiple 2D controls like images, keypoints, sketches, and unseen characters into 3D Gaussian deformations, bypassing explicit body fitting. At its core, a transformer-based motion regressor disentangles global rigid motion from fine-grained local dynamics to capture both coherent movement and subtle non-rigid effects. To resolve the inherent ambiguity of 2D-to-3D lifting while scaling beyond fitted datasets, we introduce hybrid supervision that distills soft structural priors from an LBS teacher and a loss that supports training on both limited fitted data and large in-the-wild unlabeled videos. Extensive experiments show LUNA achieves competitive visual fidelity compared to LBS-based approaches, while delivering realistic human motion and zero-shot cross-identity generalization across diverse driving modalities. To the best of our knowledge, LUNA is the first end-to-end 3D animatable model that supports implicit 2D driving.

</details>


### [138] [ERA: Entropy-Guided Visual Token Pruning with Rectified Attention for Efficient MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31982)
*Yuhao Wang,Mu Qiao,Haiwen Diao,Yunzhi Zhuge,Pingping Zhang,Xindong Zhang,Lei Zhang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ERA 提出了一种基于熵引导的视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过双重熵剪枝、偏差感知 token 回收和 logit 保持的注意力修正三个组件，解决了现有训练无关视觉 token 缩减方法中出现的"注意力 logit 崩塌"问题，在保持视觉证据的同时实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型因视觉 token 序列过长导致推理成本过高。现有训练无关的视觉 token 缩减方法虽然高效，但会扭曲注意力分布，引发"注意力 logit 崩塌"（Attention Logit Collapse）现象，损害模型性能。因此需要一种既能大幅压缩 token 又不破坏注意力结构的方法。

Method: ERA 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 双重视角熵剪枝（DEP）：联合建模视觉多样性和逐头显著性，识别代表性锚点 token；(2) 偏差感知 token 回收（BTR）：将剪枝掉的 token 回收到对应锚点中，同时估计聚类级 logit 偏差；(3) logit 保持注意力修正（LAR）：将估计的偏差注入注意力 logit，有效修正 token 缩减引发的注意力崩塌。

Result: ERA 在单图像、多图像和视频等多种设置下，在广泛的 MLLM 上均表现出鲁棒性能，即使在高压缩率下也能保持视觉证据，并带来实际推理加速。

Conclusion: ERA 将 logit 保持的视觉 token 剪枝确立为一个统一的、有原则的高效 MLLM 框架，实现了理论基础、算法设计和实际部署三者的有机统一。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) incur prohibitive inference costs due to long visual token sequences. Training-free visual token reduction provides an efficient solution. However, existing methods distort attention distributions, giving rise to a phenomenon we term Attention Logit Collapse. To address this issue, we propose ERA, an Entropy-guided visual token pruning framework with Rectified Attention for efficient MLLMs. Specifically, ERA comprises three crucial components: Dual-view Entropy Pruning (DEP), Bias-aware Token Recycling (BTR), and Logit-preserving Attention Rectification (LAR). First, DEP identifies representative anchor tokens by jointly modeling visual diversity and head-wise saliency. BTR then recycles pruned tokens into their corresponding anchors while estimating a cluster-level logit bias. Building upon this, LAR injects the estimated bias into attention logits, effectively rectifying the collapse induced by token reduction. Together, these components preserve visual evidence even under aggressive compression, enabling robust performance across single-image, multi-image, and video settings on a wide range of MLLMs. Beyond delivering practical acceleration, ERA establishes logit-preserving visual token pruning as a principled framework for efficient MLLMs, unifying theoretical foundation, algorithmic design, and practical deployment. The code is at https://github.com/924973292/ERA.

</details>


### [139] [CoLT: Teaching Multi-Modal Models to Think with Chain of Latent Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31986)
*Lianyu Hu,Shengqian Qin,Zeqin Liao,Qing Guo,Liang Wan,Wei Feng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoLT 提出用潜在思维链（Chain of Latent Thoughts）替代文本形式的链式推理，让多模态大模型在极少的潜在步骤（少至3步）中完成推理，大幅提升推理效率（推理时间减少10.1倍，文本解码时间减少22.6倍），同时通过前后向解码器监督和内部潜在转移监督保证推理质量，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本链式推理（CoT）存在两大痛点：一是推理时需生成数千个token，速度慢；二是自然语言表达力有限，束缚了推理能力。作者希望让模型在潜在空间中进行高效、紧凑的思考，而非依赖冗长的自然语言。

Method: 提出 CoLT 框架，核心包含三方面：(1) 让模型在潜在表示空间中执行链式推理，仅需少量步骤；(2) 引入轻量外部解码器，以前向模式（将当前潜在思维解码为下一步文本推理）和后向模式（基于前序文本上下文对齐解码器隐藏状态与潜在思维）提供步骤级监督；(3) 加入内部监督促进潜在状态之间的连贯转移。解码器和内部监督在推理时均被移除，保证高效性。

Result: 在8个基准测试上，CoLT 不仅优于 CODI、SIM-CoT 等现有潜在推理方法，还超越依赖辅助图像且标注成本高昂的潜在视觉推理方法。相比文本 CoT，推理时间减少 10.1 倍，文本解码时间减少 22.6 倍。

Conclusion: CoLT 验证了在潜在空间中进行链式推理的可行性与高效性，通过精巧的训练监督机制解决了单纯潜在推理训练不稳定、语义空洞的问题，在保持甚至提升推理性能的同时实现了显著的速度优势。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has enabled multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) to tackle complex visual reasoning tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, this text-based reasoning paradigm is inherently slow at inference time with even thousands of tokens and fundamentally constrained by the expressiveness of natural language. In this paper, we propose CoLT, (Chain of Latent Thoughts), a novel framework that teaches multi-modal models to reason through a chain of latent thought representations instead of verbose text tokens, which can perform thinking with as few as 3 steps. Naively forcing the model to think with latent states easily produces meaningless semantics and makes training unstable. To effectively regulate the latent reasoning process, we introduce a lightweight external decoder that provides step-level supervision for each latent reasoning step in two complementary directions: a forward mode that decodes latent thoughts into the textual reasoning of the next step, and a backward mode that aligns decoder hidden states with the model's latent thoughts given preceding textual context. We further incorporate internal supervision that encourages coherent step-by-step latent transitions. The decoder and internal supervision are removed during inference to maintain high efficiency of latent reasoning. Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that CoLT not only outperforms existing latent reasoning methods such as CODI and SIM-CoT, but also surpasses latent visual reasoning approaches that rely on auxiliary images with costly annotation requirements. Compared to text CoT methods, CoLT can notably reduce the inference time by 10.1$\times$ and text decoding time by 22.6$\times$. Code is released at https://github.com/hulianyuyy/CoLT.

</details>


### [140] [Cross-Space Distillation: Teaching One-Step Students with Modern Diffusion Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32020)
*Anh Nguyen,Ngan Nguyen,Duc Vu,Trung Dao,Viet Nguyen,Quan Dao,Kien Nguyen,Chi Tran,Phong Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen,Cuong Pham,Dimitris Metaxas,Vishal M. Patel,Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨空间蒸馏（Cross-Space Distillation），通过引入轻量级潜在空间接口Bridge，使不同潜在空间的高性能教师模型（如SD 3.5、Flux）能够蒸馏到紧凑的学生模型（如SD 1.5）中，突破共享空间假设限制，将SD 1.5的HPSv3得分从5.4提升至9.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代一步式扩散模型依赖分布级时间步蒸馏，但其核心假设——教师与学生必须处于同一潜在空间（Shared-Space）——限制了大容量教师模型向轻量部署友好型学生模型的知识迁移。SD 3.5、Flux等现代教师模型与SD 1.5等学生模型在潜在分辨率和VAE参数化上存在差异，无法直接蒸馏。

Method: 提出跨空间蒸馏框架，核心是Bridge——一个轻量级潜在空间接口。Bridge由两部分组成：冻结的学生VAE解码器作为空间先验，以及一个紧凑的可学习投影器。Bridge通过潜在重建损失和注意力保真度目标进行训练，将学生潜在表示映射到教师空间，实现稳定对齐，且不修改学生骨干网络。

Result: 在多种现代教师模型上验证，Bridge使紧凑的一步式学生模型获得显著提升。典型结果：SD 1.5的HPSv3得分从5.4提升至9.4，同时保持一步推理、低延迟和广泛的生态系统兼容性。

Conclusion: 异构大教师模型可通过轻量级潜在空间接口蒸馏到高效、可部署的学生骨干网络中，Bridge方法有效突破了共享空间限制，为模型压缩与部署开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Modern one-step diffusion models achieve impressive quality through distribution-based timestep distillation. Yet, they rely on a critical assumption: Teacher and Student must inhabit the same latent space. This Shared-Space constraint prevents knowledge transfer from modern high-capacity Teachers (e.g., SD 3.5 and Flux) into compact, deployment-friendly Students such as SD 1.5, whose latent resolution and VAE parameterization differ from the Teacher. We formalize this overlooked regime as Cross-Space Distillation, where Teacher and Student differ in both latent resolution and VAE space. To enable distillation under this mismatch, we introduce the Bridge, a lightweight latent interface that maps Student latents into the Teacher space without modifying the Student backbone. Bridge combines a frozen Student VAE decoder as a spatial prior with a compact learnable projector, and is trained with latent reconstruction and attention fidelity objectives for stable Teacher-space alignment. Across diverse modern Teachers, Bridge enables substantial gains for compact one-step Students; for example, it improves SD 1.5 from 5.4 to 9.4 HPSv3 while preserving one-step inference, low latency, and broad ecosystem compatibility. These results show that heterogeneous large Teachers can be distilled into efficient, deployable backbones through a lightweight latent-space interface.

</details>


### [141] [FLORA: A deep learning approach to predict forest attributes from heterogeneous LiDAR data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32023)
*Emilie Vautier,Clément Mallet,Cédric Vega*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLORA 是一个基于八叉树的深度学习框架，融合生态与时空辅助变量，从异质国家LiDAR点云中预测六种森林属性，在法国全境NFI数据上实现主高 rRMSE 12.3%、总蓄积量 rRMSE 39% 的性能，且单模型跨季节泛化优于季节特化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲各国正推进国家LiDAR项目，但传感器、飞行参数、季节和扫描角的差异导致现有模型（通常针对局部条件标定）难以实现稳健的墙到墙预测，制约了国家森林资源清查中的大规模应用。

Method: 提出 FLORA（Forest LiDAR Octree Regression with Auxiliary Data）框架：(1) 采用八叉树骨干网络直接处理异质点云，避免栅格化信息损失；(2) 通过晚期融合门控机制整合生态变量（气候、地形、土壤等）和时空辅助变量（采集季节、扫描角等）；(3) 在 32,052 个法国NFI样地上，使用 LiDAR HD 项目数据训练单一模型同时涵盖有叶/无叶季点云。

Result: (1) 单模型（混合季）在各项属性上均优于季节特化模型，跨季节鲁棒性显著提升；(2) 辅助变量整体增益适中，但在落叶/针叶材积等物种特异性预测上贡献更大；(3) 主高 rRMSE 约 12.3%（R²=0.88），总蓄积量 rRMSE 约 39%（R²=0.74），为异质国家LiDAR项目的大规模森林属性估算提供了稳健基线。

Conclusion: FLORA 证明了在异质国家LiDAR数据条件下，采用统一深度学习框架进行大尺度森林属性预测的可行性，八叉树表示与辅助变量门控融合是应对数据异质性的有效策略。

Abstract: Forest attributes are essential for national-scale resource monitoring. Airborne LiDAR metrics are among the auxiliary variables most strongly correlated with forest attributes used in National Forest Inventory (NFI) estimates. However, producing wall-to-wall predictions remains challenging when LiDAR data are acquired under heterogeneous conditions. As national LiDAR programs expand across Europe, variability in sensors, flight parameters, seasons, and scan angles limits the robustness of existing models, which are often calibrated for local conditions. We present FLORA (Forest LiDAR Octree Regression with Auxiliary Data), a deep learning framework that predicts six forest attributes: dominant height, total volume, deciduous volume, coniferous volume, basal area, and stem density from heterogeneous LiDAR point clouds. FLORA combines an octree-based backbone with ecological and spatiotemporal auxiliary variables through a late-fusion gating mechanism. Models are trained and evaluated on 32,052 National Forest Inventory plots across mainland France using data from the French LiDAR HD program. A single model trained on both leaf-on and leaf-off acquisitions outperforms season-specific models and improves cross-season robustness. Auxiliary variables provide modest overall gains but contribute more strongly to species-specific volume prediction. FLORA achieves an rRMSE of about 12.3% (R2 = 0.88) for dominant height and 39% (R2 = 0.74) for total volume, providing a robust baseline for large-scale forest attribute estimation from heterogeneous national LiDAR programs.

</details>


### [142] [SpheRoPE: Zero-Shot Optimization-Free 360 Panorama Generation with Spherical RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32033)
*Or Hirschorn,Aaron Olender,Eli Alshan,Ianir Ideses,Lior Fritz,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本、免训练、免优化的框架，通过在预训练扩散Transformer中注入球面先验来生成360°全景图像和视频。核心创新是球面旋转位置编码（Spherical RoPE），替代标准RoPE以在推理时解决等距矩形投影（ERP）的拓扑约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖稀缺全景数据上的昂贵微调（限制泛化），要么依赖多步优化导致推理延迟过高。观察到当代生成模型虽天然具备一些全景先验，但无法满足ERP的严格拓扑约束（如球面周期性和连续性），因此需要一种无需训练即可在推理时解决这些约束的方法。

Method: 提出两大核心技术：（1）Spherical RoPE：将低频通道重新参数化为3D笛卡尔坐标以原生编码球面流形，高频通道则进行谐波量化以强制精确周期性；（2）语义畸变无分类器引导（Semantic Distortion CFG），显式引导几何结构，避免ERP拉伸失真。整体框架为推理时即插即用，无需任何训练或优化。

Result: 在Flux.1、Flux.2和LTX-Video等多个骨干模型上验证了文本到全景图/视频的生成能力，在保持免训练的前提下取得了与基线方法相比具有竞争力的性能，并展现了跨骨干和跨模态的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过Spherical RoPE和Semantic Distortion CFG，可在不进行任何训练或优化的情况下，将标准扩散Transformer转化为高质量360°全景生成器，继承了现有最先进模型的全部创造力，同时严格满足球面拓扑约束。

Abstract: We present a zero-shot, training-free and optimization-free framework for generating 360 panoramic images and videos by directly injecting spherical priors into pre-trained diffusion transformers. Existing methods either rely on costly fine-tuning on scarce panoramic data that limits generalization, or leverage multi-step optimization that incurs prohibitive inference latency. We observe that contemporary generative models natively exhibit some panoramic priors from large-scale training. However, these emergent capabilities are insufficient, as the models fundamentally fail to satisfy the rigorous topological constraints imposed by equirectangular projection (ERP). We introduce a zero-shot and optimization-free approach that resolves these constraints at inference time. Spherical RoPE replaces standard rotary position embeddings: low-frequency channels are re-parameterized as 3D Cartesian coordinates to natively encode the spherical manifold, while high-frequency channels are harmonically quantized to enforce exact periodicity. Coupled with complementary Semantic Distortion classifier-free guidance (CFG) that explicitly steers geometry, we avoid retraining and inherit the full creative breadth of state-of-the-art models. Our approach generalizes across diverse backbones and 360 generation modalities. We demonstrate this across text-to-panorama using Flux.1, Flux.2, and LTX-Video backbones, achieving competitive performance against baselines, all while remaining training-free. Project page: https://orhir.github.io/SpheRoPE

</details>


### [143] [PointSplat: Compact Gaussian Splatting via Human-Centric Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32036)
*Yujie Guo,Yudong Jin,Lingteng Qiu,Zehong Shen,Zhen Xu,Jing Zhang,Xianchao Shen,Hujun Bao,Sida Peng,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointSplat 是一种从输入点集直接推断3D高斯原语的人体重建方法，通过在3D空间直接预测来消除多视图间冗余，实现紧凑高效的新视角渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建方法在多个视图中重复编码相同主体内容，导致显著的视图间冗余；而在沉浸式直播等场景中，需要在有限计算和带宽下同时保证表示紧凑性与高保真度。

Method: 首先通过粗几何代理估计并进行光线投射来剪枝冗余点并建立显式2D-3D对应关系；然后使用Point-Image Transformer融合外观与几何特征，在单次前向传播中预测高斯属性，将预测限制在前景感兴趣区域。

Result: 在多个数据集上，PointSplat 实现了比现有方法更高的效率与质量，且对视图数量和图像分辨率变化表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 直接在3D空间进行预测可有效消除视图间冗余，PointSplat 在紧凑性、渲染质量与鲁棒性方面均表现优越，适用于实时沉浸式直播等应用。

Abstract: Producing 3D human representations from input views on the fly is essential for immersive live streaming systems, where representation compactness is as critical as high fidelity given limited computational power and transmission bandwidth. Although recent feed-forward reconstruction methods achieve impressive quality through the view-centric prediction of 3D representations, they repeatedly encode the same subject content across multiple views, leading to significant inter-view redundancy. Our key insight is to perform predictions directly in 3D space, enabling the network to learn and produce a highly compact representation. To this end, we propose PointSplat, a novel human-centric approach that directly infers Gaussian primitives from an input point set. The proposed method first estimates a coarse geometric proxy and performs ray casting to prune redundant points and establish explicit 2D--3D correspondences. Subsequently, it employs a Point-Image Transformer to fuse appearance and geometry features, predicting Gaussian attributes in a single forward pass. This design restricts predictions to foreground regions of interest, substantially reducing the total number of Gaussians while improving novel-view rendering quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PointSplat achieves higher efficiency and quality while exhibiting strong robustness to variations in view count and image resolution across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [144] [GEAR: Guided End-to-End AutoRegression for Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32039)
*Bin Lin,Zheyuan Liu,Chenguo Lin,Sixiang Chen,Yunyang Ge,Yunlong Lin,Jianwei Zhang,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEAR 提出了一种端到端联合训练 VQ tokenizer 与 AR 生成器的框架，通过双路码本读出机制（硬分支做 next-token prediction，软分支传回表征对齐损失）解决了 VQ 索引不可微的问题，使 AR 模型能够引导 tokenizer 学习更易预测的表示，在 ImageNet 上 gFID 收敛速度相比 LlamaGen-REPA 基线提升最高 10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成模型将 tokenizer 训练与生成器训练解耦，tokenizer 仅关注重建质量，无法感知生成器对哪些表示更容易建模。这导致 tokenizer 产生的离散索引分布可能对后续的自回归生成器并不友好，限制了生成效率和质量。

Method: GEAR 设计了一种双路码本读出机制：硬分支使用 one-hot 离散索引输入 AR 模型进行标准的 next-token prediction 训练；软分支通过可微的 soft assignment 将表征对齐损失（如与 DINOv2 特征对齐）回传至 tokenizer，实现梯度流动。AR 模型的预测损失和表征对齐损失共同端到端优化，使 AR 引导 tokenizer 调整码本分配，而无需 straight-through estimator。该方法兼容多种量化器（VQVAE、LFQ、IBQ）。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 条件生成任务上，GEAR 的 gFID 收敛速度相比 LlamaGen-REPA 基线提升最高 10 倍；学习到的 patch 级特征和空间一致性特征显著更好；在文本到图像生成任务上也展现了泛化能力。与扩散模型侧"让 latent 本身语义化"的路线不同，GEAR 使 tokenizer 特征更少 DINOv2-like，而 AR 模型特征更加 DINOv2-like。

Conclusion: GEAR 成功实现了 VQ tokenizer 与 AR 生成器的端到端联合训练，通过表征对齐引导，将建模负担从 tokenizer 转移到生成器，在不牺牲重建质量的前提下大幅提升了自回归视觉生成的训练效率和特征质量，且方法具有良好的量化器兼容性和跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: Visual generative models are typically trained in two stages. A tokenizer is first trained for reconstruction and then frozen, after which a generator is trained on its discrete indices or continuous latents. This decoupling leaves the tokenizer unaware of what the generator finds easy to model. We present GEAR (Guided End-to-end AutoRegression), which trains a vector-quantized (VQ) tokenizer and an autoregressive (AR) generator jointly and end-to-end, guided by representation alignment. The key obstacle is that the VQ index fed to the AR model is non-differentiable, so gradients cannot reach the tokenizer, and a straight-through estimator collapses. GEAR resolves this with a dual read-out of the codebook assignment. A hard, one-hot branch trains the AR with next-token prediction, while a differentiable soft branch carries a representation-alignment loss that flows back to guide only the tokenizer. The AR model thereby steers its tokenizer toward an index distribution it can predict more easily. This shifts the alignment burden from the tokenizer to the AR: the tokenizer's own features become less DINOv2-like while the AR's become more so, the opposite of diffusion-side recipes that make the latent itself semantic. GEAR speeds up ImageNet gFID convergence by up to 10x relative to the strong LlamaGen-REPA baseline, learns markedly better patch-level and spatially-coherent features, and generalizes across quantizers (VQVAE, LFQ, IBQ) and to text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [145] [FaceMoE: Mixture of Experts for Low-Resolution Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32040)
*Kartik Narayan,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceMoE 将混合专家（MoE）架构引入低分辨率人脸识别，通过多个专业化 FFN 专家和 top-k 路由器实现分辨率感知的特征提取，在保持预训练知识的同时提升模型容量，无需成比例增加计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低分辨率人脸识别面临两大核心挑战：一是低质量图像导致特征提取与聚合困难；二是HR画廊与LR探针图像之间存在严重域差距，单一编码器难以跨域泛化，且在下游LR数据集微调时会出现灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出 FaceMoE，将 MoE Transformer 架构适配到低分辨率人脸识别：引入多个专门的 FFN 专家，配合 top-k 路由器对 token 进行动态分配，使不同专家自发专注于人脸不同语义区域；训练时联合使用人脸识别损失、路由器 z-loss 和负载均衡损失，保证专家专业化与训练稳定性。

Result: 在11个数据集（涵盖HR、混合质量、LR基准）上广泛实验，FaceMoE 显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 这是首个将 MoE 应用于低分辨率人脸识别的工作，证明了动态稀疏专家激活策略能够有效弥合HR-LR域差距，且在提升识别性能的同时避免了灾难性遗忘和过高计算开销。

Abstract: Low-resolution face recognition (LR-FR) remains a challenging task due to poor feature extraction and aggregation, as probe images often contain limited identity information resulting from extreme degradations such as blur, occlusion, and low contrast. Additionally, the domain gap between high-resolution (HR) gallery images and low-resolution (LR) probe images poses a significant challenge. A single feature encoder struggles to generalize effectively across both domains when fine-tuned on an LR dataset, and this issue is further magnified by catastrophic forgetting. To address these challenges, we propose FaceMoE, an effective adaptation of Mixture of Experts (MoE) transfomer architecture for low-resolution face-recognition . Specifically, we introduce multiple specialized feed-forward network (FFN) experts and incorporate a top-k router, which dynamically assigns tokens to appropriate experts. This design emergently promotes specialization across experts for different semantic regions of the face, which enables FaceMoE to perform resolution-aware feature extraction. Moreover, the top-k router facilitates sparse expert activation, enabling the model to preserve pretrained knowledge when finetuned on a LR dataset, while increasing model capacity without proportional computational overhead. FaceMoE is trained with a combined face recognition loss, router z-loss, and load balancing loss to ensure expert specialization and stable training. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work leveraging MoE for LR-FR. Extensive experiments across eleven datasets, spanning HR, mixed-quality, and LR benchmarks, demonstrate that FaceMoE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code: https://github.com/Kartik-3004/FaceMoE

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [A Single Rewrite Suffices: Empirical Lessons from Production Skill Description Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30775)
*Yangqiaoyu Zhou,Mohammad Alqudah,Kwei-Herng Lai,Aaron Halfaker,Yingqi Xiong,Yaar Harari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 企业AI Agent在路由用户查询时，因技能描述重叠导致"技能冲突"，手动调优描述费时费力。本文提出一种自动化描述优化流水线，在9技能的生产级企业群聊Agent上达到79.2% F1，匹配人工调优水平（79.4%），并将工程耗时从120分钟降至3.8分钟（32倍加速）。消融实验表明，单次LLM重写利用误报/漏报案例即可捕获大部分收益；其他设计选择影响均不足0.5%。此外，通过训练-验证F1差距诊断可识别真正的语义重叠场景，需架构层面而非文本层面干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI Agent依赖自然语言技能描述进行查询路由，当多个技能描述重叠时，路由LLM会错误分配查询，导致"技能冲突"。随着Agent扩展至数十个技能，手动调优描述以维持路由准确率成为严重工程瓶颈，亟需自动化方案。

Method: 提出自动化描述优化流水线（Description Optimization Pipeline），核心思路是利用LLM基于误报（false-positive）和漏报（false-negative）案例对技能描述进行重写优化。在生产级企业群聊Agent（9技能、372个回归案例）上部署验证，并在ToolBench（16k工具）上进行系统性消融实验，考察迭代预算、反馈信号组合、混淆对偶编辑、训练集规模等设计维度对F1的影响。同时引入训练-验证F1差距作为诊断指标，区分文本可修复的描述重叠与架构层面的真实语义重叠。

Result: 该流水线生成的描述平均F1达79.2%，与人工调优的79.4%相当（平均每技能差异仅-0.20%，处于0.78%多种子噪声地板范围内），同时将每技能工程耗时从120分钟压缩至3.8分钟（32倍加速）。消融实验揭示：单次LLM重写并利用任意可用的FP/FN案例即可捕获绝大多数可获取的改进；迭代预算、反馈信号组成、混淆对偶编辑、训练集规模等其他设计选择对最终F1影响均低于0.5%。训练-验证F1差距大的场景被诊断为真实语义重叠，需架构层面而非文本层面干预。

Conclusion: 自动化描述优化可有效解决因描述重叠导致的技能冲突，在匹配人工调优水平的同时大幅降低工程成本。关键发现是简单方案（单次LLM重写+少量案例反馈）已足够有效，复杂设计增量收益有限。对于真实语义重叠的技能，描述优化无法解决，需通过训练-验证F1差距诊断识别并诉诸架构层面改进。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents route user queries to specialized skills by matching queries against natural language skill descriptions. When two skills share overlapping descriptions, the routing LLM misroutes queries, a failure we term skill collision. As agents scale to dozens of skills, manually tuning descriptions to maintain routing accuracy becomes a significant engineering bottleneck. We deploy an automated description optimization pipeline on a production enterprise group chat agent (9 skills, 372 regression cases). The pipeline produces descriptions averaging 79.2% F1, matching manually tuned descriptions at 79.4% F1 (average per-skill difference -0.20%, within the 0.78% multi-seed noise floor), while reducing per-skill engineering effort from 120 minutes to 3.8 minutes (32 times speedup). We then examine which pipeline components actually drive this match. Systematic ablation on both the production system and ToolBench (16k tools) reveals that a single LLM rewrite using any available false-positive and false-negative cases captures most of the available improvement. Other design choices we tested (iteration budget, feedback signal composition, dual editing of confused pairs, and training set size) each affect final F1 by less than 0.5%. Description optimization addresses skill collisions caused by overlapping descriptions but cannot resolve cases where two skills intended scopes genuinely overlap. We identify a diagnostic (a large train-validation F1 gap) that flags the latter cases for architectural rather than text-level intervention.

</details>


### [147] [Indi-RomCoM: Code-Mixed Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on Romanized Indic-English Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30790)
*Avisha Das,Mihir Parmar,Mohana Ramnath,Pulkit Verma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 Indi-RomCoM 基准，系统评估大语言模型在印度罗曼化代码混合指令上的表现，发现LLM在RCM场景下性能显著下降，且混合密度越高性能越差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罗曼化代码混合（RCM）已成为多语言社区的主流交流方式，但LLM在RCM内容上的指令遵循与推理能力尚未被充分探索，亟需一个系统性的评估框架。

Method: 构建Indi-RomCoM基准，覆盖7种指令遵循任务、4种印度语言（印地语、孟加拉语、泰米尔语、泰卢固语）、3种代码混合强度等级；在零样本和少样本设定下，对闭源、开源及Indic专用LLM进行全面评估。

Result: LLM在RCM指令上一致表现不佳，随代码混合密度增加性能持续下降；推理任务比检测任务（如毒性检测）退化更少，因为生成的解释提供了必要的上下文信息。

Conclusion: Indi-RomCoM为构建包容性多语言系统提供了有价值的评估工具，揭示了当前LLM在RCM场景下的显著短板。

Abstract: Romanized Code Mixing (RCM), where bilingual speakers fluidly blend local languages with English in Roman script, has emerged as the dominant form of communication across multilingual communities. While Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on monolingual and native-script benchmarks, their ability to follow instructions and reason over RCM-based content remains largely unexplored. To this end, we introduce the Indi-RomCoM benchmark for facilitating systematic evaluation on Indic Romanized Code-Mixed instructions. Our benchmark spans seven instruction-following tasks, four widely spoken Indic languages, and three controlled code-mixing intensity levels. We extensively evaluate a suite of LLMs covering proprietary, open-weight, and Indic-focused models under zero- and few-shot settings. LLMs consistently underperform on RCM instructions, with performance degrading as code-mixing density increases. Furthermore, reasoning tasks suffer less degradation than detection tasks (e.g., Toxicity) because the generated explanations offer necessary context. We believe Indi-RomCoM helps the community in developing inclusive multilingual systems.

</details>


### [148] [Using AI Agents to Automate Black-Box Audits of Personalization Algorithms at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30801)
*Alessandro Morosini,Sarah H. Cen,Andrew Ilyas,Hedi Driss,Aleksander Mądry,Chara Podimata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用生成式AI智能体作为合成账户的行为引擎，对个性化推荐算法进行黑箱审计。通过固定人设、实验性操控人口统计信号，实现反事实审计。在X平台部署1,120个智能体，发现算法推荐放大了有毒、极化、政治性和右倾内容，且人口统计信号的影响因人设而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法审计方法面临两难：真实用户研究成本高且难以控制，傀儡账户审计虽然可扩展但行为脚本化、缺乏真实感。更关键的是，两种方法都难以将用户属性与用户行为解耦，无法对个性化算法进行因果推断。

Method: 提出基于生成式AI智能体的黑箱审计框架：每个智能体被赋予基于人口统计和政治调查数据的固定人设，通过对内容进行推理并选择交互行为。由于行为在每个人设内部保持固定，而平台可见信号（年龄、性别、地理位置等）可被实验性操控，从而实现对平台如何响应用户属性的反事实审计。案例研究中在2024年美国大选后不久于X平台部署1,120个智能体，覆盖14种人设和3种反事实条件，收集超20万次内容曝光。

Result: X的算法推荐流相较时间线流放大了有毒、极化、政治性和右倾内容，放大效应随用户意识形态显著变化。反事实分析显示，人口统计信号对人设依赖型内容分发有影响：合并效应大体为零，但子群体层面的效应在方向和强度上各不相同。

Conclusion: GenAI智能体可作为算法审计的新工具，能够实现用户属性与行为的解耦，支持反事实因果推断，为个性化推荐系统的审计提供了兼具可控性和真实感的新范式。

Abstract: Personalization algorithms determine what content users encounter on online platforms. Auditing these systems is difficult because independent auditors have only black-box access to the algorithms, while personalization depends on users' attributes, behavior, and evolving interaction histories. Existing auditing methods face a tradeoff: studies with real users capture realistic behavior but are costly and hard to control, whereas sock-puppet audits scale more easily but often rely on scripted behavior that limits realism. Beyond this, both approaches struggle to decouple user attributes from user behavior, limiting our ability to causally understand personalization. To address this gap, we introduce a framework for black-box audits of personalization algorithms using generative AI agents as behavioral engines for synthetic accounts. Each agent is instantiated with a fixed persona, grounded in demographic and political survey data, and interacts with a platform's content by reasoning about it and choosing actions. Because behavior is fixed within each persona while platform-visible signals such as age, gender, or location can be experimentally perturbed, our design enables counterfactual auditing of how platforms respond to user attributes. As a case study, we deploy 1,120 agents on X shortly after the 2024 U.S. election, spanning 14 personas and three counterfactual conditions, collecting over 200,000 content exposures. We find that X's algorithmic feed amplifies toxic, polarizing, political, and right-leaning content relative to the chronological feed, with amplification varying sharply by user ideology. Counterfactual analyses show that demographic signals affect content delivery in persona-dependent ways: pooled effects are largely null, while subgroup-level effects vary in direction and magnitude. Our work establishes GenAI-based agents as a new tool for algorithmic auditing.

</details>


### [149] [When Calibration Rankings Reverse: Accuracy-Controlled Evaluation for Fair Comparison of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30814)
*Zhichao Yang,Caiqi Zhang,Ruihan Yang,Chengzu Li,Nigel Collier,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了传统全局校准指标（如ECE、Brier Score）在跨模型比较中存在准确性混杂偏倚，并提出了ACE（Accuracy-Controlled Evaluation）框架，从实例对齐、分布对齐和候选对齐三个视角实现公平的校准评估，发现此前报告的校准优势在控制准确性后显著减弱甚至反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍使用Expected Calibration Error (ECE)和Brier Score等全局校准指标来比较不同大语言模型的校准性能。然而，这些指标与模型准确性天然耦合，导致准确性差异会混杂校准比较的结论。作者从理论和实证两方面证明了这一偏倚，并指出当前缺乏对准确性因素进行控制的公平校准评估框架。

Method: 提出ACE（Accuracy-Controlled Evaluation）框架，包含三个互补的校准评估视角：(1) Instance-Aligned Calibration：在模型预测一致的实例子集上比较校准；(2) Distribution-Aligned Calibration：通过重采样使不同模型的准确率分布对齐后比较校准；(3) Candidate-Aligned Calibration：在候选答案层面控制准确性后评估校准。框架兼容多种置信度诱导方法，并在多个基准和模型家族上验证。

Result: 实验覆盖多个基准、模型家族和置信度诱导方法，聚焦两个重要比较轴——小模型 vs 大模型、思考型 vs 非思考型模型。发现：(1) 原始全局指标下报告的校准优势在准确性控制后大幅削弱；(2) 排序反转现象频繁出现，原始指标偏好的模型在控制准确性后往往不再占优；(3) 原始全局校准指标对跨模型比较不具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨模型的公平校准比较必须控制准确性因素，原始全局校准指标（ECE、Brier Score等）因受准确性混杂影响，不适合直接用于跨模型校准比较。ACE框架为校准评估提供了更公平、更细粒度的替代方案，对LLM校准研究和实践具有重要的方法论意义。

Abstract: Calibration evaluates whether a model confidence aligns with its empirical accuracy. Existing studies often compare the calibration of different large language models using global calibration metrics such as Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score. We begin by showing, both theoretically and empirically, that such comparisons are confounded by differences in model accuracy. For fairer cross-model comparison, we then propose ACE, an accuracy-controlled evaluation framework with three complementary views: Instance-Aligned, Distribution-Aligned, and Candidate-Aligned calibration. Across multiple benchmarks, model families, and confidence elicitation methods, we use ACE to study two practically important comparison axes, small versus large models and thinking versus non-thinking models. We find that many previously reported calibration advantages under raw global metrics weaken substantially after accuracy control. We also find that ranking reversal is frequent: models favored by raw metrics often cease to be favored once accuracy is controlled. Our results show that raw global calibration metrics are not robust for cross-model comparison, and that fair calibration comparison requires accuracy-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [150] [When transformers learn "impossible" languages, what do they learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30815)
*Ram Janarthan,Coleman Haley,Sharon Goldwater*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文考察了Transformer语言模型对人类语言与"不可能"语言的偏好差异。与以往仅关注样本效率和测试集困惑度的研究不同，作者通过语法敏感性（BLiMP最小对）和生成质量两个维度直接评估模型的语言能力。结果表明，模型在语法敏感性上仅呈现渐进式退化（受信息局部性调节），但在长句生成质量上出现显著衰退，提示生成缺陷和传播失败是"不可能"语言不在人类语言中出现的合理解释机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅通过样本效率和困惑度差异来论证语言模型对人类语言存在偏好，缺乏对具体语言能力的直接评估。作者旨在通过可检验的语言学假设（语法敏感性缺陷 vs. 生成缺陷）来解释为何某些"不可能"语言在人类语言中未被证实存在。

Method: 使用GPT-2架构的模型，在扰动后的"不可能"英语变体上进行训练。通过两个维度进行评估：(1) 使用BLiMP语法最小对数据集测量语法敏感性；(2) 评估模型在不同长度下的生成质量。

Result: (1) 语法敏感性方面，模型仅呈现渐进式退化，且退化程度受语言的信息局部性（information locality）调节；(2) 生成方面，模型在较长句子上出现显著失败，生成的高质量句子数量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 生成缺陷（generative deficiency）和传播失败（transmission failures）是连接语言模型行为与"不可能"语言未被人类语言证实的合理机制假设，而非语法敏感性不足。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that transformer language models show a bias towards human languages over unnatural ("impossible") languages argued to be unacquirable by humans. However, this literature has largely based these claims on differences in sample efficiency and test-set perplexity, rather than on direct evaluations of the linguistic capacities that could plausibly explain non-attestation in human languages. We evaluate two theoretically motivated linking hypotheses: impossibility arising from deficiencies in grammatical sensitivity or generative production. Using GPT-2 style models trained on perturbed "impossible" variants of English, we measure sensitivity to grammaticality using BLiMP minimal pairs, finding that model performance exhibits only gradual degradation, mediated by the language's information locality. In contrast, these models exhibited pronounced failures in generation, producing substantially fewer high-quality sentences at longer lengths. Together, these results suggest generative deficiency and transmission failures as a plausible linking hypothesis between language model behaviour and non-attestation of impossible languages.

</details>


### [151] [Test-Time Verification for Text-to-SQL via Outcome Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30851)
*Mattia Tritto,Giuseppe Farano,Dario Di Palma,Gaetano Rossiello,Fedelucio Narducci,Dharmashankar Subramanian,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GradeSQL框架，通过自动生成候选SQL与执行结果标注，训练面向Text-to-SQL的Outcome Reward Model (ORM)，在推理时以语义验证替代启发式选择策略，在BIRD和Spider基准上分别取得最高+4.33%和+2.10%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在Text-to-SQL等结构化推理任务中的推理时策略（如Best-of-N采样、多数投票）依赖执行成功或输出频率等启发式信号，缺乏对候选输出的语义区分能力，导致可靠性不足。

Method: 提出GradeSQL框架：(1) 通过自动候选生成和执行结果比对实现无人工标注的ORM训练数据构造；(2) 训练任务特定的ORM作为语义评分函数；(3) 将ORM集成到验证驱动的Best-of-N流水线中，对候选SQL进行语义质量排序与筛选。

Result: 在BIRD和Spider基准上，多个开源LLM家族的实验表明：ORM选择一致优于基于执行的Best-of-N和多数投票，BIRD最高提升+4.33%，Spider最高提升+2.10%；ORM随候选集规模扩大而有效扩展，且在复杂查询上增益更显著。

Conclusion: ORM驱动的验证为Text-to-SQL提供了一种简单、有效且可扩展的启发式推理时选择策略的替代方案，无需人工标注即可训练，具备良好的规模化特性。

Abstract: Improving the reliability of large language models (LLMs) at inference time is a central challenge in structured reasoning tasks such as Text-to-SQL. Common test-time inference strategies, including Best-of-N sampling and Majority Voting, rely on heuristic signals such as execution success or output frequency, which provide limited semantic discrimination across candidate outputs. In this work, we study Outcome Reward Models (ORMs) as learned semantic scoring functions for test-time verification in Text-to-SQL. While ORMs have been previously explored for test-time scaling and alignment, their application to structured query generation remains underexplored. We introduce GradeSQL, a scalable framework for training task-specific ORMs via automated candidate generation and execution-based labeling, enabling verifier training without manual annotation. We integrate ORMs into a verification-driven Best-of-N pipeline and evaluate our approach on the BIRD and Spider benchmarks across multiple open-source LLM families. ORM-based selection consistently outperforms execution-based Best-of-N and Majority Voting, with gains of up to +4.33% on BIRD and +2.10% on Spider. We further show that ORMs scale effectively with larger candidate sets and yield stronger improvements on complex queries. Overall, our results demonstrate that ORM-based verification provides a simple, effective, and scalable alternative to heuristic test-time selection strategies for Text-to-SQL. Code datasets and models are publicly available.

</details>


### [152] [Multilingual Polarization Detection Using Transformer-Based Models with Class Weighting and Threshold Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30857)
*Aaron Bundi Anampiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer模型的多语言多事件在线极化检测系统，针对英语和斯瓦希里语，通过类别加权损失函数和标签阈值调优解决严重的标签不平衡问题，在SemEval-2026 Task 9三个子任务上均取得有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线极化检测面临多语言、多文化、多事件的复杂场景，且存在严重的标签不平衡问题，现有方法难以有效处理这些挑战。作者旨在构建一个能同时处理英语和斯瓦希里语的统一框架，覆盖二分类极化检测、极化类型分类和表现形式识别三个子任务。

Method: 采用双模型策略：英语使用RoBERTa-base，斯瓦希里语使用AfroXLMR-base。针对严重的标签不平衡问题，引入类别加权损失函数（class-weighted loss）；在多标签分类子任务中，采用逐标签阈值调优（per-label threshold tuning）策略以优化分类边界。整个框架覆盖三个子任务的全流程。

Result: 测试集上，子任务1（二分类极化检测）：英语F1 macro 0.7901，斯瓦希里语0.7910；子任务2（极化类型分类）：英语0.4615，斯瓦希里语0.4808；子任务3（表现形式识别）：英语0.4791，斯瓦希里语0.5830。在排行榜上具有竞争力。错误分析表明模型在去人化（dehumanization）检测和共情缺失（lack of empathy）方面表现较差。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Transformer的框架配合类别加权损失与逐标签阈值调优，能有效应对多语言多标签极化检测中的标签不平衡问题，整体性能具有竞争力，但在去人化与共情缺失等细微极化表现形式上仍需改进。

Abstract: This paper describes our submission to SemEval-2026 Task 9 on detecting multilingual, multicultural, and multievent online polarization. We address all three subtasks: binary polarization detection, polarization type classification, and manifestation identification for English and Swahili. Our approach leverages transformer-based models (RoBERTa-base for English, AfroXLMR-base for Swahili) with class-weighted loss functions to address severe label imbalance and per-label threshold tuning to optimize multi-label classification. On the test set, we achieve F1 macro scores of 0.7901 (English) and 0.7910 (Swahili) for Subtask 1, 0.4615 (English) and 0.4808 (Swahili) for Subtask 2 and 0.4791 (English) and 0.5830 (Swahili) for Subtask 3, which give competitive performance on the leaderboard, demonstrating the effectiveness of our methods for handling imbalanced multi-label polarization detection. Our error analysis reveals that models struggle with dehumanization detection and lack of empathy.

</details>


### [153] [Training Therapeutic Judges and Multi-Agent Systems for Human-Aligned Mental Health Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30887)
*Mizanur Rahman,Abeer Badawi,Elahe Rahimi,Laleh Seyyed-Kalantari,Frank Rudzicz,Enamul Hoque,Elham Dolatabadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出TheraAlign框架，将心理健康LLM的回复生成重构为基于多维评估的决策-优化问题：先用偏好优化训练TheraJudge评估器（7个心理维度），再用TheraAgent通过Critic/Coach/Therapist三种角色将评估信号转化为定向回复修订，实现"以评估驱动行动"的治疗质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在心理健康支持中的应用存在关键瓶颈：治疗质量只有在评估信号能够作为可操作的控制信号（而非被动指标）时才能真正提升。现有方法多将评估视为静态度量，缺乏将评估结果转化为生成改进的闭环机制。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) TheraJudge：基于偏好优化在人类标注数据上训练的开源治疗评估器，可跨7个心理维度（安全性、相关性、共情等）输出可靠判断；(2) TheraAgent：通过Critic（批判分析）、Coach（策略指导）、Therapist（执行修订）三种协调角色，将评估信号转化为定向响应修订的协同优化流程。

Result: (1) TheraJudge与临床医生评分达到高一致性（ICC=0.87-0.95），在安全性、相关性、共情等关键维度超越监督基线和强闭源评估器；(2) 基于TheraJudge的评估信号，TheraAgent在盲评中将人类评定的治疗质量提升+0.43（5分制），评分者信度达96%；(3) 低质量回复（≤3分）平均提升+2.45分，恢复率达94%，证明了对不安全输出的定向纠正能力。

Conclusion: 心理健康LLM的有效对齐源于"依据人本评估采取行动"，而非单纯依赖更强的生成模型。评估信号必须成为驱动优化的可操作控制机制，这是提升AI心理治疗质量的关键范式。

Abstract: Large language models show promise for mental health support, yet therapeutic quality improves only when evaluation functions as an actionable control signal rather than a passive metric. We introduce a framework that formulates therapeutic response generation as a decision-refinement problem driven by multi-dimensional, human-aligned evaluation. In Stage I, we introduce TheraJudge, an open-source therapeutic evaluator trained via preference-based optimization on human-annotated data to produce reliable judgments across 7 psychological dimensions. In Stage II, we introduce TheraAgent, which operationalizes TheraJudge's evaluations through a coordinated refinement process with specialized Critic, Coach, and Therapist roles that translate evaluative signals into targeted response revisions. Empirically, TheraJudge achieves strong agreement with clinician ratings, with intraclass correlation coefficients (ICC = 0.87-0.95), surpassing supervised baselines and strong closed-source judges, particularly on critical dimensions such as Safety, Relevance, and Empathy. Acting on these evaluations, TheraAgent yields a +0.43 improvement in human-rated therapeutic quality (on a 5-point scale) under blind evaluation, with 96\% clinician inter-rater reliability. Low-quality responses ($\leq 3$) improve by +2.45 points with a 94\% recovery rate, demonstrating targeted correction of unsafe outputs. Overall, our results indicate that effective alignment of mental-health LLMs stems from acting on human-aligned evaluation, rather than relying solely on stronger generation. We release code at https://github.com/vis-nlp/TheraAlign.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Clean Text: Evaluating Encoder and Decoder Robustness for Bangla Event Detection in Noisy Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30914)
*Tanvir Ahmed Sijan,S. M Golam Rifat,Nayeemul Islam,Md. Musfique Anwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了孟加拉语新闻事件检测基准，涵盖干净文本、ASR转录和拼写损坏文本，系统比较了编码器模型与解码器大语言模型在不同噪声条件下的鲁棒性，发现解码器LLM对噪声更鲁棒，混合训练可显著缩小架构间的鲁棒性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件检测系统多在干净文本上评估，针对低资源语言（如孟加拉语）在真实世界噪声下的鲁棒性研究严重不足，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 首先构建了泛化的孟加拉语新闻事件本体（40种事件子类型），并创建了包含9,979条标注句子的基准数据集，覆盖干净新闻文本、真实ASR转录和正字法损坏文本三种条件。随后系统评估了微调的编码器模型（BanglaBERT、XLM-R）和指令微调的解码器大语言模型（Llama 3、Gemma 3），并探索了在指令微调中嵌入标注指南、模型扩展以及干净+噪声混合训练等策略。

Result: 编码器模型在干净文本上性能更高，但在噪声下退化显著；解码器LLM对噪声明显更鲁棒，尤其当事件触发词被损坏时。嵌入标注指南可提升噪声文本的性能基线，但对不同噪声条件下的退化程度改善不一致。模型扩展持续提升解码器LLM的鲁棒性，而干净+噪声混合训练作为有效的正则化策略，对编码器架构的收益尤为显著，大幅缩小了鲁棒性差距。

Conclusion: 在低资源语言的噪声事件检测中，解码器LLM架构天然更具鲁棒性优势；混合训练是弥补编码器鲁棒性不足的有效策略，为实际部署提供了架构选择与训练策略的指导。

Abstract: Event detection (ED) systems are typically evaluated on clean, curated text, leaving their robustness to real-world noise largely unexplored, particularly for low-resource languages such as Bangla. We introduce a generalized Bangla news event ontology and a benchmark comprising 9,979 annotated sentences across 40 event subtypes, spanning clean news text, real-world Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) transcripts, and orthographically corrupted text. We systematically evaluate fine-tuned encoder-only models (BanglaBERT and XLM-R) alongside instruction-tuned decoder-only large language models (Llama 3 and Gemma 3). Our results reveal a clear architectural trade-off: encoder models achieve higher performance on clean text but degrade substantially under noise, whereas decoder-only LLMs are markedly more robust, particularly when event triggers are corrupted. We further show that embedding annotation guidelines during instruction tuning establishes a higher performance baseline on noisy text but yields inconsistent reductions in performance degradation across noisy conditions. Finally, model scaling consistently improves the robustness of decoder-only LLMs, while combined training on clean and noisy data serves as an effective regularization strategy that disproportionately benefits encoder architectures, significantly narrowing the robustness gap.

</details>


### [155] [Bridging Scientific Heritage: An Arabic--Russian Parallel Corpus and LLM Benchmark for Sustainable Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30943)
*M. K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个阿拉伯语-俄语科学翻译基准，包含约27,000句对的混合平行语料库，并在此基准上使用LoRA对mT5、NLLB-200和Qwen2.5-7B三个多语言模型进行微调，其中Qwen2.5-7B结合QLoRA取得了最佳翻译性能，显著超越零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俄语和阿拉伯语是全球科学交流的主要语言之一，但语言障碍阻碍了两大研究社群之间的成果交流，影响国际合作和可持续发展相关研究的推进。当前缺乏针对阿拉伯语-俄语科学翻译的专门基准和领域适配模型，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: （1）构建了一个包含约27,000句对的阿拉伯语-俄语混合平行语料库，涵盖科学摘要和通用领域文本（宗教、新闻、对话）；（2）在三个预训练多语言模型（mT5-base 580M、NLLB-200-distilled-1.3B、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）上，使用LoRA（rank 8/16/32/64）进行参数高效微调，其中Qwen2.5-7B采用QLoRA；（3）以零样本和少样本提示（3个示例）作为基线进行对比评估。

Result: Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct + QLoRA（rank 8）取得最佳结果：BLEU 23.15、chrF 43.89、BERTScore 0.906、COMET 0.758，相比零样本基线提升+4.36 BLEU和+0.051 COMET。少样本提示（3示例）未能提升性能，表明领域特定微调是必要的。

Conclusion: 领域特定的微调对于阿拉伯语-俄语科学翻译至关重要，少样本提示不足以弥合领域差距。该工作通过降低科学文本的语言障碍，促进阿拉伯语和俄语研究者之间的知识交流，有助于可持续伙伴关系（UN SDG 17）和创新基础设施（SDG 9）建设。

Abstract: Russian and Arabic are among the major languages of scientific communication. Language barriers impede the exchange of research results between these communities, which affects international collaboration and the progress of sustainability-related research. We present a benchmark for Arabic--Russian scientific translation. The benchmark includes a hybrid parallel corpus of about 27,000 sentence pairs, compiled from scientific abstracts and general-domain texts (religion, news, conversations). We fine-tune three multilingual language models -- mT5-base (580M parameters), NLLB-200-distilled-1.3B (1.3B), and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (7B) -- using LoRA with ranks 8, 16, 32, and 64. The Qwen2.5-7B model with QLoRA (rank 8) yields BLEU 23.15, chrF 43.89, BERTScore 0.906, and COMET 0.758. These are +4.36 BLEU and +0.051 COMET above the zero-shot baseline. Few-shot prompting with three examples does not improve performance, indicating that domain-specific fine-tuning is required. We release the models, the corpus, and the evaluation code. By lowering the language barrier for scientific texts, the work enables knowledge exchange between Arabic-speaking and Russian-speaking researchers. It contributes to sustainable partnerships (UN SDG 17) and innovation infrastructure (SDG 9), aligning with the conference's focus on technology-driven sustainable development.

</details>


### [156] [Linguistic Distancing on Social Media: Indicators of Emotion Regulation Across Age Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30957)
*Daniela Teodorescu,Saif M. Mohammad,Alona Fyshe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分析大规模社交媒体文本，研究语言距离化（linguistic distancing）如何随年龄增长而变化，发现语言距离化随年龄增长比例上升，这与心理学中老年人情绪调节能力更强的结论一致，为以语言距离化作为幸福感标志及健康干预提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明心理距离化是一种情绪调节策略，且在语言中会留下可量化的标记（语言距离化）。然而，语言距离化在整个生命周期中如何变化尚不清楚。理解这一变化规律有助于阐明情绪调节与年龄的关系，并为社交媒体数据上的幸福感评估提供基准。

Method: 作者在先前语言距离化度量指标的基础上进行改进和扩展，系统性地分析大规模社交媒体文本数据，通过比较不同年龄群体的语言距离化程度，探究其随年龄变化的规律。

Result: 研究提供了额外证据，进一步强化了"语言距离化随年龄增长而比例上升"的假设，即年长者表现出更高程度的语言距离化。

Conclusion: 语言距离化随年龄增长而增强，这一发现与心理学中关于老年人主观幸福感更高的既有研究一致。语言距离化可作为幸福感的有效标记，并能为制定有效的健康干预措施提供信息。该研究为利用文本数据进一步探索情绪调节奠定了基础。

Abstract: Managing our emotional responses to events is key to emotional well-being, a process referred to as emotion regulation in psychology. Previous work has established that the degree to which we distance events is a type of emotion regulation. When we psychologically distance from events there can be markers in our language. These markers have been referred to as linguistic distancing. We build upon a previous metric to operationalize linguistic distancing, and explore how it changes across the lifespan. We explore this systematically by analyzing large amounts of social media text, a venue where people express their emotions. By investigating how distancing varies across age groups we can better understand how emotion regulation varies with age and provide initial benchmarks on social media data. We provide additional evidence further strengthening the hypothesis that linguistic distancing occurs in proportionally more instances with age. These findings align with past work in psychology which indicate improved well-being with older age. Better understanding how linguistic distancing changes with age is important because it functions as a marker of well-being and can inform effective health interventions. We provide a foundation for further exploring emotion regulation through linguistic distancing in text data.

</details>


### [157] [From Propositional to Perceptual Asymmetry: Extending Frictive Policy Optimization to Asymmetric Partial Information Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30973)
*Yifan Zhu,Kyeongmin Rim,James Pustejovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了摩擦策略优化(FPO)框架，将其从命题不对称扩展至感知不对称情境。通过对HCRC MapTask等语料的跨语料分析和LLM探测，发现FPO的摩擦泛函仅在参与者各自信息视界内有效；不同地标配置产生质上不同的共识失败模式，且模糊配置通过"表面成功但悄然偏离"的轨迹驱动了不成比例的误解。LLM探测表明，拥有"正确视角"比全知视角更重要。作者提出了两种标注改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPO框架仅处理命题不对称（同一场景下不同命题解读），但真实对话中普遍存在感知不对称：参与者持有不对称的部分信息，同一指称表达式因参照不同信息状态而产生不同外延。这种感知不对称下的摩擦机制尚未被系统建模和验证。

Method: (1) 将FPO框架从命题不对称形式化扩展至感知不对称情境；(2) 在HCRC MapTask等指称不对称对话任务上进行跨语料分析；(3) 使用LLM探测实验，比较"知情单一视角"与"全知双视角"条件在对话理解中的表现。

Result: (1) FPO摩擦泛函仅在参与者各自信息视界内评估时才具有经验有效性；(2) 不同地标配置产生质上不同的共识失败模式，少量模糊配置驱动了大部分误解，这些误解沿"表面成功但悄然偏离"的轨迹展开；(3) LLM探测确认"正确视角"优于"全知视角"：知情单一视角的表现超过同时访问双方上下文的配置。

Conclusion: 感知不对称是协作对话中摩擦的关键维度，建模时应重视参与者各自的"信息视界"而非追求全知。作者提出两项标注改进：待定共识状态的子类型分解，以及适应感知不对称的对齐分类方案。

Abstract: Frictive Policy Optimization (FPO; Pustejovsky et al., 2025) treats friction in collaborative dialogue -- misalignment, misunderstanding, repair -- as an epistemic signal essential to common-ground construction, rather than noise to be minimized. However, FPO and its implementations assume shared perceptual contexts, where friction arises from differently interpreted propositions over the same scene, which we define as propositional asymmetry. We extend FPO to perceptual asymmetry, where participants hold asymmetric partial information and the same referring expression yields different denotations depending on whose information state grounds the reference. We evaluate this through cross-corpora analysis and LLM probing on referentially asymmetric dialogue tasks, primarily the HCRC MapTask (Anderson et al., 1991). We find that FPO's friction functional is empirically valid only when evaluated from within each participant's information horizon: different landmark configurations produce qualitatively distinct grounding failure modes, with a small class of ambiguous configurations driving a disproportionate share of misunderstandings through trajectories that appear successful but silently diverge. The LLM probe confirms that having the "right perspective" matters more than having all perspectives: the informed single viewpoint outperforms omniscient access to both participants' contexts. We propose two annotation refinements: subtype decomposition of pending grounding states and accommodation-aware alignment classification.

</details>


### [158] [Measuring Judgment Quality in Natural-Language Explanations: Evidence from Forecasting Tournaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30987)
*Christopher W. Karvetski,Sheldon S. Huang,Simas Kučinskas,Nadja Flechner,Jingyu Hu,Philip Tetlock,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出解释质量标记（EQMs），一套由理论引导、LLM评分的60种推理模式，用于从书面解释中提取判断质量信号。在超过55,000对预测-理由的大规模预测锦标赛数据中，EQMs在预测层面和预测者层面均能预测准确性，且系统性优于传统文本分析方法。超过90%的模式-准确性相关性符合理论假设，信号呈不对称性：EQMs识别低表现者比区分顶尖预测者更可靠。作为预测技能指标，EQMs在预测层面最强，在预测者层面有竞争力但弱于历史准确性。人工评分与准确性的相关性较弱且过度依赖理由长度。结果在独立研究中得到复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者依赖专家判断及其书面解释，但解释质量难以大规模测量。预测锦标赛提供了天然的测试场景，但缺乏可扩展、可解释的方法来从书面理由中提取判断质量信号。传统文本分析方法（如LIWC）效果有限。

Method: 研究者提出解释质量标记（EQMs）——60种由理论引导的推理模式，利用大型语言模型（LLMs）对预测理由进行自动评分。在包含55,000+预测-理由对的多年度预测锦标赛数据中进行预注册分析，检验EQMs在预测层面和预测者层面与准确性的相关性。将EQMs与LIWC等传统文本分析方法、人工质量评分、历史准确性等进行基准对比，并在独立预测研究中验证迁移性。

Result: （1）EQMs在预测和预测者层面均能预测准确性，系统性优于LIWC等传统方法；（2）超过90%的统计显著模式-准确性相关性符合方向性假设；（3）信号不对称：EQMs识别可能低表现者的可靠性高于区分最顶尖预测者；（4）EQMs是预测层面最强的预测指标，在预测者层面有竞争力但弱于历史准确性；（5）人工理由质量评分与准确性的相关性较弱且过度依赖理由长度；（6）结果在独立预测研究中成功迁移。

Conclusion: EQMs提供了一种可扩展、可解释的方法，用于从书面解释中提取与判断质量相关的信息。其不对称性暗示该方法更适合筛选低质量判断而非识别顶尖人才，在情报分析、专家评估等实际决策场景中有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Decision-makers routinely rely on expert judgments accompanied by written explanations, yet explanation quality is difficult to measure at scale. Forecasting tournaments offer a natural testing ground: probabilistic judgments are paired with natural-language rationales and scored against realized outcomes. We introduce Explanation Quality Markers (EQMs), a set of sixty theory-guided reasoning patterns scored by large language models (LLMs). In a pre-registered analysis of over 55,000 forecast-rationale pairs from a multiyear forecasting tournament, EQMs predict accuracy at both the forecast and forecaster levels, consistently outperforming pre-LLM text-analysis methods. More than 90% of statistically significant pattern-level EQM-accuracy correlations match our directional hypotheses. The signal is asymmetric: EQMs identify likely underperformers more reliably than they distinguish the very best forecasters. Benchmarked against traditional indicators of forecasting skill, EQMs are the strongest predictor at the forecast level and competitive at the forecaster level, though weaker than prior accuracy. Human ratings of rationale quality are less consistently correlated with accuracy and place disproportionate weight on rationale length. Results transfer to an independent forecasting study. EQMs provide a scalable, interpretable method for extracting judgment-relevant information from written explanations.

</details>


### [159] [Wait, am I Being Fair? Characterizing Deductive Stereotyping and Mitigating It with Fair-GCG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30989)
*Naihao Deng,Yilun Zhu,Joan Nwatu,Clayton Scott,Rada Mihalcea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别了LLM中的"演绎刻板印象"失败模式，即模型将群体统计规律错误应用于个体导致偏见推理；作者提出推理时注入框架Fair-GCG，通过系统发现有效注入短语来引导公平感知推理，在多个公平性基准上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管推理过程能普遍提升LLM的公平性，偏见推理的失败案例仍然存在。作者旨在识别一种特定的失败模式——"演绎刻板印象"（deductive stereotyping），即模型将人口统计层面的统计规律不合逻辑地应用于个体，产生逻辑连贯但社会偏见的推理，并寻求在推理阶段有效引导模型走向公平的解决方案。

Method: 作者首先对"演绎刻板印象"现象进行了统计学解释。在此基础上，提出了一个推理时注入（reasoning-time injection）框架，通过在推理过程中注入特定短语来引导模型进行公平感知推理。进一步提出了Fair-GCG方法，用于系统性地发现有效的注入短语。

Result: Fair-GCG发现的注入短语在多个公平性基准上提升了性能，能够从小型LLM泛化到更大型的LLM，改善了推理层面的公平性，减少了开放式生成中的偏见，并能迁移到真实世界的公平敏感任务中。

Conclusion: 演绎刻板印象是LLM推理中一种重要的公平性失败模式，通过Fair-GCG框架系统发现的推理时注入短语可以有效缓解这一问题，且具有良好的泛化性和迁移能力。

Abstract: Warning: This paper contains several toxic and offensive statements. While reasoning generally improves fairness in recent large language models (LLMs), failures persist. In this work, we identify a failure mode, deductive stereotyping, in which models apply population-level statistical regularities to individual cases, producing logically coherent yet socially biased inferences. We provide a statistical interpretation of this phenomenon. To steer models toward fairness-aware reasoning, we propose a reasoning-time injection framework. We further introduce Fair-GCG to systematically discover effective injection phrases. Injection phrases discovered by Fair-GCG improve performance across multiple fairness benchmarks, generalize from smaller to larger LLMs, improves reasoning-level fairness, reduces bias in open-ended generation, and transfer to real-world fairness-sensitive tasks.

</details>


### [160] [CORTEX: Token-Level Hallucination Detection in RAG via Comparative Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31033)
*Kazuaki Furumai,Shuichiro Haruta,Kazunori Matsumoto,Daisuke Kamisaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CORTEX 是一种用于检索增强生成（RAG）的令牌级幻觉检测方法，通过对比大语言模型在有/无检索文档条件下的内部表征，并利用前序令牌的信息传播和后处理平滑，实现对长文本输出中幻觉的细粒度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长文本RAG输出中，幻觉往往出现在局部片段而非整篇响应，现有方法缺乏对幻觉的细粒度定位能力。作者的核心直觉是：基于检索文档的令牌应比幻觉令牌更强烈地受到文档影响，因此可通过对比LLM在有/无文档条件下的内部表征来检测幻觉。

Method: CORTEX 包含三个关键组件：(1) 对比LLM在有检索文档和无检索文档两种条件下每个令牌的内部表征，衡量文档诱导效应；(2) 利用前序令牌中已吸收的文档信息传播，减少对依赖上下文已包含证据的令牌的误判；(3) 后处理平滑步骤，建模幻觉标签在连续片段上的持续性，降低局部噪声并鼓励片段一致的预测。

Result: 在两个RAG基准数据集和三个大语言模型上的实验表明，CORTEX 显著提升了令牌级幻觉检测性能，且每个组件均对性能提升有持续贡献。

Conclusion: CORTEX 通过对比表征、信息传播建模和标签平滑三个互补机制，在令牌级别实现了高效且精确的RAG幻觉检测，为长文本生成的事实一致性验证提供了有效的细粒度解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose CORTEX, a token-level hallucination detection method for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). In long-form RAG outputs, hallucinations often arise in localized spans rather than throughout an entire response. CORTEX therefore identifies ungrounded content at the token level, enabling fine-grained localization of hallucinations. The key intuition behind CORTEX is that tokens grounded in retrieved documents should be more strongly influenced by those documents than hallucinated tokens. To capture this document-induced effect, CORTEX compares internal representations of a large language model (LLM) under two conditions: with and without the retrieved documents. Instead of relying solely on each token's immediate sensitivity to the retrieved documents, CORTEX also leverages the propagation of document-grounded information through preceding tokens, reducing false positives for tokens whose evidence has already been absorbed into the context. Finally, CORTEX applies post-processing smoothing step that models the tendency of hallucination labels to persist over contiguous spans, reducing local noise and encouraging span-consistent predictions. Experiments on two RAG benchmarks and three LLMs show that CORTEX substantially improves token-level hallucination detection, with each component consistently contributing to performance gains.

</details>


### [161] [Truth or Sophistry? LoFa: A Benchmark for LLM Robustness Against Logical Fallacies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31039)
*Xudong Shen,Li Yuan,Ye Chen,Xin Wu,Yi Cai,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LoFa基准，通过多智能体流水线构建事实问答与谬误论证配对，并引入LFR@k指标，系统评估大语言模型对逻辑谬误攻击的鲁棒性。实验揭示了不同模型在各类谬误类型上的差异化脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM识别或分类逻辑谬误的能力，但对其在谬误性劝说下的鲁棒性研究严重不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统评估LLM在面对逻辑谬误攻击时是否会改变正确判断。

Method: (1) 构建LoFa基准：采用多智能体流水线，将事实性问题与谬误论证配对；(2) 设计多轮辩论框架，模拟持续的对抗性劝说场景；(3) 提出LFR@k（Logical Fallacy Resistance at k）指标，在k轮辩论后量化模型对谬误攻击的抵抗能力，并将谬误鲁棒性与模型固有知识局限解耦。

Result: 实验表明，LLM在不同谬误类型上表现出差异化的鲁棒性水平，各模型呈现出独特的脆弱性画像。

Conclusion: LLM对逻辑谬误的鲁棒性存在显著差异且高度依赖谬误类型，需针对性提升模型的抗谬误推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic capabilities, yet their resilience to manipulative linguistic patterns such as logical fallacies remains underexplored. Prior work has primarily examined whether LLMs can identify or classify fallacies, leaving their robustness against fallacious persuasion insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce LoFa (Logical Fallacy), a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating LLM robustness against fallacies. LoFa is constructed through a multi-agent pipeline that pairs factual questions with fallacious arguments, and is accompanied by a multi-round debate framework for assessing model resilience under sustained adversarial persuasion. To disentangle fallacy robustness from a model's inherent knowledge limitations, we further propose Logical Fallacy Resistance at k (LFR@k), a metric that quantifies resistance to fallacious attacks. Experiments show that LLMs exhibit varying levels of robustness across different fallacy types, revealing distinct vulnerability profiles among models.

</details>


### [162] [A Semantic-Layer-Mediated Agent for Natural Language to SQL over Heterogeneous Enterprise Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31041)
*Ha Jeong Kim,Saksonita Khoeurn,Ye Ji Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语义层中介的自然语言到SQL（NL2SQL）智能体系统。该系统通过一个称为语义模型查询（SMQ）的紧凑中间表示，在精心构建的语义层上进行推理，而非直接在原始数据库模式上生成SQL。SMQ经确定性编译器转换为特定方言的SQL，智能体将其组合成最终查询。系统采用受约束的思考-行动循环，支持SQLite、BigQuery和Snowflake后端。在Spider2-snow基准测试（547个任务）上，使用Gemini 3 Pro达到了94.15%的执行准确率，排名官方排行榜第三，显著优于仅使用模式的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实企业数据库中的NL2SQL比学术基准更具挑战性，因为企业模式通常包含数百张物理表、晦涩的列名、异构SQL方言，以及需要嵌套聚合、时序推理和多表连接的复杂分析工作负载。现有直接基于原始模式生成SQL的方法难以应对这些复杂性。

Method: 提出语义层中介的NL2SQL智能体架构：（1）构建一个精心整理的语义层，解耦语义意图与物理SQL执行；（2）设计语义模型查询（SMQ）作为紧凑的中间表示；（3）智能体在语义层上推理生成SMQ，而非直接生成SQL；（4）确定性编译器将SMQ转换为特定方言的SQL，提供可验证的构建块；（5）智能体将这些构建块组合成最终查询；（6）采用受约束的思考-行动循环（constrained think-act loop）；（7）支持SQLite、BigQuery、Snowflake三种后端，并集成端到端评估框架。

Result: 使用Gemini 3 Pro，该系统在Spider2-snow基准测试的547个任务上达到94.15%的执行准确率，在官方排行榜上排名第三，大幅优于仅使用模式（schema-only）的方法。

Conclusion: 语义层中介的NL2SQL方法通过解耦语义意图与物理SQL执行，显著提升了企业级NL2SQL的性能。同时讨论了语义层质量以及改进的接地性（grounding）与过拟合之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Natural language-to-SQL (NL2SQL) over real-world enterprise databases remains significantly more challenging than on academic benchmarks. Enterprise schemas often contain hundreds of physical tables with cryptic column names, heterogeneous SQL dialects, and complex analytical workloads requiring nested aggregations, temporal reasoning, and multi-table joins. We present a semantic-layer-mediated NL2SQL agent that decouples semantic intent from physical SQL execution. Rather than generating SQL directly over raw schemas, the agent reasons over a curated semantic layer through a compact intermediate representation called the Semantic Model Query (SMQ). A deterministic compiler translates each SMQ into dialect-specific SQL, providing verified building blocks that the agent composes into the final query. The system employs a constrained think-act loop, supports SQLite, BigQuery, and Snowflake backends, and is integrated into an end-to-end evaluation framework. Using Gemini 3 Pro, the system achieves 94.15% execution accuracy on the 547-task Spider2-snow benchmark, ranking third on the official leaderboard and substantially outperforming schema-only approaches. We describe the system architecture, SMQ representation, agent workflow, evaluation results, and discuss semantic-layer quality and the trade-off between improved grounding and overfitting.

</details>


### [163] [Reference-Based Prosody and Rhythm Evaluation for Spoken Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31055)
*Ashish Hallur,Thomas Thebaud,Georgi Tinchev,Venkatesh Ravichandran,Laureano Moro-Velazquez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对语音到语音AI智能体评估中缺乏可解释的语音原生韵律与节奏指标的现状，本文提出一种基于百分位数的评估协议：利用4000+小时对话数据构建匹配参考层，通过将S2S输出波形与最接近的人类参考层对比，报告百分位偏差或超出5-95分位范围的标记，从而提供行为合理性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前S2S评估缺乏可解释的、语音原生的对话韵律与节奏度量。由于基频(F0)、语速、发音速率和停顿会随模型预测的说话人特质和交互状态而变化，汇总的人类统计数据在评估特定输出时校准效果差，需要构建更精细的匹配参考体系。

Method: 基于Seamless Interaction数据集中4000+小时的英语双向对话，为F0均值、F0表现力、语速、发音速率、停顿比例和平均停顿时长构建匹配参考层(strata)。定义基于百分位数的评估协议：从S2S输出波形中提取相同指标，将其与最接近的匹配人类参考层进行比较，报告百分位偏差或标记超出第5-95百分位范围的样本。

Result: 在留出的人类数据上，汇总参考会过度标记状态条件化的F0表现力和节奏，而匹配参考的标记率更接近名义上的10%，且使偏差方向具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法作为行为合理性检查，是对感知评估和以用户为中心的评估的补充而非替代，为S2S系统的韵律与节奏评估提供了可解释的语音原生方案。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech (S2S) AI agents are advancing rapidly, yet evaluation lacks interpretable speech-native measures for conversational prosody and rhythm. Because $F_0$, speaking rate, articulation rate, and pausing shift with model-predicted speaker traits and interaction state, pooled human statistics can be poorly calibrated for evaluating a particular output. Using 4000+ hours of dyadic English conversation from the Seamless Interaction dataset, we construct matched reference regimes for $F_0$ mean, $F_0$ expressivity, speech rate, articulation rate, pause ratio, and mean pause duration. We then define a percentile-based evaluation protocol: extract the same metrics from an S2S output waveform, compare them to the closest matched human reference stratum, and report percentile deviations or 5th-95th percentile out-of-regime flags. On held-out human rows, pooled references over-flag state-conditioned $F_0$ expressivity and rhythm, while matched references return flag rates closer to the nominal 10% and make deviation direction interpretable. These outputs serve as behavioral plausibility checks that complement, rather than replace, perceptual and user-centered evaluation.

</details>


### [164] [Exploring the relationship between team institutional composition and novelty in academic papers based on fine-grained knowledge entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31058)
*Ziling Chen,Chengzhi Zhang,Heng Zhang,Yi Zhao,Chen Yang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以自然语言处理领域为例，将作者团队按机构构成分为纯学术、纯工业和学术-工业混合三类，从全文提取方法、数据集、工具和指标四类细粒度知识实体，基于实体组合衡量论文新颖性，揭示了混合团队更关注方法-指标新颖性、工业团队更关注方法-工具新颖性的差异化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对机构构成在论文新颖性中的作用关注不足，且新颖性测度多停留在宏观层面，难以解释新颖性的具体来源和类型。

Method: 以NLP领域为对象，将作者团队分为学术机构、工业机构、学术-工业混合三类；从全文提取方法、数据集、工具、指标四类细粒度知识实体；基于实体组合测度论文新颖性，并分析不同实体类型配对对新颖论文的贡献。

Result: 学术-工业混合合作比纯工业合作更易产出新颖论文；混合团队更注重方法-指标组合的新颖性，工业团队则更注重方法-工具组合的新颖性。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度新颖性测度揭示了机构团队构成与论文新颖性之间的关系，为提升论文质量和促进产学研合作提供了有益证据。

Abstract: The composition of author teams is an important factor influencing the novelty of academic papers. However, existing studies have paid limited attention to the role of institutional composition, and most novelty measures remain at a general level, making it difficult to explain the specific sources and types of novelty in papers. Taking the field of natural language processing as an example, this study investigates the relationship between team institutional composition and the fine-grained novelty of academic papers. Author teams are classified into three types: academic institutions, industrial institutions, and mixed academic and industrial institutions. Four types of fine-grained knowledge entities are extracted from full-text papers, including methods, datasets, tools, and metrics. The novelty of papers is then measured based on entity combinations, and pairwise combinations of different entity types are further analyzed to examine their contributions to novel papers. The results show that, in the field of natural language processing, collaboration between industrial and academic institutions is more likely to produce novel papers than purely industrial collaboration. From the perspective of fine-grained knowledge entities, mixed academic and industrial teams pay more attention to the novelty of method-metric combinations, whereas industrial teams pay more attention to the novelty of method-tool combinations. This study reveals the relationship between institutional team composition and paper novelty through fine-grained novelty measurement, providing useful evidence for improving paper quality and promoting industry-academia-research collaboration.

</details>


### [165] [Building a Multimodal Dataset of Academic Paper for Keyword Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31069)
*Jingyu Zhang,Xinyi Yan,Yi Xiang,Yingyi Zhang,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含文本、图像、音频的多模态学术论文关键词提取数据集（1000样本），对比了单模态与多模态信息在该任务上的表现差异，实验表明融合图文音三模态文本可有效提升关键词提取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关键词提取任务仅依赖文本数据，忽视了图像和音频模态中的视觉与听觉信息，导致信息丰富度不足、潜在关联未被挖掘，限制了模型的表征学习能力与预测精度；同时多模态关键词提取数据集极度稀缺，制约了该方向的研究进展。

Method: 1）构建包含论文文本、图像、音频及关键词的1000条多模态学术论文数据集；2）基于无监督和有监督关键词提取方法，分别使用论文纯文本、从图像中提取的文本以及从音频中提取的文本进行实验；3）对比不同模态信息及多模态信息融合在关键词提取任务上的性能差异。

Result: 不同模态的文本在模型中表现出差异化特征；将论文文本、图像文本和音频文本进行拼接融合，能够有效提升学术论文关键词提取的性能。

Conclusion: 多模态信息融合对关键词提取任务具有显著增益，图像和音频模态所含的文本信息可作为文本模态的有效补充，为多模态关键词提取研究提供了数据基础与方法参考。

Abstract: Up to this point, keyword extraction task typically relies solely on textual data. Neglecting visual details and audio features from image and audio modalities leads to deficiencies in information richness and overlooks potential correlations, thereby constraining the model's ability to learn representations of the data and the accuracy of model predictions. Furthermore, the currently available multimodal datasets for keyword extraction task are particularly scarce, further hindering the progress of research on multimodal keyword extraction task. Therefore, this study constructs a multimodal dataset of academic paper consisting of 1000 samples, with each sample containing paper text, images, audios and keywords. Based on unsupervised and supervised methods of keyword extraction, experiments are conducted using textual data from papers, as well as text extracted from images and audio. The aim is to investigate the differences in performance in keyword extraction task with respect to different modal information and the fusion of multimodal information. The experimental results indicate that text from different modalities exhibits distinct characteristics in the model. The concatenation of paper text, image text and audio text can effectively enhance the keyword extraction performance of academic papers.

</details>


### [166] [Triospect: A Three-Dimensional Framework for Robust Statistical AI-Generated Text Detection Against Diverse Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31074)
*Guangsheng Bao,Lihua Rong,Yanbin Zhao,Xiao Yu,Qiji Zhou,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Triospect检测框架，通过结合内容（核心思想）和表达（风格元素）两个额外视角来增强AI生成文本检测的鲁棒性，在多个基准测试中显著优于基线方法，能有效抵御各种对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成文本检测器在面对通过操纵文本特征来逃避检测的攻击时表现脆弱，缺乏足够的鲁棒性。因此需要一种能够从多维度审视文本的检测方法，以提升检测可靠性。

Method: 提出Triospect检测框架，在传统文本特征分析基础上，额外引入内容视角（捕捉文本的核心思想）和表达视角（捕捉风格元素），从三个维度联合对给定文本进行检测判别。这是一种基于统计方法的创新框架。

Result: 在涵盖17种攻击、12个领域和17个源模型的两个基准测试上，Triospect展现出强鲁棒性。在Humanize-16K攻击后子集上，AUROC提升22.3%，TPR01提升13%；在对抗性RAID数据集上，AUROC提升9.1%，TPR01提升22%。

Conclusion: Triospect通过在内容和表达层面进行多视角分析，显著增强了AI生成文本检测的鲁棒性，是统计方法中提升检测抗攻击能力的开创性工作。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Existing AI-generated text detectors are vulnerable to attacks that manipulate textual characteristics. In this study, we propose a novel Triospect Detection Framework by using additional perspectives of content (core ideas) and expression (stylistic elements) within a given text. Experiments on two benchmarks involving 17 attacks, 12 domains, and 17 source models demonstrate that Triospect is robust against these attacks. It improves the strong baseline by a significant margin of 22.3% (AUROC) and 13% (TPR01) on the Humanize-16K after-attack subset, and by 9.1% (AUROC) and 22% (TPR01) on the adversarial RAID. This framework marks a pioneering effort in statistical methods to enhance detection reliability against attacks. We release our data and code at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/triospect.

</details>


### [167] [When Reranking Hurts: Uncertainty-Based Gating for Few-Shot Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31087)
*Orian Dabod,Amir Cohen,Gabriel Stanovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"重排序检索示例总能提升少样本性能"的传统观点，提出了免训练门控重排序方法，基于模型不确定性决定是否重排序，在降低15%-80%计算成本的同时提升最高2%的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本选择方法普遍默认对检索到的示例进行重排序总能提升性能，但作者发现这一昂贵步骤实际上可能反而降低性能，因此需要一种智能机制来判断何时应跳过重排序。

Method: 提出免训练门控重排序（Training-Free Gated Reranking）方法：利用语言模型对当前实例的不确定性估计作为门控信号，仅在高不确定性实例上触发重排序，低不确定性实例则直接使用初始检索结果，从而避免不必要的计算开销。

Result: 在8个大语言模型、7个自然语言理解数据集和9个机器翻译领域-语言组合上的实验表明，该方法减少15%-80%的计算成本，同时平均性能提升最高达2%，证明更高计算成本并不保证更好性能。

Conclusion: 重排序并非总是有益的，应针对高不确定性实例进行定向重排序。免训练门控机制以极低成本实现了对重排序的智能调度，在降低计算开销的同时提升了整体性能。

Abstract: Few-shot selection typically assumes that reranking retrieved examples always improves performance. We challenge this view by identifying that the expensive reranking step can in fact degrade performance. Instead, we propose \emph{Training-Free Gated Reranking}, which decides whether to rerank the few-shot examples based on the model's uncertainty. Extensive experiments across 8 LLMs, covering 7 NLU datasets and 9 MT domain-language combinations, demonstrate that our approach reduces computational costs by 15\%-80\% while improving average performance by up to 2\%. These findings indicate that higher computational cost does not guarantee better performance, and that reranking is most beneficial when targeted at high-uncertainty instances.

</details>


### [168] [What Counts as an Error? Dual-Reference Benchmarking for Atypical ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31112)
*Hawau Olamide Toyin,Srinivasan Umesh,Hanan Aldarmaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了非典型语音（以口吃为例）ASR评估中长期被忽视的问题：逐字转录和意图转录两种参考文本的混用导致模型性能排名差异显著，呼吁根据用例选择合适的转录参考以进行有效的模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统在非典型语音上表现不佳是已知问题，但评估中常混淆两种有效的转录参考——逐字转录（包含重复、延长等不流畅现象）和意图转录（规范化的去不流畅文本）。这种二元性在现有评估中被混为一谈，奖励删除不流畅现象的系统，忽视了逐字忠实度。作者旨在量化这种参考文本差异对模型评估和排名的影响。

Method: 以口吃语音为案例研究，选取11个ASR模型（涵盖编码器-解码器、CTC和Transducer三大架构家族），分别使用逐字转录和意图转录两种参考文本进行基准测试。通过对比两种转录风格下的模型性能指标和排名变化，进行定量评估。

Result: 定量评估表明，使用两种转录参考风格时，模型性能和排名存在显著差异。同一模型可能在不同参考文本下得到截然不同的评估结论，排名顺序发生明显变化。

Conclusion: 在非典型语音ASR中，选择合适的转录参考对有效的模型选择至关重要，应根据具体用例来决定采用逐字转录还是意图转录作为评估标准，而非将两者混为一谈。

Abstract: ASR systems have been often reported to underperform on atypical speech. An often conflated compounding factor is the existence of two valid transcription references: verbatim (actual produced speech, including repetitions/prolongations) and intended (the canonical form of the text with disfluencies removed) in atypical speech recognition depending on context and use-case. Most ASR evaluations conflate this duality into a single ground truth and reward systems that delete disfluencies, ignoring verbatim faithfulness. We benchmark 11 ASR models from encoder-decoder, CTC and transducer families using both verbatim and intended references on atypical stuttered speech as a case study. Our quantitative assessment underlines the disparity in model performance and rankings using the two transcript styles. Through this analysis, we highlight the importance of selecting a suitable transcription reference for valid model selection depending on the use-case, particularly for atypical ASR.

</details>


### [169] [SeKV: Resolution-Adaptive KV Cache with Hierarchical Semantic Memory for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31145)
*Amirhossein Abaskohi,Giuseppe Carenini,Peter West,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SeKV 提出一种分辨率自适应的语义KV缓存方法，将长上下文按熵引导的语义跨度组织起来，利用GPU-CPU混合存储层级：GPU上保留轻量摘要向量用于粗粒度路由，CPU上存储低秩SVD基用于按需的token级重建。通过训练的"放大"机制在解码时选择性扩展与查询相关的跨度，从而在保持模型冻结的前提下，以极少可训练参数实现精确检索与显著内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文场景中KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，成为显存瓶颈。现有方法中，token驱逐丢失信息，语义分组在预填充阶段固定压缩决策，均无法在生成过程中从压缩跨度中恢复token级细节。

Method: 1) 将上下文按熵引导的语义跨度分割；2) 每个跨度在GPU上保留轻量摘要向量（用于粗粒度路由），在CPU上保留低秩SVD基（用于按需token级重建）；3) 训练一个"zoom-in"选择机制，在解码时选择性展开查询相关跨度的token级细节；4) 基座LLM完全冻结，仅增加不到0.05%的可训练参数。

Result: 在四个基准上，SeKV 比最强语义压缩基线平均提升5.9%，同时在128K上下文长度下相比完整KV缓存减少53.3%的GPU显存占用。

Conclusion: SeKV 通过分辨率自适应的语义KV缓存设计，在保持上下文保真度的同时实现了显著的GPU内存压缩，为长上下文LLM推理提供了一种高效且无损信息的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate over long contexts, where the KV cache becomes a dominant memory bottleneck: its size grows linearly with sequence length and must be retained throughout decoding, making full GPU caching prohibitively expensive without compression. Existing KV cache compression methods struggle to balance efficiency with faithful context preservation. Token eviction discards information, while semantic grouping fixes compression decisions at prefill time; neither can recover token-level detail from a compressed span once it becomes relevant during generation. As a solution, we propose SeKV, a resolution-adaptive semantic KV cache that organizes context into entropy-guided semantic spans and stores them across a GPU-CPU memory hierarchy without discarding information. Each span keeps a lightweight summary vector on GPU for coarse routing and a low-rank SVD basis on CPU for on-demand token-level reconstruction. A trained zoom-in mechanism selectively expands query-relevant spans during decoding, enabling precise retrieval without materializing the full KV cache on GPU. SeKV enables adaptive token-level reconstruction while keeping the base LLM fully frozen and adding fewer than 0.05% trainable parameters. Across four benchmarks, SeKV improves over the strongest semantic compression baseline by 5.9% on average while reducing GPU memory by 53.3% versus full KV caching at 128K context. Code is available on https://github.com/AmirAbaskohi/SeKV.

</details>


### [170] [TAG-DLM: Diffusion Language Models for Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31166)
*Lingjie Chen,Yuanchen Bei,Haobo Xu,Yanjun Zhao,Yuzhong Chen,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于掩码扩散语言模型的统一框架，通过将图的局部邻域线性化为token序列并利用拓扑注意力掩码注入图结构，实现了文本推理与图消息传递的深度融合，在多个TAG基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本属性图（TAG）方法存在文本与图结构两模态割裂的问题：图神经网络仅依赖浅层文本特征，而LLM与图结合的混合方法仅将语言模型作为文本编码器，结构学习仍需单独的图模块。因此需要一种真正统一文本推理与图消息传递的方法。

Method: 提出一种基于掩码扩散语言模型（masked diffusion language model）的统一方法：（1）对每个图实例，采样局部邻域并将其线性化为token序列；（2）通过拓扑注意力掩码（topology attention mask）注入图结构信息，在注意力计算中实现图上的消息传递；（3）扩散语言模型兼具文本理解与生成能力，仅需修改prompt即可适配节点分类、链接预测及跨数据集迁移等多种任务，无需针对特定目标微调。

Result: 在两个任务、三个TAG基准数据集上，该方法优于图神经网络、图Transformer以及基于LLM的基线方法，相较最强基线最高提升3.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 将图结构信息通过拓扑注意力掩码融入扩散语言模型是一种有效的TAG统一建模范式，在无需任务特定微调的前提下实现了跨任务、跨数据集的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs), where each node carries a natural language description, require models to jointly reason over text and graph topology. Existing approaches often handle the two modalities separately: graph neural networks operate on shallow text features, while hybrids of LLMs and graphs use the language model mainly as a text encoder and delegate structure learning to a separate graph module. We propose method that unifies textual reasoning and graph message passing within a masked diffusion language model, a language model with bidirectional attention and generative decoding. For each graph instance, method linearises a sampled local neighbourhood into a token sequence and injects graph structure through a topology attention mask, which realises message passing over the graph. Because the diffusion language model can both interpret and generate text, the method adapts to different tasks simply by changing the prompt, supporting node classification, link prediction, and cross-dataset transfer with no target-specific fine-tuning.
  Experiments show that method outperforms graph neural networks, graph transformers, and LLM-based baselines on all three TAG benchmarks across two tasks, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 3.9 points.

</details>


### [171] [Gated Multi-Graph Fusion via Graph Attention Networks for Alzheimer's Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31186)
*Jinyu Li,Xiao Wei,Bin Wen,Kai Li,Yuqin Lin,Xiaobao Wang,Longbiao Wang,Jianwu Dang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种多视角门控图注意力网络，通过ASR转录语音后构建语义图、依存图和基于PMI的共现图，以"内容-结构-流"框架刻画自发语音，并通过自适应门控融合应对临床异质性，在ADReSSo数据集上达到90.00%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自发语音的阿尔茨海默病检测系统普遍忽视了语言中的非线性结构破坏以及临床人群的异质性，导致模型在不同症状表现的患者中鲁棒性不足。

Method: 首先通过ASR将语音转录为文本；然后分别构建语义图、依存句法图和基于规范语料库PMI的共现图，形成"内容-结构-流"三视角表征；最后采用自适应门控融合机制动态整合多视角特征，并基于图注意力网络进行分类。

Result: 在ADReSSo数据集上达到90.00%的分类准确率；消融实验证实，基于PMI的共现图和异质性感知的门控机制对跨多样化临床人群的鲁棒分类至关重要。

Conclusion: 所提出的多视角图建模方法能够有效捕捉AD患者自发语音中的非线性语言异常，且自适应门控融合策略显著提升了模型在临床异质场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Spontaneous speech is a vital non-invasive biomarker for Alzheimer's Disease (AD), yet many systems overlook non-linear structural disruptions and clinical heterogeneity in pathological language. We propose a Multi-View Gated Graph Attention Network that transcribes audio via Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) to construct semantic, dependency, and co-occurrence graphs, characterizing speech through a "content-structure-flow" framework. Notably, the co-occurrence graph leverages Pointwise Mutual Information (PMI) from a normative corpus to quantify narrative logic and linguistic deviation. To address symptomatic diversity, an adaptive gated fusion mechanism dynamically integrates these views. Evaluated on the ADReSSo dataset, our model achieves 90.00% accuracy. Ablation results confirm that the PMI-based graph and heterogeneity-aware gating are essential for robust classification across diverse clinical populations. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/opeacc/AD.

</details>


### [172] [Can LLMs Imagine Moral Alternatives Beyond Binary Dilemmas?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31213)
*Jongchan Choi,Nari Yang,Sung Soo Park,Jaemin Cho,Han Seoyoung,Haerin Shin,Jun-Hyung Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 MoralAltDataset，一个包含 307 个道德困境的数据集，每个困境都补充了折中方案和重新框架的替代选项。研究发现，LLM 在面对替代方案时会显著改变道德判断，且 LLM 生成的替代方案在结构性和伦理性标准上常优于人类编写的方案，但在实际可行性上存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于 LLM 处理道德困境的研究忽视了一个人类道德认知的核心方面：想象超越给定选项的替代方案的能力。随着 LLM 越来越多地被部署为道德顾问和智能体，理解它们如何在竞争性价值之间导航、以及是否能生成超越二元对立的创造性解决方案变得至关重要。

Method: 1. 构建 MoralAltDataset 数据集，包含 307 个道德困境，涵盖叙述性顾问困境和 AI 面对的智能体困境，每个困境配有折中方案和重新框架的替代选项（由人类撰写）。2. 在 15 个 LLM 上测试引入替代选项后模型道德判断的变化。3. 使用成对偏好比较和基于专家标准的质量评估，对比 LLM 生成与人类撰写的替代方案。

Result: 1. 引入折中替代方案后，LLM 的道德选择发生显著变化，折中方案常比原始二元选项更受偏好。2. LLM 生成的替代方案在成对比较中常被偏好，并在细粒度的结构性和伦理性标准上表现更优。3. 存在结构性质量与实际可行性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: LLM 不仅能识别道德困境中的替代方案，还能生成高质量的替代选项。这揭示了 LLM 在道德推理中超越二元框架的潜力，但也表明在将 LLM 部署为实际道德决策者之前，需关注结构性质量与可行性之间的张力。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as moral advisors and agents, they need to address dilemmas between two competing values.
