In certain cases, these upper bounds can attain the convergence order of $\mathcal{O}\big( n_3 r_t\big(\max_{k} s_k^2\big) \log\big((n_1+n_2)n_3\big) /n \big)$, and the minimax lower bound analysis is further presented to show that the upper-bounds can approach the lower bound with the gap of order $\mathcal{O}(\max_k s_k^2/max(n_1, n_2))$ up to a logarithmic factor. Finally, multiple groups of experiments on synthetic, image and video tensor data sets are conducted to support our theoretical results and demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [207] [Residual-on-Residual Regression as a Tool for Effect Estimation in Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30976)
*Ashley I. Naimi,Qianhui Jin,Ya-Hui Yu,Sara M. Parisi,Lisa M. Bodnar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种高维混杂调整方法：增广逆概率加权(AIPW)、目标最大似然估计(TMLE)和残差-残差回归。研究发现，当暴露效应近似恒定时，三者结果一致；但在弱正性违背情况下，残差-残差回归表现优于AIPW和TMLE。该方法可解释性强、计算简单，可作为观察性因果推断的三角验证策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行病学家在处理高维混杂时，常用AIPW和TMLE，但二者可能产生不同结果，且在弱正性违背条件下均会变得不稳定。因此需要一种更稳定的替代方法来估计部分线性模型中的暴露效应。

Method: 提出残差-残差回归（residual-on-residual regression）：先分别拟合结局和暴露的混杂调整模型，再用普通最小二乘法(OLS)将结局残差对暴露残差进行回归，以估计部分线性模型中编码的暴露效应。研究使用nuMoM2b队列数据（n=7,923）估计蔬菜摄入密度与子痫前期的关联，并通过模拟实验比较三种方法的偏倚、置信区间覆盖率和在正性违背下的表现。

Result: (1) 实证分析：残差-残差回归、AIPW和TMLE得出一致估计，均表明高蔬菜摄入密度可适度降低子痫前期风险。(2) 模拟实验：当暴露效应近似恒定时，残差-残差回归无偏且置信区间覆盖率接近名义水平，表现与AIPW和TMLE相当，且远优于错误指定的参数模型。(3) 在正性违背条件下，当真实效应编码为部分线性模型时，残差-残差回归优于AIPW和TMLE。

Conclusion: 当暴露效应近似恒定时，残差-残差回归具有可解释性强、计算简单的优势，可作为观察性研究中因果推断的三角验证策略，尤其适用于正性违背条件下的稳健估计。

Abstract: Epidemiologists increasingly use machine learning to adjust for high-dimensional confounding. Augmented inverse probability weighting (AIPW) and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE) are most widely used but may yield different results and both can become unstable under weak positivity violations. Residual-on-residual regression is a stable alternative that estimates an exposure effect encoded in a partially linear model by fitting confounder adjusted models for the outcome and exposure, then regressing outcome residuals against exposure residuals using ordinary least squares. We illustrate the approach using data from the Nulliparous Pregnancy Outcomes Study: Monitoring Mothers-to-Be (nuMoM2b; $n = 7{,}923$), estimating the association between high vegetable intake density and preeclampsia. Residual-on-residual regression, AIPW, and TMLE yielded concordant estimates, indicating a modest reduction in preeclampsia risk. In simulations, residual-on-residual regression was unbiased with near-nominal confidence interval coverage, performing comparably to AIPW and TMLE and substantially better than a misspecified parametric model when the exposure effect is approximately constant. However, in simulation settings with positivity violations, residual on residual regression outperformed AIPW and TMLE when the true effect was coded in a partially linear model. When the exposure effect is approximately constant, residual-on-residual regression is interpretable, computationally simple, and provides a triangulation strategy for observational causal inference.

</details>


### [208] [Universal Inference for model selection on networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30981)
*Eric Yanchenko,Jonathan P. Williams,Ryan Martin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于通用推断（Universal Inference）的通用网络模型选择框架，通过边采样获得两个依赖数据集，构造出e值统计量，首次在有限样本下实现网络假设检验的第一类错误控制，并在模型选择与社区数量确定等任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络数据存在内在依赖性，且通常仅观测到单一网络实现，现有模型选择与假设检验方法多需针对特定模型定制，且仅提供渐近理论保证。亟需一个适用于多种检验场景、具备有限样本理论保证的通用框架。

Method: 采用通用推断框架，利用边采样（edge sampling）将原始网络拆分为两个具有可处理依赖关系的子网络；基于这两个依赖划分构造检验统计量，并证明其为e值（e-value），从而在几乎任意假设检验下实现有限样本的第一类错误控制。

Result: 理论上证明了所提统计量是e值，能在有限样本下控制第一类错误率；同时证明对数检验统计量在各类备择模型下发散至正无穷。在模拟与真实网络实验中，该方法在随机图模型选择和社区数量确定等任务上表现良好。

Conclusion: 该工作首次从依赖数据划分中构造出通用推断型统计量，也是首次为网络假设检验提供有限样本检验保证，为网络数据分析提供了一个兼具普适性与严格理论保障的工具。

Abstract: Model selection and hypothesis testing are important tasks on networks. A key challenge lies in the inherent dependence in network data, as well as the fact that typically only a single realization is observed. As a result, many existing methods must be carefully tailored to specific models and only come with asymptotic theoretical guarantees. In this work, however, we propose a general model selection framework using Universal Inference, making our method widely applicable to various testing scenarios. Since Universal Inference requires two sets of data, we employ edge sampling to obtain proper networks with tractable dependence. We prove that the proposed statistic is an e-value, thus controlling the type I error rate in finite samples under nearly any hypothesis test. To our knowledge, this is the first Universal Inference-type statistic constructed from dependent splits of data as well as the first finite-sample testing guarantee for hypothesis testing on networks. We also prove that the logarithm of the test statistic diverges to positive infinity under various alternative models. On simulated and real-world networks, the proposed method performs well on tasks such as choosing the random graph model and the number of communities.

</details>


### [209] [Semiparametric Efficiency in Sequential Experiments: Characterization and Design via Average Propensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31190)
*Jiachun Li,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文建立了序贯实验的效率基准理论，证明每个非预期性设计会诱导一个平均倾向得分，并以该得分下的i.i.d.效率作为半参数下界；进一步提出两种可实施的批量自适应设计方法——基于有效影响函数的回归调整和自适应协变量平衡——均能以少量策略更新达到基准效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代实验（如AI服务评估、平台干预）常偏离i.i.d.采样，面临自适应分配、协变量平衡、曝光/公平性/预算等约束。缺乏统一的效率基准来刻画和指导此类序贯实验设计。

Method: 理论层面：证明非预期性设计诱导平均倾向得分，建立半参数局部无偏估计的效率下界；方法层面：提出两条路径——(1)基于有效影响函数的回归调整，对一般平滑估计量在标准冗余速率下达到基准，对结果均值的线性泛函达到尖锐二阶速率；(2)自适应协变量平衡通过分配机制本身达到相同基准，支持简单的矩估计。两者均仅需少量策略更新批次。

Result: 数值实验和AI医疗助手评估的实证研究表明，所提方法在多处理设置中均实现显著的实践效率提升，验证了统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文为序贯实验的效率表征与设计提供了统一框架：平均倾向得分作为核心基准连接设计与估计，两种互补的自适应设计策略（回归调整与协变量平衡）均能以可操作的方式接近该基准，兼顾灵活性与易监控性。

Abstract: Modern experiments, including evaluations of AI-enabled services and platform interventions, often depart from independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) sampling because assignments may be adaptive, balanced across covariates, or subject to rollout constraints such as exposure, fairness, and budget limits. This paper studies the efficiency benchmark for estimating causal targets in such sequential experiments. We show that every non-anticipating design induces an average propensity score, and we establish a semiparametric lower bound: for regular locally unbiased estimators, attainable precision is bounded by the i.i.d. efficiency benchmark evaluated at this induced score. The average propensity score thereby serves as a common benchmark and design target, allowing sequential experimental design to be viewed as choosing or learning an efficient allocation rule, with operational constraints entering through the admissible set when present. We then develop implementable batched adaptive designs that approach this benchmark through two complementary mechanisms. The first uses regression adjustment based on efficient influence functions; for general smooth estimands it attains the benchmark under standard nuisance-rate conditions, while for linear functionals of outcome means it achieves a sharp second-order rate. The second uses adaptive covariate balancing to attain the same benchmark through the assignment mechanism, enabling simple moment-based estimation. Both routes require only a small number of policy updates, making them compatible with delayed feedback and easier to monitor in operational deployments. Numerical experiments and an empirical study of AI medical-assistant evaluation demonstrate the practical efficiency gains, including in multi-treatment settings. Overall, the paper provides a unified framework for characterizing and designing efficient sequential experiments.

</details>


### [210] [Scalable Joint Modeling of Dependent Multi-Type Survey Data for Small Area Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31964)
*Zewei Kong,Paul A. Parker,Scott H. Holan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种贝叶斯区域级小域估计框架，通过共享空间随机效应联合建模二项响应和高斯响应，利用Polya-Gamma数据增强和空间基函数降维实现高效Gibbs采样，在ACS数据上相比单变量模型显著降低了后验方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国社区调查（ACS）在小域上直接估计的标准误较大，影响联邦资金分配和政策制定。现有单变量模型未充分利用多结局间的关联信息，需要一种能跨结局和空间邻居借力以提高估计精度的联合建模方法。

Method: 提出Multi-type模型：通过共享空间随机效应联合建模二项（如贫困率）和高斯（如收入中位数）响应变量。二项分量采用Polya-Gamma数据增强获得条件高斯表示，空间分量采用基函数进行降维，两者结合使得所有条件后验均为闭式，实现高效Gibbs采样。

Result: 模拟研究表明，联合模型相比独立单变量模型提高了估计精度。在ACS收入中位数和贫困率数据的实际应用中，Multi-type模型产生相似的点估计，但后验方差更小。

Conclusion: 所提出的Multi-type联合模型有效利用多结局间相关性，在保持点估计准确性的同时显著提升估计精度，为ACS等调查数据的小域估计提供了更优的统计框架。

Abstract: We develop a Bayesian area-level small area estimation framework that jointly models binomial and Gaussian survey responses through shared spatial random effects. This work is motivated by the American Community Survey (ACS), which provides useful information that contributes to federal funding and policy making decisions, and often yields direct estimates with large standard errors in small domains. The proposed Multi-type model borrows strength across outcomes and spatial neighbors to improve the precision of the associated estimates. For the binomial component, Polya-Gamma data augmentation yields a conditionally Gaussian representation, while spatial basis functions provide dimension reduction for high-dimensional spatial data. Together, these features lead to closed-form conditional posteriors and, thus, an efficient Gibbs sampler. Through empirical simulations, we show that the proposed joint model improves estimation precision relative to independent Univariate models. Applying the method to ACS median income and poverty rate data, we find that the proposed Multi-type model yields similar point estimates but smaller posterior variances than the corresponding Univariate models.

</details>


### [211] [Payment Process Estimation in Aggregated Insurance Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31970)
*Martin Bladt,Marcus Christiansen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仅观测到宏观状态和实现赔付的情况下，如何对聚合多状态系统的逗留-赔付模型进行统计推断，建立了基于逆概率加权的状态特定累积赔付过程估计量，并证明了其强相合性和弱收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险赔付往往依赖于潜在的微观状态，但实际中只能观测到宏观状态和已实现的赔付金额。现有方法难以处理这种微观状态不可直接观测的聚合多状态系统，尤其是在存在左截断和右删失的情形下。因此需要发展新的统计方法来估计状态特定的累积赔付过程。

Method: 作者从微观到宏观的投影出发，构建了状态特定累积赔付过程的逆概率加权（IPW）估计量，并在左截断和右删失的复杂观测机制下，推导了估计量的渐近性质。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的逆概率加权估计量具有强相合性（strong consistency），并且满足弱收敛（weak convergence）性质，从而为推断提供了坚实的渐近理论基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为聚合多状态系统下的保险赔付分析提供了一个严谨的统计框架，即使在微观状态不可观测、数据存在截断和删失的情况下，也能有效估计状态特定的累积赔付过程。

Abstract: Insurance payments may depend on latent micro states although only macro states and realized payments are observed. We study a sojourn-payment model for such aggregated multi-state systems under left-truncation and right-censoring. Starting from a micro-to-macro projection, we establish strong consistency and weak convergence for inverse-probability-weighted estimators of state-specific cumulative payment processes.

</details>


### [212] [Hierarchical Clustering As a Novel Solution to the Notorious Multicollinearity Problem in Observational Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30992)
*Yufei Wu,Zhiying Gu,Alex Deng,Jacob Zhu,Linsha Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对观测性因果推断中普遍存在的多重共线性问题，提出了一种基于层次聚类的新方法。通过按营销支出相关性对地理单元进行层次聚类，在聚类层面实施贝叶斯营销组合模型，有效缓解了共线性，使得不同营销渠道的因果效应可以被分别识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重共线性是观测因果推断中的长期难题，高度相关的自变量使得无法分离各自对结果的独立影响。传统方法（如收缩估计、主成分回归）在预测问题中有用，但无法保留原始的因果关系，因此不适用于因果推断。此外，现有利用细粒度截面数据的营销研究也未明确解决共线性问题，这构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 提出两步层次聚类方法：（1）对地理层面的数据进行标准化和去均值处理，建立统一尺度并消除共同趋势；（2）计算地理单元之间营销支出的成对距离，汇总相关性信息，将具有中等到强相关性的地理单元聚类。随后，在聚类层面数据上实施贝叶斯营销组合模型进行因果识别。

Result: 描述性证据和回归分析均证实，该层次聚类方法有效缓解了多重共线性，并促进了不同营销渠道影响的分别识别。

Conclusion: 层次聚类通过聚合相关变量可以有效缓解共线性问题，为多重共线性情境下的因果推断提供了一种通用、直观且可操作的解决方案，尤其在营销组合建模等广告效果测量领域具有实用价值。

Abstract: Multicollinearity is a long lasting challenge in observational causal inference, especially in regressions -- highly correlated independent variables make it hard to isolate their individual impacts on outcomes of interest. While common solutions such as shrinkage estimators and principal component regressions are helpful in prediction problems, a crucial limitation hinders their applicability to causal inference problems -- they cannot provide the original causal relationships. To fill the gap, we present an innovative and intuitive solution, by employing hierarchical clustering to aggregate data in a way that effectively alleviates collinearity. This method is generally applicable to causal problems featuring multicollinearity. We use a marketing application to demonstrate how and why it works.
  Expenditures on different advertising channels often exhibit correlations, making it exceedingly difficult to separately measure their impact. Many previous studies proposed to leverage granular cross-sectional data for better identification but, to our knowledge, none explicitly addressed multicollinearity, which undermines causal identification even with granular data. We propose to hierarchically cluster geographic units based on marketing spend correlation to reduce collinearity, and to implement a Bayesian Marketing Mix Model with cluster-level data. Such clustering happens in two steps -- we first normalize and demean geo-level data to establish a common scale and to eliminate the common trends; we then calculate pairwise distance to summarize marketing spend correlation between geos and cluster the ones with moderate to strong correlation. Both descriptive evidence and regression analysis affirm that such hierarchical clustering effectively mitigates collinearity and facilitates the separate identification of the impact of different marketing channels.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Joint discovery of governing partial differential equations from multi-source datasets by competitive optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30699)
*Hao Xu,Siyu Lou,Yuntian Chen,Dongxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MCO-PDE的竞争优化框架，能够从多源异构观测数据中自动发现共享的偏微分方程（PDE）。该框架通过训练独立神经代理、软竞争加权机制以及遗传算法进行结构搜索，同时识别控制方程的函数形式和参数，在少量观测数据条件下即可高精度恢复典型方程，并能处理不规则边界和异质系数等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于数据驱动的PDE发现方法通常仅依赖单一数据集，在观测受限时性能有限。然而实际中同一物理系统往往存在仅初始条件或边界配置不同的多个数据集。如何有效融合这些多源异构数据以提升方程发现的准确性和鲁棒性，是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出MCO-PDE框架：(1) 为每个数据源训练独立的神经网络代理模型；(2) 引入软竞争加权机制，动态评估各数据集的可靠性并聚合出全局一致性系数；(3) 结合遗传算法进行方程结构的组合搜索，同步辨识控制方程的函数形式与参数。

Result: 在七个案例中，每个数据集仅需50个观测点即可高精度恢复典型方程。框架能够处理具有不规则边界和异质系数的二维及三维问题，并成功从真实波浪水槽实验数据中提取出具有物理意义的控制方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为通过异构数据融合实现自动化科学发现开辟了一条有前景的路径，展示了多源数据协同在可解释科学机器学习中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Discovering governing equations directly from observational data is a key step towards interpretable scientific machine learning. Current data-driven approaches typically operate on a single dataset, inherently limiting their performance when faced with restricted observations. In practice, multiple datasets are often available for the same physical system, distinguished only by distinct initial conditions or boundary configurations. Here, we present a competitive optimization framework designed to discover shared partial differential equations (PDEs) from multi-source datasets, termed MCO-PDE. The framework first trains independent neural surrogates for each data source, and then employs a soft-competitive weighting mechanism to dynamically assess dataset credibility and aggregate a consensus global coefficient. Integrated with a genetic algorithm for structural search, this approach simultaneously identifies the functional forms and parameters of the governing laws. We demonstrate that fusing as few as 50 observations per dataset across seven cases recovers canonical equations with high accuracy. The framework inherently handles two- and three-dimensional domains characterized by irregular boundaries and heterogeneous coefficients, and successfully extracts physically meaningful laws from real-world wave-tank experiments. Overall, this work establishes a promising route for automated scientific discovery via heterogeneous data fusion.

</details>


### [214] [Accelerometry-Derived Digital Biomarkers for Cardiometabolic Risk: A Population-Representative Tabular Benchmark with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30702)
*Federico Felizzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入基于NHANES 2003-2006的加速度计心脏代谢基准数据集，包含1,381名成年人的加速度计数据、实验室生物标志物、饮食摄入和人体测量指标。评估三种表格学习方法（岭回归、XGBoost、TabPFN v2）预测HbA1c、甘油三酯和CRP的表现，并利用分割共形预测检验亚组公平性，揭示了边际覆盖与条件覆盖之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据基准未能反映真实临床场景中的复杂调查抽样、人口统计过采样和亚组公平性问题，需要一个更具代表性的基准来评估表格学习方法在临床预测中的公平性和不确定性量化能力。

Method: (1) 构建NHANES加速度计心脏代谢基准数据集，整合加速度计表型、空腹生物标志物、饮食及人体测量数据；(2) 采用岭回归、XGBoost和基础模型TabPFN v2三种方法预测HbA1c、甘油三酯和CRP；(3) 使用分割共形预测生成分布无关的90%预测区间，并按性别和种族/族裔亚组评估覆盖公平性。

Result: TabPFN v2整体性能最优（HbA1c R²=0.156，CRP R²=0.383），甘油三酯几乎不可预测（R²<0.05）。CRP和HbA1c的边际覆盖接近90%目标，但甘油三酯未达标。亚组层面存在局部覆盖不足（如墨西哥裔美国人的HbA1c），揭示了边际保证与临床公平所需的条件覆盖之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了表格基础模型在临床预测中的潜力与局限，强调亚组公平性评估需超越边际覆盖指标，条件覆盖对于临床公平至关重要。

Abstract: Structured tabular data dominates clinical medicine, yet existing benchmarks fail to reflect real-world properties like complex survey sampling, demographic oversampling, and subgroup fairness. We introduce the NHANES Accelerometry Cardiometabolic Benchmark, derived from NHANES 2003-2006, comprising 1,381 adults with hip-worn accelerometry, fasting laboratory biomarkers, dietary intake, and anthropometrics. We evaluate three tabular learning methods -- ridge regression, XGBoost, and the foundation model TabPFN v2 -- to predict glycated haemoglobin (HbA1c), fasting triglycerides, and C-reactive protein (CRP) from activity phenotypes and lifestyle covariates. TabPFN v2 achieves the best overall performance (HbA1c R^2=0.156, CRP R^2=0.383), while triglycerides remain largely unpredictable (R^2 < 0.05), consistent with known genetic dominance. We apply split conformal prediction to generate distribution-free 90% prediction intervals and evaluate demographic coverage equity across sex and race/ethnicity subgroups. Marginal coverage aligns with the 90% target for CRP and HbA1c but falls below for triglycerides. At the subgroup level, we observe localized undercoverage (e.g., HbA1c for Mexican American participants), illustrating the gap between marginal guarantees and the conditional coverage required for clinical fairness. Code and data are at https://github.com/felizzi/nhanes-accel-cardiometabolic-benchmark.

</details>


### [215] [From Search to Synthesis: Training LLMs as Zero-Shot Workflow Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30704)
*Gan Luo,Zihan Qin,Bin Dong,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaFlow 将工作流生成建模为元学习问题，通过监督微调+执行反馈强化学习两阶段训练，使 LLM 学会为任务组合可复用的算法工作流，在保持域内性能的同时展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的逐实例解决方案缺乏结构一致性，而手工设计任务级算法工作流虽能提供鲁棒性、可解释性和可复用性，但成本高昂。现有自动生成方法要么只产生实例级方案而无法学习任务级模式，要么无法泛化到训练配置之外。因此需要一种能自动学习任务级、可泛化工作流的方法。

Method: MetaFlow 采用两阶段训练：第一阶段，在合成工作流数据上进行监督微调（SFT），让模型学习基本的算子组合能力；第二阶段，使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），利用跨实例的执行反馈来优化端到端成功率。该方法将工作流生成框架化为给定任务和算子集的元学习问题。

Result: 在问答、代码生成和数学推理等基准测试上，MetaFlow 在域内任务上通过单次推理即可达到与 SOTA 基线相当的性能，同时在域外任务和新算子集上展现出显著的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaFlow 通过元学习框架成功实现了任务级工作流的自动生成，兼具域内高性能和域外强泛化性，为 LLM 的结构化、可靠部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel across a wide range of tasks, yet their instance-specific solutions often lack the structural consistency needed for reliable deployment. Workflows that encode recurring algorithmic patterns at the task level provide a principled framework, offering robustness across instance variations, interpretable traces for debugging, and reusability across problem instances. However, manually designing such workflows requires significant expertise and effort, limiting their broader application. While automatic workflow generation could address this bottleneck, existing methods either produce instance-specific solutions without learning task-level patterns, or cannot generalize beyond their training configurations. We present MetaFlow, which casts workflow generation as a meta-learning problem: given a task and an operator set, the model learns to compose solution strategies. MetaFlow trains in two stages: supervised fine-tuning on synthetic workflow data, followed by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) that uses execution feedback across problem instances in the task to improve end-to-end success. The resulting model produces effective workflows for trained tasks and exhibits strong generalization to untrained tasks and novel operator sets. Across benchmarks in question answering, code generation, and mathematical reasoning, MetaFlow achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art baselines on in-domain tasks with single inference, while demonstrating remarkable zero-shot generalization capabilities on out-of-domain tasks and operator sets.

</details>


### [216] [Why Do Few-Step Text Latents Fail When Image Latents Work? Non-Commitment at Sharp Categorical Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30705)
*Zhongyao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 连续文本潜在空间中，确定性少步生成失败源于解码器几何锐度导致的离散分支选择冲突，而非训练或规模不足。本文通过定理证明、诊断指标测量和两类逃逸机制（分类承诺与随机重注入），揭示了精度-深度-刚度之间的不可约权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性少步生成在连续图像潜在空间中表现良好，但在连续文本潜在空间中却产出不连贯文本。作者旨在揭示这一现象的根本原因——不是训练或规模问题，而是几何本质问题：平滑、正则性受限的确定性映射无法在锐利分类读出前解决离散分支选择。

Method: 1. 理论分析：在重叠区域证明后验均值终端步以决策边界周围 O(s(t)) 管内的潜在质量速率翻转 token（定理3）；在理想分离区域证明匹配的锐利输运律，包括维度相图——分离 M 个模态所需确定性刚度在 d=Ω(log M) 时增长为 Θ(√log M)（定理5-7），并用量面积恒等式连接至重叠管（定理17）。2. 诊断工具：提出 DABI（读出锐度）和 CCI（分类承诺）两个指标，在公开检查点上进行测量。3. 机制验证：识别两类逃逸连续界的方法——分类承诺（自回归解码器）和随机重注入（确定性 ODE 在 K=4 时 PPL=294 vs SDE=50）。

Result: 四个独立构建的连续文本解码器将边界对齐扰动的放大率远超范数匹配的各向同性扰动（DABI 从 5×10² 到 >10⁵），而图像解码器 DABI≈1。确定性 ODE 在 K=4 步时 PPL=294，同模型 SDE 仅 50。层级深度 B 的层次结构使每步峰值刚度减小 √B 倍。

Conclusion: 确定性连续类方法中，精度-深度-刚度之间的代价是不可约的，两类有效逃逸方案（分类承诺和随机重注入）均需跳出该类方法的框架。

Abstract: Deterministic few-step generation succeeds on continuous image latents but collapses to incoherent text on continuous text latents, and we show the cause is geometric rather than a training or scaling deficiency: a smooth, regularity-limited deterministic map cannot resolve a discrete branch choice before a sharp categorical readout, so few-step failure is governed by decoder sharpness, not transport accuracy. In the overlapping regime of real text autoencoders, we prove (Theorem 3) that the posterior-mean terminal step flips tokens at the rate of the latent mass in an $O(s(t))$ tube around decision boundaries. Two diagnostics, DABI (readout sharpness) and CCI (categorical commitment), measured on published checkpoints show that four independently built continuous-text decoders amplify a boundary-aligned perturbation far beyond a norm-matched isotropic one (DABI from $5\times10^{2}$ to $>10^{5}$), while image decoders have DABI $\approx 1$. Two mechanisms escape the continuous bound: categorical commitment (autoregressive decoders succeed despite sharper readouts) and stochastic re-injection (deterministic ODE at $K=4$ gives PPL 294 versus SDE 50 on the same model). In the idealized separated regime we prove matching sharp transport laws, including a dimension phase diagram: the deterministic stiffness needed to separate $M$ modes grows as $Θ(\sqrt{\log M})$ once the latent dimension is $Ω(\log M)$ (and as $M^{1/n}$ in fixed dimension), with a depth-$B$ hierarchy giving a $\sqrt{B}$-smaller per-step peak (Theorems 5-7); a coarea identity links these to the overlapping tube (Theorem 17). The result is an accuracy-depth-stiffness tradeoff: within the deterministic-continuous class the cost is irreducible, and both escapes step outside it.

</details>


### [217] [Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30709)
*Woernle Frank,Fedosov Vladimir,Grinenko Artemiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HGA 是一种即插即用的稀疏注意力机制，通过两级层次路由（块级摘要检索 + 组级精炼）在保持密集注意力近似质量（KL 散度仅 0.01–0.02 nats）的同时，将 GPU 显存需求从上下文长度解耦，使单张 RTX 5090 即可运行 64K token 上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 Transformer 的密集因果注意力需要存储全部历史 token 的 K/V，GPU 显存随上下文长度线性增长，严重制约了单卡可处理的最大上下文长度。现有稀疏注意力方法往往引入额外校准参数或需要重新训练。HGA 旨在实现零修改、零重训练的层次化稀疏注意力，使大模型在消费级 GPU 上也能处理超长上下文。

Method: HGA 采用两级层次路由：第一级利用紧凑的 RoPE 感知摘要（chunk-level summaries）快速检索相关块；第二级进一步将块内 token 分组，仅路由最相关的组；最终仅对筛选出的 token 子集执行精确注意力计算。完整历史 K/V 驻留在主机 RAM 或 NVMe 上，仅将路由后的小规模工作集传输至 GPU 显存。整个过程中不修改预训练权重 W_Q/W_K/W_V/W_O，不引入校准参数，无需重训练。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct 模型上，单张 RTX 5090（32GB）即可开箱运行 64K token 上下文（原模型在此硬件上无法存下 token 级 K/V）。在 4K–64K 所有测试长度上，路由注意力与密集注意力的 KL 散度保持在 0.01–0.02 nats 以内，稀疏度仅约 3%。

Conclusion: 层次路由引入的近似误差极小，剩余质量差距可能主要源于长上下文位置编码而非路由算法本身。HGA 证明即插即用的层次化稀疏注意力可有效将 GPU 显存与上下文长度解耦，使 RAM/NVMe 支持的 K/V 存储方案切实可行。

Abstract: Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) is a drop-in replacement for dense causal attention in pretrained long-context transformers. HGA preserves the original checkpoint parameters: the pretrained $W_Q$, $W_K$, $W_V$, and $W_O$ projections remain unchanged, no calibration parameters are introduced, and no retraining is required.
  Applied to Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8 on a single RTX~5090 (32GB), the patched model runs out of the box at a 64K-token context, where token-level K/V storage is not feasible on this hardware.
  Unlike previous sparse-attention methods, HGA performs hierarchical two-level routing. It first retrieves relevant chunks using compact RoPE-aware summaries and then refines the selection by routing only the most relevant groups before performing exact token-level attention. This hierarchical retrieval significantly reduces the number of fetched tokens while preserving exact attention over the retrieved token set, making RAM- and NVMe-backed storage practical.
  The full historical token K/V resides in host RAM or NVMe storage, while only a small routed working set is transferred to GPU memory during attention. Consequently, GPU memory consumption depends primarily on model weights and the routed working set rather than on the total context length.
  Across all tested context lengths (4K - 64K tokens), routed attention remains within approximately $0.01$--$0.02$ nats of dense attention while the sparsity used is just about 3%. These results suggest that the approximation introduced by hierarchical routing is small, and that the remaining quality gap is likely dominated by long-context positional encoding rather than by the routing algorithm itself.

</details>


### [218] [ReactionAtlas: Ab origine exploration of chemical reaction networks with machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30778)
*Stefan Gugler,Max Eissler,Khaled Kahouli,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReactionAtlas 是一种从少量种子分子出发、无需手工规则即可从头构建化学反应网络的框架。它结合了机器学习生成模型和 DFT 训练的机器学习力场（MLFF），从8个前生命种子分子出发，发现了约12,000种化合物和约47,000个反应，以前所未有的规模和精度绘制了小碳水化合物化学（最高至 C₄H₈O₄）的反应网络，为甲醛聚糖循环等生命起源关键路径提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建完整的化学反应网络（包含极小值点和过渡态）是化学的核心任务，但传统方法（如DFT）速度太慢且需要预先指定反应物和产物，难以实现大规模构建。

Method: 采用两步框架：(1) 机器学习生成模型从动力学采样的候选化合物中提出可能的反应；(2) 用DFT训练的机器学习力场（MLFF）筛选并验证过渡态，反应产物自动作为新种子进入后续搜索，实现从头开始的迭代式网络扩展。

Result: 从8个前生命种子分子出发，发现了约12,000种化合物和约47,000个反应。MLFF预测的过渡态在85%的情况下与PBE0参考值的RMSD在0.5 Å以内，且可轻松精修至PBE0水平。成功绘制了最高至C₄H₈O₄的小碳水化合物化学网络，包含电荷和立体化学信息。

Conclusion: ReactionAtlas 实现了大规模、高精度的化学反应网络从头构建，为深入理解甲醛聚糖化学等生命起源关键反应路径提供了有力工具，并揭示了甲醛聚糖化学的替代反应途径。

Abstract: Mapping a chemical reaction network, the graph of minima and transition states (TS) and the elementary reactions connecting them, is the natural language of chemistry, from catalysis to combustion to the origin of life. Constructing such a reaction network for a given chemistry has been impractical: it requires finding and characterizing tens of thousands of TS, a task for which traditional methods such as density functional theory (DFT) are typically prohibitively slow and require reactant and product as input. We introduce ReactionAtlas, which builds a reaction network $\textit{ab origine}$ from a handful of seed molecules and without hand-crafted rules. Specifically, our machine-learned generative model proposes reactions from kinetically sampled candidate compounds and a DFT-trained machine learned force field (MLFF) filters them to valid TS, the resulting products of which enter the search as new seeds. Starting from eight pre-biotic seeds (CH$_2$O, H$_2$O, OH$^-$, H$_3$O$^+$, CO$_2$, H$_2$CO$_3$, HCO$_3^-$, H), ReactionAtlas discovers $\sim$47,000 reactions among $\sim$12,000 compounds. The MLFF TSs match the PBE0 references within 0.5 Å RMSD in 85% of the cases and can be easily brought to the PBE0 level. Thus, ReactionAtlas maps small carbohydrate chemistry up to C$_4$H$_8$O$_4$ at unprecedented scale and accuracy, including charge and stereo information. It enables novel insights into many well-studied reaction paths, including the formose cycle, which we highlight for its centrality to the chemical origins of life. Notably, our framework also allows establishing alternative reaction pathways for formose chemistry.

</details>


### [219] [Revocable Learned State via Process Sidecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30788)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"过程边车"（process sidecars）方法，用于在语言模型多阶段微调后撤销记忆。核心思想是：安全训练阶段会"运输"记忆方向，因此不能简单用任务算术减法撤销记忆。该方法通过双系数编辑族 $\hatθ(λ,γ)$，利用安全训练过程的割线估计来校正方向，理论上可达二阶精度，实验表明在多个模型上优于朴素任务算术和一阶过程JVP方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常按阶段微调：先注入技能/记忆，后进行安全训练使其拒绝相关输出。当需要撤销记忆时，由于安全训练阶段的优化器已将记忆方向"运输"（transport）到新方向，直接减去原始记忆更新无法正确恢复。现有任务算术方法忽略这一过程信息，导致一阶反事实误差。因此需要一种利用安全训练过程信息来精确撤销记忆的方法。

Method: 提出过程边车（process sidecars）编辑族：$\hatθ(λ,γ)=θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}-λΔ_{\mathrm{M}}-γ\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$，其中 $\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}=\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}(Δ_{\mathrm{M}})-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$，$\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}$ 是通过已实现的安全训练AdamW过程构造的中心割线算子。实现时取 $\varepsilon=1$（自然记忆编辑尺度），复用 $θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}$ 作为正端点，并额外计算一条安全轨迹 $θ_{\mathrm{A}}-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$。理论上证明：(1) 精确边车在 $(λ,γ)=(1,1)$ 时恢复反事实纯安全模型 $θ_{\mathrm{AS}}$ 至二阶精度（将AdamW视为参数、一阶矩、二阶矩上的增广状态映射）；(2) 一旦未来安全训练弯曲了记忆方向，任何标量任务算术编辑都存在一阶反事实误差，而过程边车编辑达到二阶精度。

Result: 在三个模型上验证：验证集选择的二维编辑在所有实验中均优于朴素任务算术（naive task arithmetic），并且在所有配对实验中优于 $γ=λ$ 的过程JVP子族（即缓存二维网格的对角线切片），在保留拒绝闭包（held-out refusal closure）指标上表现更优。

Conclusion: 在安全训练后再撤销记忆，必须考虑安全优化器对记忆方向的运输效应。过程边车方法通过利用已实现的安全训练过程信息（仅需额外一条安全轨迹），实现了二阶精度的记忆撤销，显著优于忽略过程信息的任务算术方法。

Abstract: Language models are often adapted in stages: a public skill phase, a private memory phase, and a later safety phase that learns to refuse outputs tied to the remembered entities. Revoking the memory after the safety phase is not the same problem as subtracting the memory update: the later safety optimizer has transported the memory direction. We introduce process sidecars, a two-coefficient edit family $\hatθ(λ,γ)=θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}-λΔ_{\mathrm{M}}-γ\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$, with $\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}=\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}(Δ_{\mathrm{M}})-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$, where $\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}$ is a centered secant through the realized future AdamW safety-training process. The implementation uses $\varepsilon=1$ at the natural memory-edit scale; it reuses $θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}$ as the positive endpoint and computes one additional safety trace at $θ_{\mathrm{A}}-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$. We prove two things. First, the exact sidecar, using the true transported direction $R_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$ rather than the secant estimate, at $(λ,γ)=(1,1)$ recovers the counterfactual safety-only oracle $θ_{\mathrm{AS}}$ up to second order; the proof treats AdamW as an augmented-state map over parameters, first moments, and second moments. Second, this process information is necessary: whenever future safety training bends the memory direction, every scalar task-arithmetic edit leaves first-order counterfactual error, while the process-sidecar edit is second-order accurate. Across three models, the validation-selected 2D edit improves held-out refusal closure over naive task arithmetic in all trials, and over the $γ=λ$ process-JVP subfamily, the diagonal slice of the cached 2D grid, in all paired trials.

</details>


### [220] [Predictable GRPO: A Closed-Form Model of Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30789)
*Rajat Ghosh,Datta Nimmaturi,Aryan Singhal,Vaishnavi Bhargava,Henry Wong,Johnu George,Debojyoti Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为GRPO训练动态建立了第一性原理的降阶模型，将经验性的单指数饱和律作为过阻尼极限纳入，揭示了惯性项对应的慢启动阶段，并推导出组大小不变性、刷新间隔稳定性阈值及过阻尼-振荡转变等可独立检验的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的标准工具，但其训练动态仍停留在经验描述层面：奖励曲线用低参数函数形式拟合，拟合常数缺乏机制性含义，超参数选择依赖试错。需要从第一性原理出发建立具有机制解释力的动力学模型。

Method: 作者从GRPO更新规则出发，在平均场假设下推导出描述策略分布的降阶动力学方程，将其映射为受驱阻尼振荡器的运动方程。该模型自然包含惯性项和势能函数，经验单指数饱和律作为过阻尼极限被包含其中。模型预言：(1)确定性轨迹的组大小不变性及1/G的稳态涨落；(2)由刚度决定的刷新间隔稳定性阈值；(3)过阻尼到振荡动力学的相变。在可控的softmax-bandit精确约化实验中验证稳定性和振荡预测。

Result: 在三类模型和两种组大小设置下，闭合形式的轨迹对训练奖励的拟合达到R²≥0.91；预测的组大小不变性在奖励曲线和八个数学基准的分布外迁移上均成立；softmax-bandit精确约化实验复现了过阻尼-振荡转变，刷新间隔稳定性阈值落在独立测量的刚度值处。

Conclusion: 该降阶模型为GRPO训练动态提供了机制性理解，将经验拟合常数重新解释为势能的固定点、逆刚度和曲率标度指数，并提供了可分离奖励黑客、优势退化、策略集中和动力学不稳定等不同失效模式的诊断工具。深度网络上的实验验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a standard tool for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, yet its training dynamics are still described empirically: reward trajectories are fit with low-parameter functional forms whose constants carry no mechanistic meaning, and hyperparameter choices remain a matter of trial and error.  We develop a first-principles reduced-order model of these dynamics. The reduction has three consequences. First, it subsumes the empirical single-exponential saturation law as its overdamped limit, recasting the fitted plateau, timescale, and size exponent as the fixed point, inverse stiffness, and curvature-scaling exponent of the underlying potential, and adding, through the retained inertial term, the slow-start phase the single exponential cannot represent. Second, it yields predictions tied to independently measurable quantities rather than fitted ones: group-size invariance of the deterministic trajectory with a $1/G$ stationary fluctuation, a sharp stability threshold in the refresh interval, and an overdamped-to-oscillatory transition. Third, it furnishes diagnostics that separate failure modes a reward curve alone conflates -- reward hacking, advantage degeneracy, policy concentration, and dynamical instability. Across three models and two group sizes, the closed-form trajectory fits training reward to $R^2 \geq 0.91$ and the predicted group-size invariance holds on both the reward curve and out-of-distribution transfer to eight math benchmarks. The stability and oscillatory predictions are exercised in a controlled exact-reduction setting where the mean-field assumption holds exactly: a softmax-bandit reduction reproduces the predicted overdamped-to-oscillatory transition and locates the refresh-interval stability threshold at the independently measured stiffness, with a deep-network demonstration left to future work.

</details>


### [221] [Gradient Smoothing: Coupling Layer-wise Updates for Improved Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30813)
*Haoming Meng,Anton Sugolov,Vardan Papyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"深度梯度增强"的通用优化范式，通过对深度维度上各层的优化器更新进行变换来改进训练。作者具体研究并实例化了一种简单的局部窗口平滑算子，该方法可直接作用于任意基础优化器产生的逐块更新，计算开销极小，并在语言模型预训练、LLM推理后训练、扩散模型和ViT图像分类等多种任务中持续提升优化和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络（如Transformer）中具有重复架构块的层之间，在训练过程中往往会涌现出结构化的跨层关系。利用这种跨深度结构来改进优化过程是一个有前景但尚未被充分探索的方向。

Method: 提出"深度梯度增强"（Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation）范式：对基础优化器产生的逐块更新沿深度维度进行变换后，再应用到各层。在此框架下，研究"梯度平滑"（Gradient Smoothing）方法族，并实例化为简单的局部"窗口平滑"（Window Smoothing）算子。该方法与任意基础优化器（SGD、Adam、Muon等）兼容，计算开销极低，无需修改模型架构或训练目标。

Result: 在语言模型预训练、LLM推理RL后训练、扩散建模、ViT图像分类等多种架构和训练设置下，梯度平滑持续提升优化效果和泛化性能。此外，它促进了更结构化的跨深度表示演化，与其作为结构化深度预处理方法的解释一致。

Conclusion: 深度梯度增强是一个有前景的利用跨深度结构进行优化的框架，梯度平滑作为一种简单且广泛适用的实例化方法，在不修改模型架构或训练目标的前提下，可一致性地提升多种场景下的性能。

Abstract: Deep neural networks with repeated architectural blocks, such as transformers, often exhibit structured relationships across layers that emerge during training. Motivated by this observation, we introduce \emph{Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation}, a general optimization paradigm in which the update applied to each layer is obtained by transforming the collection of block-wise optimizer updates along the depth dimension. Within this framework, we study \emph{Gradient Smoothing}, a family of depth-wise smoothing methods, and instantiate it with a simple local \emph{Window Smoothing} operator. The resulting method operates directly on block-wise updates produced by arbitrary base optimizers (e.g., SGD, Adam, Muon), incurs minimal computational overhead, and is compatible with existing optimization pipelines. We evaluate Gradient Smoothing across a diverse set of architectures and training regimes, including language model pretraining, RL post-training of LLMs for reasoning, diffusion modeling, and image classification with Vision Transformers. Across these settings, Gradient Smoothing consistently improves optimization and generalization performance without modifying model architectures or training objectives. We further show that it promotes more structured representation evolution across depth, consistent with its interpretation as a structured depth-wise preconditioning method. Together, these results establish Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation as a promising framework for exploiting cross-depth structure in optimization and demonstrate Gradient Smoothing as a simple and broadly applicable instantiation.

</details>


### [222] [Mind the Residual Gap: Probabilistic Downscaling under Real-World Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30821)
*Yujin Kim,Nidhi Soma,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了均值-残差降尺度范式中"残差目标错误设定"这一根本问题——训练和测试时所需残差分布存在系统性偏差，并提出ReMatch方法，通过PCA空间中的最优传输对齐训练残差分布与测试时域，从而修复该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值-残差框架在实际降尺度中普遍产生有偏和欠离散的集合预报，现有观点常归因于预测不确定性校准不足。作者指出根本原因在于残差目标错误设定（residual target misspecification）——确定性均值预测的降尺度偏差导致训练时诱发的残差分布与测试时所需的不一致，这是一个比通用校准问题更基础的分布失配问题。

Method: 提出ReMatch（Residual Distribution Matching）：首先利用PCA降维将残差投影到低维空间，然后在该空间中通过最优传输（optimal transport）将训练残差分布向测试时目标分布对齐，从而修正训练阶段随机生成器所见的残差目标，保持均值-残差框架的统计优势同时消除训练-测试分布失配。

Result: 在受控合成基准实验（不同偏差水平）和真实HRRR-ERA5风场降尺度任务上，ReMatch显著降低了欠离散性，改善了校准指标（SSR和CRPS），并优于标准均值-残差模型及其变体以及当前最先进的超分辨率模型。

Conclusion: 残差目标错误设定是均值-残差降尺度范式的根本缺陷，ReMatch通过最优传输驱动的分布匹配有效弥合了训练-测试残差分布鸿沟，为概率降尺度提供了一条原理性修复路径。

Abstract: Probabilistic downscaling is the task of modeling the conditional distribution of high-resolution fields given coarse inputs, and is a central challenge to atmospheric science, climate modeling, and other multiscale physical systems. A widely used paradigm decomposes the problem into a deterministic mean predictor followed by a stochastic residual generator. While effective in idealized settings, this mean--residual approach frequently produces biased and under-dispersive ensembles in real-world applications. Is this merely generic predictive uncertainty miscalibration? We show that the root cause is more fundamental: residual target misspecification, the residual distribution induced during training differs systematically from the one required at test time due to downscaling bias. To close this gap, we introduce ReMatch (Residual Distribution Matching). ReMatch aligns the training residual distribution toward the test-time regime via optimal transport in a low-dimensional PCA space. This preserves the statistical benefits of the mean--residual framework while reducing the train--test mismatch in the residual targets seen by the stochastic generator. On a controlled synthetic benchmark with varying bias levels and a real-world HRRR--ERA5 wind field downscaling task, ReMatch substantially reduces under-dispersion, improves calibration (SSR and CRPS), and outperforms strong baselines, including the standard mean--residual model and its variants, as well as state-of-the-art super-resolution models. Our code is available at https://github.com/sdean-group/ReMatch.git.

</details>


### [223] [A Stationary-Distribution Theory for Triplet-Based Plateau Search in Random Forest Ensemble-Size Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30837)
*Andrey A. Dukhovny,Andrey M. Lange*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为基于plateau调参的随机森林建立了平稳分布理论，将中心集成规模Bt建模为几何网格上的生灭马尔可夫链，推导其平稳分布，证明平稳中心B*=O(ε⁻²)，并刻画平稳散布为σ_{B,*}=O(ε⁻²)，方差O(ε⁻⁴)，相对散布独立于ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林中树的数量是核心计算参数，plateau-based调参通过OOB得分的局部比较自适应调整该参数。但在其他超参数稳定后，中心三元组点并不收敛到确定值，而是围绕一个平稳状态波动。现有研究缺乏对这一随机过程的理论刻画，需要建立平稳分布理论来理解其长期行为。

Method: 将中心集成规模Bt建模为几何网格上的生灭马尔可夫链，利用局部平衡推导平稳分布。采用前导中心折叠正态近似获得原始更新规则及对称修正变体的平衡方程。通过局部高斯近似和Fokker-Planck解释给出网格级方差常数，并转换到集成规模尺度。

Result: 平稳中心B*=O(ε⁻²)（ε↓0）；平稳标准差σ_{B,*}=O(ε⁻²)；方差O(ε⁻⁴)；前导相对散布独立于ε，仅受尺度因子和更新规则控制。

Conclusion: Plateau-based随机森林调参应被理解为一个随机过程而非确定性停止规则。该理论为自适应集成规模选择提供了概率视角的数学基础。

Abstract: The number of trees is a central computational parameter in Random Forests: increasing it reduces finite-ensemble variability but increases training and prediction cost. Plateau-based tuning adapts this parameter through local comparisons of out-of-bag scores at a geometric triplet of tree counts. After the remaining hyperparameters have stabilized, however, the central triplet point need not converge to a deterministic value; instead, it fluctuates around a stationary regime.
  This paper develops a stationary-distribution theory for this process. The central ensemble size $B_t$ is modeled as a birth-death Markov chain on a geometric grid, and its stationary distribution is derived through local balance. Under a leading centered folded-normal approximation, equilibrium equations are obtained for the original update rule and a symmetric modified variant, implying that the stationary center $B_*=O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ as $\varepsilon\downarrow 0$.
  The stationary spread is also characterized. A local Gaussian approximation and a Fokker-Planck interpretation give grid-level variance constants. After conversion to the ensemble-size scale, $σ_{B,*}=O(\varepsilon^{-2})$, while the variance is $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$. The leading relative spread is independent of $\varepsilon$ and controlled by the scale factor and update rule. These results interpret plateau-based Random Forest tuning as a stochastic process rather than a deterministic stopping rule.

</details>


### [224] [Partition-Guided Distance Saliency: Bridging Decision and Objective Spaces in Many-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30836)
*Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes,Flávio Vinícius Cruzeiro Martins,Elizabeth Fialho Wanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PGDS框架，通过三阶段流水线（代理建模→自动分区与目标锚点识别→扰动敏感性量化）将决策变量分类为"驱动因子"和"阻碍因子"，解决了高维目标优化中决策变量与目标间关系的可解释性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着目标数量增加，Pareto前沿复杂度急剧上升，决策变量与目标之间的关系变得高度不透明，传统可视化方法失效，决策者面临"认知干旱"——无法在没有先验知识的情况下识别相关权衡或指定目标区域。

Method: 提出PGDS三阶段框架：(1)训练代理模型，学习决策空间中几何距离到目标空间邻近性的映射；(2)自动对目标景观进行分区，识别局部"支配点"作为自动化改进目标；(3)通过衡量每个变量的扰动引起的距离偏移，量化解决方案位置对每个决策变量的敏感性，据此将特征分类为促进收敛的"驱动因子(Drivers)"或代表几何约束的"阻碍因子(Blockers)"。

Result: 在10目标基准测试和焊接梁物理信息工程问题上验证，PGDS提供了差异化的、可操作的见解，这是传统可视化和基于规则的XAI方法无法提供的。

Conclusion: PGDS框架有效弥合了多目标优化中的可解释性鸿沟，通过几何直觉驱动的自动化解释流程，为决策者提供了关于决策变量如何影响收敛路径的清晰、可操作的分类洞察。

Abstract: Explainability in Many-Objective Optimization (MaO) is currently hindered by the escalating complexity of the Pareto front, which renders the relationship between high-dimensional decision variables and objective outcomes increasingly opaque. As the number of objectives exceeds the limits of traditional visualization, decision-makers encounter a ``cognitive drought'' in identifying relevant trade-offs or specifying target regions without a priori knowledge. To bridge this interpretability gap, we introduce the {Partition-Guided Distance Saliency (PGDS)} framework, a novel XAI approach designed for continuous optimization landscapes. Our framework automates the explanation process through a three-stage pipeline that prioritizes geometric intuition over abstract rules. First, we employ a surrogate model that learns how geometric distances in the decision space map to proximity in the objective space. Second, to address the difficulty of manual target selection in high dimensions, the framework automatically partitions the objective landscape into distinct regions and identifies local ``Dominating Points'' to serve as automated targets for improvement. Third, we quantify how sensitive a solution's position is to each decision variable by measuring the distance shifts induced by perturbations to each variable. This allows PGDS to categorize features as either ``Drivers'' which facilitate convergence toward preferred regions, or ``Blockers'' which represent geometric constraints hindering further progress. Validation on 10-objective benchmarks and a physics-informed engineering problem (Welded Beam) demonstrates that PGDS provides differentiated, actionable insights that traditional visualization and rule-based XAI methods fail to provide.

</details>


### [225] [Behavior Cloning is Not All You Need: The Optimality of On-Policy Distillation for Noisy Expert Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30923)
*Ved Sriraman,Peihan Liu,Daniel Hsu,Adam Block*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出"噪声专家模型"来解释离线模仿学习与在线模仿学习之间的性能差距：离线学习在噪声专家数据下样本复杂度随 horizon 呈指数增长，而在线方法（如 OPD 变体）仅需多项式依赖，为语言模型训练中在线蒸馏优于监督微调提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机源于一个实践与理论的矛盾：理论上离线模仿学习可以是 horizon-free 且最优的，但实践中在线方法（如 on-policy distillation）常优于离线方法（如 SFT）。作者通过"噪声专家"设定来建模这一现象——学习器只能访问专家的噪声版本，但需与干净专家的奖励竞争，这贴合了语言模型训练中专家本身不完美（如长链思维推理）的现实。

Method: 作者提出噪声专家模型（noisy expert model），在该框架下：(1) 证明离线学习从噪声轨迹中竞争干净专家需指数级样本复杂度，与干净专家设定形成鲜明对比；(2) 提出一种新的 OPD 在线交互变体，证明其在一般情况下对 horizon 仅需多项式依赖；(3) 在专家噪声分布满足自然条件下，给出 horizon-free 保证，尽管牺牲了对策略类大小的统计效率；(4) 将结果扩展到干净专家确定性、但 corruption 未知的更现实设定中，并提供算法与下界。

Result: 在噪声专家设定下，严格证明了离线与在线模仿学习之间存在尖锐分离：离线学习样本复杂度随 horizon 指数增长，而在线学习仅需多项式依赖。分析还导出了一种在 LM 训练中已被经验使用的替代损失函数。扩展至未知 corruption 设定下的结果，为"OPD 可优于 SFT"提供了理论支撑。

Conclusion: 当专家不完美时，在线模仿学习（OPD）相比离线方法（SFT）具有根本性的理论优势。该结论为语言模型从非完美教师中训练时选择在线蒸馏策略提供了坚实的理论依据。

Abstract: Imitation Learning is a natural framework for learning in sequential decision-making systems and has emerged as the dominant paradigm through which we understand language model training. A central puzzle is that, while in theory offline IL can be horizon-free and optimal, in practice online methods such as on-policy distillation often outperform offline methods such as supervised fine-tuning. We propose a noisy expert model to explain this gap, in which the learner only has access to a noisy version of the expert's policy, but wishes to compete against the reward achieved by a clean expert, motivated by the fact that in many applications, e.g. training language models to perform long chains of thought, the expert is often imperfect. In this setting, we show a sharp separation between offline and online IL. Offline learning from noisy trajectories is fundamentally hard: to compete with the clean expert, the sample complexity must grow exponentially, in contradistinction to the clean expert setting where no explicit horizon dependence exists. In contrast, we prove that online interaction with the noisy expert via a novel variant of OPD enables polynomial dependence on the horizon in general. We further show that, under a natural condition on the expert noise distribution, which we show to be necessary for any horizon-free sample complexity, one can obtain such a guarantee, although our proposed algorithm sacrifices statistical efficiency in its dependence on the size of the policy class. Our analysis leads to an alternative loss function that is commonly considered empirically for LM training. We further provide algorithms and lower bounds, and extend our results to the more realistic setting of unknown corruption when the clean expert is deterministic, thereby providing a theoretical foundation for why OPD can outperform SFT when training language models from imperfect teachers.

</details>


### [226] [A Transferable Learned Temporal Prior for Transmission Reconstruction and Decision-Relevant Uncertainty in Real Outbreak Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30842)
*Md Ahsan Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了疫情传播重建中时序和传播标签的不确定性，提出了一种在11个疾病家族上预训练、参数锁定后零样本应用于目标疫情的逻辑回归时序先验，在ANDV基准上显著优于基线；同时通过mpox系统发育审计揭示了标签不可靠性，并证明保留不确定边会实质改变干预优先级的来源病例排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前疫情传播重建方法将流行病学时序和传播标签视为确定性真实值，但这两者从未被系统评估过。这种对确定性假设的依赖可能导致传播链推断偏差，进而影响公共卫生干预决策的准确性。

Method: （1）在11个疾病家族上训练逻辑回归时序先验模型，在接触任何目标疫情数据之前锁定所有参数；（2）将锁定先验零样本应用于包含29个任务的严格ANDV父节点排序基准；（3）对75对纽约mpox宿主间传播对进行系统发育一致性审计，作为独立的标签可靠性证据；（4）在ANDV和广东Delta图中保留不确定边，分析其对Top-5来源优先级集合的影响（Jaccard系数评估）。

Result: 锁定先验的MRR达0.571（基线0.274），Top-1准确率37.9%（基线13.8%），均显著优于最佳源训练参数基线（置换检验p≤0.0002）。mpox审计中54.67%（精确95%CI: 42.75-66.21%）的宿主间传播对在基因组水平上未获解决或未被支持。保留不确定边后，Top-5来源优先级集合的Jaccard系数为0.429-0.667，表明来源病例排序发生实质性改变。

Conclusion: 在所检验的疫情证据模块中，传播标签的不确定性是可测量的；保留不确定链接会改变哪些来源病例被优先列为干预目标。这挑战了传播重建中将时序和标签视为确定性真值的传统做法，倡导采用概率性框架以更稳健地指导疫情响应。

Abstract: Outbreak transmission reconstruction treats epidemiological timing and transmission labels as deterministic ground truth; neither has been systematically evaluated. We trained a logistic regression temporal prior on eleven disease families, locked all parameters before accessing any target outbreak data, and applied it without refitting to a strict Andes virus (ANDV) parent-ranking benchmark of 29 tasks. The locked prior achieved mean reciprocal rank (MRR) 0.571 versus 0.274 and Top-1 accuracy 37.9% versus 13.8% against the best source-trained parametric baseline (permutation p <= 0.0002; 7-8 reversals to lose MRR significance). A phylogenetic concordance audit of 75 NYC mpox inter-host pairs - independent label-reliability evidence rather than a prior validation - found that 54.67% (exact 95% CI: 42.75-66.21%) were genomically unresolved or unsupported. Retaining uncertain edges in ANDV and Guangdong Delta graphs shifted top-5 source-priority sets (Jaccard 0.429-0.667). Transmission-label uncertainty was measurable in the outbreak evidence modules examined, and retaining uncertain links changed which source cases were prioritized for intervention.

</details>


### [227] [Multistage Defer Trees for Hybrid Interpretability: If at First You Can't Succeed, Tree Again](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30995)
*Zakk Heile,Hayden McTavish,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多阶段延迟树（Multistage Defer Trees），一种由多个稀疏决策树组成的序列模型：每棵树对大部分样本做出预测，同时将少量困难样本延迟到下一棵树或最终的黑箱模型处理。该方法能在匹配复杂树集成模型性能的同时，让大多数样本仅经过一棵或少数几棵稀疏决策树完成分类，从而在单棵树方法不足的场景下拓展了准确率-可解释性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前存在准确率与可解释性之间的权衡困境：单棵优化良好的决策树在噪声领域可媲美黑箱模型，但在其他场景下，复杂的树集成模型虽准确率更高却丧失可解释性。作者希望实现在大多数样本上保持可解释性（仅用少量稀疏树），同时维持最先进的准确率。

Method: 提出 Multistage Defer Trees 框架：构建一系列稀疏决策树，每棵树对大部分样本直接预测，对不确定样本则"延迟"（defer）至序列中的下一棵树或最终的黑箱模型。训练过程中采用多种技术以保证模型的简洁性，使大部分样本仅路由通过一棵或少数几棵稀疏树。

Result: 该方法能够匹配复杂树集成模型（如梯度提升树、随机森林等）的预测性能，同时将大多数样本路由通过仅一棵或少数几棵稀疏决策树，显著提升了整体模型的可解释性。

Conclusion: 即使在复杂模型不可或缺的场景下，模型也未必需要完全黑箱化。Multistage Defer Trees 拓展了准确率-可解释性前沿边界，为实践者在需要高准确率时提供了更具可解释性的建模选择。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that well-optimized individual decision trees can match complex black box models in some settings, primarily in noisy domains. For the remaining settings, however, complex ensembled compositions of trees often achieve higher accuracy at the cost of interpretability, leaving practitioners with difficult modeling decisions along an accuracy-interpretability tradeoff. Ideally, we would like to classify as much of the data as possible with one or a small number of trees, achieving interpretability for most samples while maintaining state-of-the-art accuracy. We introduce Multistage Defer Trees: a sequence of sparse decision trees that each make predictions for most samples, while deferring a small proportion to the next tree in the sequence or, ultimately, to a black box. We demonstrate that we can train this model class to match the performance of complex tree-based ensembles while routing most samples through only one or a small number of sparse decision trees. We discuss a range of techniques for training these models while maintaining simplicity. Our method expands the accuracy--interpretability frontier in settings where single-tree methods remain insufficient, demonstrating that even when complex models are necessary, they need not be fully opaque.

</details>


### [228] [Can Tabular In-Context Learners Generalize to Biomolecular Property Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31126)
*Davy Guan,Lu Zhang,Asiri Wijesinghe,Allen Zhu,He Zhao,Helen Power,F. Hafna Ahmed,Andrew Warden,Cheng Soon Ong,Daniel M. Steinberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文发现，基于合成表格数据预训练的表格基础模型（如TabPFN3、TabICL）在生物分子性质预测的小样本场景中表现出色，但其性能强烈依赖于所使用的序列或分子表征，而非模型自身的因果先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子性质预测的核心瓶颈在于有限标注数据下的数据高效学习。现有强预训练编码器已能提供丰富的固定长度表征，挑战转向了小样本回归/分类器的构建。作者好奇：在合成因果图表上预训练的表格基础模型，其归纳偏置是否能够迁移到蛋白质序列和分子图这类完全不同的数据模态上。

Method: 将每种方法视为"预测器-表征"对，在两大领域进行评估：(1) 蛋白质适应度回归——使用固定ESMC表征，在ProteinGym和酯酶数据集上测试表格上下文学习（in-context learning）；(2) 小分子分类——使用ECFP/RDKit描述符，在TDC ADMET、MoleculeNet、FS-Mol和DrugOOD四个基准上对比不同表征与预测器的组合效果。

Result: 蛋白质任务中，基于ESMC表征的表格上下文学习方法始终具有竞争力；小分子任务中，没有单一的"预测器-表征"配对在所有基准上占优，表征选择成为决定性能的首要因素——当预测器先验对分子结构无差异时，表征的作用被放大。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型是生物分子预测任务上的强劲选手，但其成功与否高度依赖所用表征，单纯的表格因果先验并不足以保证跨域迁移，表征工程仍是关键。

Abstract: Predicting biomolecular properties from limited labeled data is a central bottleneck in protein engineering and small-molecule design. As strong pretrained encoders now supply rich fixed-length representations, the difficulty has shifted from representation learning to building a data-efficient predictor for the few-shot regime. Tabular foundation models such as TabPFN3 and TabICL are unlikely candidates for this role: they are in-context learners pretrained on synthetic tables drawn from random causal graphs, a generative prior with no obvious correspondence to the processes that produce protein sequences or molecular graphs. That this tabular, causal inductive bias should transfer to biomolecular data at all is unintuitive, yet we find it does. Treating each method as a predictor-representation pair, we evaluate across two domains. Over a fixed ESMC representation, tabular in-context learning is consistently competitive for protein fitness regression on ProteinGym and a diverse esterase dataset. For small-molecule classification with ECFP/RDKit descriptors, no single pairing dominates across TDC ADMET, MoleculeNet, FS-Mol, and DrugOOD; representation choice becomes a primary determinant, as expected when the predictor's own prior is indifferent to molecular structure. We conclude that tabular foundation models are strong performers on biomolecular prediction tasks, but that their performance depends strongly on the sequence or molecular representation used.

</details>


### [229] [Personalizing Marketplace Policies with Competing Objectives and Constrained Experiments: Evidence from a Job Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30932)
*Yufei Wu,Zhen Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成框架，用于在双边招聘市场中个性化设定免费价值阈值（零值门槛政策）。面对交叉边外部性和市场干扰导致的实验约束（仅少量离散处理水平），作者通过集成式混合排序模型分别优化目标指标与防护指标，并结合处理效应外推方法，在防护约束下实现了显著的目标指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边市场中，优化雇主侧指标可能损害求职者体验，标准 uplift 方法由于两个原因失效：(1) 交叉边外部性要求多目标优化；(2) 市场干扰迫使采用集群随机化，导致仅有少量离散处理水平（正性违反），排除了连续处理方法的直接应用。

Method: 提出三组件集成框架：(1) 基于集成的混合排序模型，分别建模目标指标和防护指标，将防护风险降低超10%；(2) 处理效应外推方法，基于单调性假设（并经实证验证），从有限实验变异外推至未测试的政策水平；(3) 生产部署，用上线后数据验证外推准确性和防护合规性。

Result: 个性化政策在目标指标上实现了统计显著且经济上可观的提升，同时遵守了参与度防护约束。上线后数据确认了处理效应外推的准确性及防护指标的合规性。

Conclusion: 即使在实验严重受限且多目标竞争的条件下，有原则的方法论仍可实现有意义的个性化——这是许多现实市场中的常见情形。

Abstract: Two-sided marketplaces connect distinct user groups whose interests often conflict -- improving outcomes on one side could degrade the other side's experience. To address this challenge, we deploy an integrated framework for personalizing free-value thresholds -- a policy governing the scope of complimentary services for job listings -- across a two-sided job marketplace connecting millions of employers and job seekers. Our personalized policy delivers statistically significant and economically sizable lift in the target metric while respecting engagement guardrail constraints.
  Direct application of standard uplift methods proves insufficient here for two reasons. First, cross-side externalities demand multi-objective optimization: maximizing employer-side metrics risks harming job seeker engagement, with effects varying substantially across job segments. Second, marketplace interference necessitates cluster-level randomization, limiting us to few discrete treatment levels -- effectively a form of positivity violation that rules out methods designed for continuous treatments.
  We contribute an integrated framework with three components. Our ensemble-based hybrid ranking models target and guardrail metrics separately, cutting guardrail risk by over 10% for equivalent target gains compared to single-objective approaches. A treatment effect extrapolation method extends our estimates from limited experimental variation to untested policy levels, relying on monotonicity assumptions that we validate empirically. Finally, we present production deployment, where post-launch data confirms both extrapolation accuracy and guardrail compliance.
  Our deployed system demonstrates that principled methodology can enable meaningful personalization even when experiments are severely constrained and different objectives compete -- common conditions that characterize many real-world marketplaces.

</details>


### [230] [Learning Gaussian Graphical Models from a Glauber Trajectory Without Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31230)
*Eric Shen,Tony Wu,Mahbod Majid,Ankur Moitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多项式时间算法，能够从Glauber动力学的单条轨迹中恢复$d$-稀疏高斯图模型的条件独立图，且轨迹长度保证不依赖于混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典i.i.d.设定下，虽然次线性样本保证已知，但多项式时间算法仍悬而未决；此外，许多实际应用产生的是时间相关观测而非独立同分布样本。本文旨在弥合这一差距，利用单条Glauber轨迹在多项式时间内实现图结构恢复。

Method: 算法包含三个技术组件：(1) 估计条件方差并重缩放轨迹，将问题归约为单位对角情形而不改变底层图结构；(2) 设计局部边检验，通过隔离成对影响，从短更新窗口中提取邻接信息；(3) 采用基于中位数的鲁棒估计器聚合局部统计量，并证明尽管存在单条轨迹带来的时间依赖性，估计仍然准确。

Result: 给出了一个多项式时间算法，能够在$d$-稀疏高斯图模型上从单条Glauber轨迹中成功恢复条件独立图，且所需的轨迹长度与混合时间无关。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的局部检验与鲁棒聚合策略，成功克服了单条轨迹中时间依赖性的挑战，在多项式时间内实现了此前仅在i.i.d.设定下具有信息论保证但缺乏高效算法的图结构恢复任务。

Abstract: We study the task of learning the structure of a $d$-sparse Gaussian graphical model on $n$ variables from a single trajectory of Glauber dynamics. Beyond algorithmic considerations, many applications present temporally correlated observations rather than i.i.d.\ samples. In the classical i.i.d.\ setting, under comparably general sparsity and minimum edge-strength assumptions, sublinear-in-$n$ sample guarantees are known, but achieving them in polynomial-time remains open. Motivated in part by this gap, we give a polynomial-time algorithm that recovers the conditional-independence graph from a single Glauber trajectory, with a trajectory-length guarantee that does not depend on the mixing time.
  Technically, our algorithm has three components. First, we estimate the conditional variances and rescale the trajectory to reduce to the unit-diagonal case, without changing the underlying graph. Second, we design a local edge test that extracts adjacency information from short update windows by isolating pairwise influence. Third, we aggregate these local statistics using a robust median-based estimator, and prove accuracy despite temporal dependence arising from a single trajectory.

</details>


### [231] [Quality-Aware Modulation for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30934)
*Luke Budny,Yuhong Guo,Kevin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出质量表征模块(QRM)，一个轻量级Transformer模块，通过学习质量感知表征来调整DiT中的自适应LayerNorm调制，从而在不改变采样调度或扩散主干的前提下，为去噪过程注入质量敏感信号，持续提升生成图像的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型（如DiT）仅依赖时间步或提示嵌入来调节去噪强度，缺少质量感知信息，导致生成图像存在对齐偏差、视觉不一致和保真度不足的问题。

Method: 提出Quality Representation Module (QRM)，一个轻量级Transformer模块。QRM基于现有模型输入学习质量感知表征，生成一组向量M_qrm，用于调整DiT Transformer模块内的自适应LayerNorm调制，从而将质量敏感信号注入去噪参数中。

Result: 实验包括QRM训练损失和架构的消融研究，实证结果表明，相较于基线DiT模型，QRM能够持续提升图像质量。

Conclusion: QRM以轻量、即插即用的方式为扩散模型提供了质量感知能力，无需改动采样调度或扩散主干，即可有效改善生成图像的alignment、一致性和保真度。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image diffusion models, such as diffusion transformers (DiT), rely on timestep or prompt embeddings to modulate the strength of the denoising process in each timestep. While this modulation communicates the current noise level, it does not provide any quality-aware information, which can lead to generated images that are unaligned, visually inconsistent, and lacking in fidelity. In this paper, we propose the Quality Representation Module (QRM), a lightweight transformer module that learns a quality-aware representation based on existing model inputs, and produces a set of vectors $M_{qrm}$. These vectors adjust the adaptive LayerNorm modulation within the DiT transformer blocks, thereby injecting a quality-sensitive signal into the denoising parameters. The QRM introduces no significant changes to the sampling schedule or diffusion backbone. Experiments include ablations on QRM training losses and architectures, as well as empirical results demonstrating consistent image quality improvements over baseline DiT-based models.

</details>


### [232] [Sequential sparse Gaussian process quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31284)
*Hugo Nicolas,Olivier Le Maître*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稀疏高斯过程分位数回归框架，利用诱导变量和拉普拉斯近似解决贝叶斯分位数回归的计算难题，并通过方差分解驱动的自适应机制（诱导输入填充与数据采集）在序列算法中优化计算资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯高斯过程分位数回归虽能提供不确定性量化，但面临两大挑战：非对称拉普拉斯似然的非共轭性导致后验推断困难，以及高斯过程在大规模数据下的计算代价过高。

Method: 构建稀疏高斯过程框架，通过诱导变量（inducing variables）降维表示分位数函数，采用拉普拉斯近似进行后验推断；将预测不确定性分解为条件先验方差和后验诱导方差两个分量，据此设计两种自适应机制——诱导输入填充（inducing-input infilling）和数据采集（data acquisition），并将二者整合进序列算法中，动态分配计算资源以针对主导不确定性来源，同时自适应控制模型复杂度。

Result: 在基准问题上的数值实验表明：拉普拉斯近似具有良好精度，基于方差的诱导输入放置策略效果显著，所提出的序列增强策略优于预定义的数据采集策略。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解了贝叶斯分位数回归的计算瓶颈，通过不确定性分解引导的自适应机制实现了高效且准确的推断，为大规模分位数回归问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Quantile regression aims to estimate the conditional quantiles of a response variable from observed data. In a Bayesian setting, Gaussian process quantile regression provides uncertainty quantification but faces significant computational challenges due to the nonconjugacy of the asymmetric Laplace likelihood and the cost of posterior inference. We develop a sparse Gaussian process framework in which the quantile function is represented through a reduced set of inducing variables and posterior inference is performed using a Laplace approximation. A decomposition of the predictive uncertainty into conditional-prior and posterior-induced variance components is then exploited to drive two complementary adaptive mechanisms: inducing-input infilling and data acquisition. These mechanisms are combined within a sequential algorithm that allocates computational effort toward the dominant source of predictive uncertainty and adaptively controls model complexity. Numerical experiments on benchmark problems demonstrate the accuracy of the Laplace approximation, the benefits of variance-based inducing-input placement, and the effectiveness of the proposed sequential enrichment strategy compared with predefined data-acquisition strategies.

</details>


### [233] [Physics-informed Conditional Normalizing Flows for Angles-only Cislunar Orbit Determination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30936)
*Walther Litteri,Massimiliano Vasile*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式天体动力学方法，将生成建模（归一化流）应用于地月空间仅角度观测的轨道确定问题，通过条件密度估计学习初始状态的后验分布，为经典非线性最小二乘优化提供高质量的温启动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间（cislunar environment）中短弧段仅角度观测条件下的轨道确定具有高度不确定性，传统方法易陷入局部最优。作者希望利用生成模型捕捉后验分布的多模态特性，为传统算法提供更鲁棒的初始猜测。

Method: 1. 将轨道确定问题形式化为条件密度估计；2. 在近直线晕轨道（NRHO）扰动的地心-站心观测数据上训练归一化流（normalising flow）；3. 给定新测量值时，从学习的后验分布中采样，生成物理一致的状态假设；4. 以非线性最小二乘精化采样结果，作为经典算法的温启动。

Result: 归一化流能够灵活表示后验分布的多模态特性；采样生成的状态假设经过最小二乘精化后，可为经典轨道确定算法提供具有竞争力的温启动解。

Conclusion: 生成式建模可有效扩展至地月空间轨道确定问题，通过学习条件密度并提供物理一致的初始状态样本，显著改善传统优化算法的收敛性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generative Astrodynamics is advanced in this work by extending generative modelling to an orbit determination problem in the cislunar environment. The task is formulated as conditional density estimation, aiming to infer the probability distribution of the initial state from angles-only measurements over short observation arcs. A normalising flow is trained on perturbed topocentric observations from Near Rectilinear Halo Orbits, enabling a flexible and potentially multimodal posterior representation. Given new measurements, the learned density is sampled to generate statistically consistent and physics-informed state hypotheses. These estimates are refined via nonlinear least-squares minimisation, providing a competitive warm start for classical algorithms.

</details>


### [234] [Contextual Slate GLM Bandits with Limited Adaptivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31449)
*Tanmay Goyal,Sukruta Prakash Midigeshi,Gaurav Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限自适应下广义线性奖励的上下文Slate Bandit问题，提出两种算法：B-SlateGLinCB（分批设置，仅需O(log log T)批）和RS-SlateGLinCB（极少切换设置，仅需O(Nd log T)次参数更新），在多样性假设下分别实现O(Nd^{3/2}√T)和O(Nd√T)的遗憾界，且与非线性参数κ无关，同时在语言模型上下文示例选择任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用（如推荐系统、大语言模型上下文示例选择）中，完全自适应的bandit算法需要在每轮更新参数，导致高昂的计算与通信成本。因此，研究者希望设计在有限自适应（分批更新或极少切换）约束下仍能保持强遗憾界的算法，同时克服现有GLM bandit算法遗憾界对非线性参数κ的依赖问题。

Method: 针对两种有限自适应设置：(a) 分批设置中，B-SlateGLinCB将时间范围划分为O(log log T)个批次，每批策略仅依赖之前批次数据；(b) 极少切换设置中，RS-SlateGLinCB自适应地仅执行O(Nd log T)次参数更新。两者均基于广义线性模型，利用多样性假设处理组合动作空间（从N个集合各选一项构成slate），并通过poly(N)时间复杂度的计算实现高效推理。

Result: 理论上，B-SlateGLinCB和RS-SlateGLinCB分别取得O(Nd^{3/2}√T)和O(Nd√T)的遗憾界，且均不依赖非线性参数κ。实验上，算法显著优于现有有限自适应基线，与全自适应SOTA算法Slate-GLM-OFU表现相当；经轻微修改的B-SlateGLinCB甚至可匹敌该基线。在语言模型上下文示例选择的实际任务中也展现了强大性能。

Conclusion: 该工作成功在有限自适应约束下解决了上下文slate bandit问题，所提算法在理论（遗憾界与κ无关）和实践（计算高效、实验表现优异）两个层面均取得了重要进展，为推荐系统、LLM上下文选择等应用提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate the contextual slate bandit problem with generalized linear rewards under limited adaptivity. At each round, the learner is presented with $N$ sets of items, where each item is represented by a $d$-dimensional feature vector. The learner then constructs a slate by selecting one item per set; the resulting slate yields a scalar reward sampled from a Generalized Linear Model (GLM). We propose algorithms under two limited-adaptivity settings: (a) Batched and (b) Rarely-Switching. For the batched setting, we introduce B-SlateGLinCB, which partitions the time horizon into $\mathcal{O}(\log\log T)$ batches such that each batch's policy relies only on data from previous batches. For the rarely-switching setting, we propose RS-SlateGLinCB, which adaptively performs only $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log T)$ parameter updates. Under a diversity assumption on the item sequences, we prove that B-SlateGLinCB and RS-SlateGLinCB achieve regret bounds of $\mathcal{O}(Nd^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$ and $\mathcal{O}(Nd\sqrt{T})$, respectively. Notably, both bounds are independent of the non-linearity parameter $κ$ that is typically found to scale the regret of GLM bandit algorithms. Our algorithms are computationally efficient, requiring only $\text{poly}(N)$ time per round despite $2^{Ω(N)}$ possible slates. Simulations show our algorithms outperform existing baselines with limited adaptivity and remain competitive with Slate-GLM-OFU, a fully adaptive state-of-the-art algorithm. Notably, a slightly modified B-SlateGLinCB empirically matches this baseline. Finally, we demonstrate strong performance in a practical in-context example selection task for language models.

</details>


### [235] [On the Convergence of Self-Improving Online LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31524)
*Xudong Wu,Pangpang Liu,Vaneet Aggarwal,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对SAIL算法目标函数非强凹的问题，提出加入逆向KL散度正则化的SAIL-RevKL，证明其满足PL条件并具有全局收敛性和近线性采样复杂度，实验表明优于原始SAIL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始SAIL算法虽在分布偏移问题上表现良好，但其目标函数因Hessian矩阵的不良性质无法保证强凹性，缺乏正式的收敛性分析。

Method: 提出SAIL-RevKL，在原始目标函数中引入逆向KL散度（Reverse KL divergence）惩罚项作为正则化，改善优化景观；理论上证明该正则化目标在有界参数空间内满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz（PL）条件，并据此建立全局收敛保证。

Result: 理论上证明了SAIL-RevKL满足PL条件，获得了近线性采样复杂度的全局收敛保证。实验上，SAIL-RevKL在MuJoCo基准测试和LLM对齐任务上均优于原始SAIL算法。

Conclusion: 通过逆向KL散度正则化，SAIL-RevKL有效解决了原始SAIL目标函数非强凹的理论缺陷，兼具理论收敛保证与实证性能提升。

Abstract: The Self-Improving Alignment (SAIL) algorithm addresses distribution shift by reducing a bilevel formulation of the problem to an efficient, single-level method. Empirically, SAIL has demonstrated strong performance on this task. However, a formal analysis of its convergence properties has been lacking. We identify a key theoretical challenge: the standard SAIL objective function is not guaranteed to be strongly concave due to unfavorable properties of its Hessian. To address this limitation, we propose a regularized objective, SAIL-RevKL, which incorporates a reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence penalty to improve the optimization landscape. Our central theoretical contribution is to prove that this regularized objective satisfies the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition within a bounded parameter space. We establish global convergence guarantees, achieving a near-linear sample complexity. We further validate the effectiveness and stability of SAIL-RevKL through empirical evaluations, demonstrating that it outperforms the vanilla SAIL on both MuJoCo benchmarks and LLM alignment tasks.

</details>


### [236] [Estimating Supply Incrementality in Two-sided Marketplaces: A Causal Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30999)
*Yufei Wu,Daniel Schmierer,Dan Zylberglejd*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因果机器学习方法，结合双去偏机器学习与分层贝叶斯框架，利用地理空间相似性特征，估计双边市场中新增供应对交易结果的异质性因果效应，并以Airbnb为例验证了方法的合理性和良好的样本外表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质性产品的双边市场中，理解新增供应与市场结果（如总交易量或交易额）之间的因果关系至关重要，但传统方法难以在不同产品细分市场中获得可靠的异质性因果效应估计。

Method: 提出将双/去偏机器学习与分层贝叶斯框架相结合的方法。利用地理空间文献中的产品细分相似性度量构建可解释特征，作为贝叶斯先验知识融入模型，实现对不同产品细分市场供应回报的异质性估计。

Result: 该方法在Airbnb新增房源供应对总预订量影响的场景下，提供了合理的市场供应回报估计，并展现出较强的样本外预测性能。

Conclusion: 结合因果机器学习与分层贝叶斯的方法能够有效估计双边市场中供应增加的异质性回报，地理空间相似性特征提升了模型的可解释性和估计质量，该方法可推广至其他双边市场场景。

Abstract: In two-sided marketplaces with heterogeneous products, it is important to understand the causal relationship between additional supply and marketplace outcomes, such as the total quantity transacted or transaction value in the marketplace. This paper studies a causal machine learning approach to estimating this relationship across product segments. We use the Airbnb marketplace as an example, focusing on the impact of additional listing supply on total bookings, but the methodology applies to other two-sided marketplaces. Our approach combines double/debiased machine learning with a hierarchical Bayesian framework that leverages pre-existing knowledge as priors. We construct tractable and informative features for the model by leveraging measures of product segment similarity from the geospatial literature. We find that such a model provides plausible estimates of the marketplace returns to additional supply and strong out of sample performance.

</details>


### [237] [Policy Optimization Achieves Data-Dependent Regret Bounds in MDPs with Unknown Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31769)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对未知转移核的表格马尔可夫决策过程，提出一种基于乐观跟随正则化领导者的策略优化算法，在未知转移条件下同时实现一阶、二阶和路径长度等数据依赖的遗憾界，并在随机环境中获得间隙依赖的多重对数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明策略优化在已知转移条件下可实现数据依赖的自适应遗憾界（一阶、二阶、路径长度界），但在未知转移核下，这类数据依赖保证是否可由策略优化实现仍是悬而未决的问题。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文开发了一种基于乐观跟随正则化领导者（optimistic FTRL）的新算法。核心创新包括：（1）新的乐观Q函数估计器设计；（2）数据依赖的转移奖励项，通过损失预测误差控制估计器偏差。该方法在未知转移条件下统一处理对抗性与随机损失。

Result: 算法在未知转移条件下同时获得一阶、二阶和路径长度遗憾界，其中包含一个不可回避的转移依赖复杂度项（刻画估计转移核的内在代价）。在随机环境中，算法额外获得间隙依赖的 poly log(T) 遗憾界，实现真正的 best-of-both-worlds 保证。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明在未知转移核下，策略优化可以同时实现对抗与随机环境的数据依赖遗憾界，解决了该开放问题。转移依赖复杂度项被证明是内在不可避免的，标志着该方向的理论完备性。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for online episodic tabular Markov decision processes with unknown transition kernels, aiming for best-of-both-worlds guarantees together with data-dependent regret bounds. Recent work (Dann et al., 2023; Li et al., 2026) has shown that policy optimization can adapt to both adversarial and stochastic losses with first-order, second-order, and path-length bounds, but only under known transitions, leaving open whether such data-dependent guarantees are achievable by policy optimization when the transition kernel is unknown. We resolve this by developing a new algorithm based on optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader that attains these guarantees under unknown transitions. The key ingredient is a new design of optimistic $Q$-function estimators together with a data-dependent transition bonus that controls estimator bias through the loss-prediction error. Our analysis further identifies an unavoidable transition-dependent complexity term that captures the intrinsic cost of estimating the transition kernel. As a result, we obtain first-order, second-order, and path-length bounds with the transition-dependent complexity term while simultaneously achieving gap-dependent $\mathrm{polylog}(T)$ regret in the stochastic regime.

</details>


### [238] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Fluid Controls: Data-based Multi-observational Policy Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31025)
*Deepak Akhare,Luning Sun,Xin-Yang Liu,Xiantao Fan,Timo Bremer,Ben Zhu,Jian-Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离线强化学习的主动流动控制框架，通过传感器位置条件化的Point Attention架构，使单一策略网络能适应多种传感器布置，避免传统在线RL的实时交互和重复训练成本，并在KS方程和翼型流动控制两个问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线强化学习用于主动流动控制面临两大挑战：(1) 需与高保真环境进行大量实时交互，计算成本高昂；(2) 每次传感器配置变化都需重新训练整个策略。这两点严重制约了RL在真实工程流动控制中的应用。

Method: 提出离线RL框架，从预采集数据集中提取策略，避免在线交互。核心创新是传感器位置条件化（position-conditioned）架构：通过Point Attention层建模传感器空间关系，使单一策略网络能泛化到不同的传感器布置方案，无需为每种配置单独训练。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程的混沌抑制和Navier-Stokes方程翼型流动控制两个代表性问题上验证了框架有效性，证明了从数据集中提取策略可为传感器位置优化提供前所未有的灵活性。

Conclusion: 该离线RL方法通过数据驱动策略提取和位置条件化架构，显著降低了计算成本并提高了传感器布置灵活性，是迈向自适应、智能流动控制系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Active flow control is a fundamental application in engineering. Recent advances in deep reinforcement learning have made progress in this field. However, the classical online RL approaches require extensive real-time interactions with the high fidelity environment, while each sensor configuration change necessitates whole policy retraining. All these factors result in prohibitive computational costs for real-world applications. In this work, we propose a novel offline RL framework that addresses both challenges through data-driven policy extraction. We develop a sensor position-conditioned architecture that enables a single policy network to adapt seamlessly to multiple sensor arrangements. The position-conditioned approach incorporated spatial relationship modeling through Point Attention layers to ensure the generalizability to varying sensor placements. We demonstrate the framework on two representative problems, mitigating chaoticity in the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation and flow control over airfoils governed by the Navier-Stokes equation. The result demonstrates that the policy extraction from the dataset provides unprecedented flexibility for sensor placement optimization. This approach represents a significant step towards adaptive, intelligent flow control systems.

</details>


### [239] [Relational and Sequential Conformal Inference for Energy Time Series over Graphs via Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31804)
*Keivan Faghih Niresi,Alice Cicirello,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STOIC 是一种将时空图神经网络点预测与表格基础模型的上下文学习相结合的非参数化保形预测框架，无需任务特定重训练即可为复杂能源系统生成具有统计覆盖保证的预测区间，在五个基准数据集上一致优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源系统运营不仅需要精确的点预测，还需要可靠的不确定性量化以支持风险感知决策和电网稳定。虽然保形预测能提供统计覆盖保证，但现有方法难以充分捕捉能源系统的复杂时空结构，因此需要一种能同时建模时序动态和关系依赖的不确定性量化方法。

Method: STOIC 框架分三步：(1) 使用时空图神经网络 (STGNN) 生成点预测；(2) 将时空残差重构为适合上下文学习的表格表示，同时保留序列和关系信息；(3) 利用表格基础模型（如 TabPFN）进行零样本校准，生成预测区间，无需任务特定重训练。

Result: 在合成仿真、真实电力和区域供热网络等五个多样化基准数据集上，STOIC 在预测区间的可靠性和鲁棒性方面一致优于现有保形预测基线方法。

Conclusion: STOIC 通过将图结构预测与表格基础模型的零样本校准能力相融合，有效解决了能源系统时空不确定性量化难题，为安全关键的能源应用提供了一种更具可靠性且易于部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate energy demand forecasting is essential for the reliable operation and planning of modern sustainable energy systems. Spatial-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) have recently achieved strong performance in point forecasting by jointly modeling temporal dynamics and relational dependencies across interconnected energy nodes. However, in real-world energy systems, accurate point forecasts alone are insufficient, as operators also require reliable uncertainty estimates to support risk-aware decision-making, grid stability, and operational planning under uncertainty. Conformal prediction provides a principled and model-agnostic framework for uncertainty quantification with statistical coverage guarantees, making it particularly attractive for safety-critical energy applications. However, existing conformal prediction approaches often fail to fully capture the complex spatial-temporal structure of energy systems. To address these limitations, we propose STOIC (Spatial-Temporal Graph Conformal Prediction with In-Context Learning), a novel framework that integrates graph-based forecasting with the zero-shot calibration capabilities of tabular foundation models. STOIC first generates point forecasts using an STGNN and subsequently reformulates spatial-temporal residuals into a tabular representation suitable for in-context learning. Leveraging a tabular foundation model, STOIC calibrates prediction intervals without task-specific retraining, effectively capturing both sequential and relational dependencies. We evaluate STOIC on five diverse benchmarks, including synthetic simulations as well as real-world electricity and district heating networks. Across all datasets, STOIC consistently outperforms existing conformal prediction baselines, delivering more reliable and robust uncertainty estimates for complex graph-structured energy time series.

</details>


### [240] [OTCache: Optimal Transport for Geometry-Aware Caching in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31026)
*Huanlin Gao,Fang Zhao,Qiang Hui,Fuyuan Shi,Shaoan Zhao,Yantao Li,Chao Tan,Ting Lu,Yuren You,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OTCache 提出一种免训练的扩散采样加速框架，通过最优传输启发的缓存调度预测，在不同推理预算下实现策略平滑演化，在多个模型上取得 3.66x–4.7x 加速并提升生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的缓存方法依赖可加性独立假设，在低 NFE（函数评估次数）场景下该假设失效，导致缓存调度策略在推理预算变化时性能下降。因此需要一种能够在不同推理预算间平滑过渡的调度建模方法。

Method: OTCache 分三阶段：(1) 在保守预算下用图缓存方法获取高保真参考调度；(2) 在极低预算下通过 Optuna 优化，以端到端感知目标进行轻量锚点搜索；(3) 利用连续变形表示的参考策略与锚点策略之间进行分位数插值，预测目标预算下的调度方案。

Result: 在 FLUX.1 [dev]、Qwen-Image 和 HunyuanVideo 上分别实现 4.5x、4.7x 和 3.66x 加速，且生成保真度一致优于现有 SOTA 缓存基线方法。

Conclusion: OTCache 证明了通过最优传输视角进行缓存调度建模，可在免训练条件下有效加速扩散模型推理，为扩散采样加速提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose OTCache, a training-free framework for accelerating diffusion sampling via caching schedule prediction. Existing graph-based caching methods reduce redundant computation by optimizing shortest-path objectives, but rely on an additive independence assumption, which often breaks down in the low NFE regime. To address this issue, OTCache models caching schedules across inference budgets as a smooth evolution in policy space, inspired by Optimal Transport (OT).
